  public
  inheriting from ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC
  create public .

public section.

  types:
    TY_DD07V TYPE TABLE OF DD07V WITH DEFAULT KEY .

  class-data GV_CALL_FROM_FIORI type BOOLEAN .

  methods VALIDATE_ON_USER_ACTION
    importing
      !IV_WIID type SWW_WIID
      !IV_CASE_ID type SCMG_EXT_KEY optional
      !IV_ACTIVITY type SRMACTIV optional
      !IV_EXECFUNC type SRMACTFC optional
      !IV_MD_SKIP type BOOLEAN_FLG optional
    exporting
      !ET_RETURN type BAPIRET2_T .
  methods CHECK_PAYMENT_FOR_PERMIT
    importing
      !IV_PERMIT_ID type ZDE_EGA_PERMIT_REF_NUMBER
    exporting
      !EV_BALANCE_TO_PAY type BOOLEAN .
  methods GET_EXPANDED_FISHING
    importing
      !IV_ENTITY_NAME type STRING optional
      !IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME type STRING optional
      !IV_SOURCE_NAME type STRING optional

      !IS_PAGING type /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING optional

      !IT_KEY_TAB type /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR optional

      !IV_SEARCH_STRING type STRING optional
      !IO_EXPAND type ref to /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ODATA_EXPAND optional
      !IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT type ref to /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET optional
    exporting
      !ER_ENTITYSET type ref to DATA
      !ET_EXPANDED_CLAUSES type STRING_TABLE
      !ET_EXPANDED_TECH_CLAUSES type STRING_TABLE
    raising
      /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
      /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION .
  methods GET_DOMAIN
    importing
      !DOMAIN type STRING
    returning
      value(VALUES) type TY_DD07V .
  methods GET_EXPANDED_NOC_CONSULTANT
    importing
      !IV_ENTITY_NAME type STRING optional
      !IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME type STRING optional
      !IV_SOURCE_NAME type STRING optional
      !IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS type /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION optional
      !IT_ORDER type /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER optional
      !IS_PAGING type /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING optional
      !IT_NAVIGATION_PATH type /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH optional
      !IT_KEY_TAB type /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR optional
      !IV_FILTER_STRING type STRING optional
      !IV_SEARCH_STRING type STRING optional
      !IO_EXPAND type ref to /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ODATA_EXPAND optional
      !IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT type ref to /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET optional
    exporting
      !ER_ENTITYSET type ref to DATA
      !ET_EXPANDED_CLAUSES type STRING_TABLE
      !ET_EXPANDED_TECH_CLAUSES type STRING_TABLE
    raising
      /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
      /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION .

    redefinition .

    redefinition .

  methods OVERVIEWSET_FISH_GET_ENTITYSET

      !IV_SOURCE_NAME type STRING
      !IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS type /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
      !IS_PAGING type /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
      !IT_KEY_TAB type /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
      !IT_NAVIGATION_PATH type /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
      !IT_ORDER type /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
      !IV_FILTER_STRING type STRING
      !IV_SEARCH_STRING type STRING
      !IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT type ref to /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET optional
    exporting

    raising

CLASS ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT IMPLEMENTATION.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->/IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~CREATE_DEEP_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH(optional)
* | [--->] IO_EXPAND                      TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ODATA_EXPAND
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_C(optional)
* | [<---] ER_DEEP_ENTITY                 TYPE REF TO DATA
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD /iwbep/if_mgw_appl_srv_runtime~create_deep_entity.
    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ty_appl_output_struc,
        subactivityset TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_subactivity WITH DEFAULT KEY,
        feesset        TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_fees WITH DEFAULT KEY.
            INCLUDE TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_application.

    TYPES END OF ty_appl_output_struc.

    DATA: wa_data TYPE ty_appl_output_struc,
          it_data TYPE TABLE OF ty_appl_output_struc.

    DATA:ls_parameter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_name_value_pair.
    DATA: input_data TYPE zst_epda_fees_input.

    DATA: ls_input_sub_activity     LIKE LINE OF input_data-sub_activities_tab,
          lt_input_sub_activity_tab LIKE  input_data-sub_activities_tab.

    DATA: ls_input_fees     LIKE LINE OF input_data-fees_table,
          lt_input_fees_tab LIKE  input_data-fees_table.

    DATA: lo_serv_mgr TYPE REF TO /bobf/if_tra_service_manager,
          lo_trxn_mgr TYPE REF TO /bobf/if_tra_transaction_mgr,
          lt_fee      TYPE ztt_cs_ega_epda_fee_details.
    DATA: lv_caseid TYPE scmg_ext_key,
          lv_applno TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no.

    DATA: lt_final_fees   TYPE ztt_cs_ega_epda_fee_details,
          ls_final_fees   LIKE LINE OF lt_final_fees,
          lt_final_subact TYPE zst_ct_ega_epda_sub_activities,
          ls_final_subact LIKE LINE OF lt_final_subact.

    DATA: lt_key TYPE /bobf/t_frw_key,
          ls_key TYPE /bobf/s_frw_key.

    DATA: lt_subact TYPE TABLE OF string.
    DATA ls_subacts TYPE zst_cs_ega_epda_sub_activities.

    DATA: lt_modification  TYPE /bobf/t_frw_modification,
          ls_modification  LIKE LINE OF lt_modification,
          lt_changedfields TYPE /bobf/t_frw_name.

    DATA lv_case_type TYPE  scmgcase_type.

    DATA(lr_root)     = NEW zst_cs_ega_epda_appln_root( ).
    DATA(lo_tra_mgr) = /bobf/cl_tra_trans_mgr_factory=>get_transaction_manager( ).
    DATA(lo_srv_mgr)  = /bobf/cl_tra_serv_mgr_factory=>get_service_manager( zif_ci_epda_appln_bo=>sc_bo_key ).


    io_data_provider->read_entry_data( IMPORTING es_data = wa_data ).
    READ TABLE wa_data-feesset INTO DATA(wa_applno) INDEX 1.
    lv_caseid = |{ wa_data-case_id ALPHA = IN }|.
    lv_applno = wa_applno-application_number.

    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
      EXPORTING
        iv_read_type      = 'C'
        ir_applic_id      = VALUE #( ( option = 'EQ' sign = 'I' low = lv_applno ) )
      RECEIVING
        et_data           = DATA(lt_data)
    ).

    TRY .
        DATA(ls_appln) = lt_data[ 1 ].
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    IF wa_data-feesset IS NOT INITIAL.

      ls_appln-pay_range = wa_data-pay_range.
      IF ls_appln-pay_range IS NOT INITIAL.
        APPEND: zif_ci_epda_appln_bo=>sc_node_attribute-root-pay_range TO lt_changedfields.
      ENDIF.

      """ Update changed validity onto Application BO.
      READ TABLE wa_data-feesset INTO
                     DATA(ls_license_fee) WITH KEY fee_type = zif_epda_interface=>c_fee_type_license_fee.
      ls_appln-license_validity = ls_license_fee-years_duration. "wa_data-license_validity.
      APPEND zif_ci_epda_appln_bo=>sc_node_attribute-root-license_validity TO lt_changedfields.
      """ End - Update changed validity onto Application BO.

      ls_modification-change_mode = /bobf/if_frw_c=>sc_modify_update.
      ls_modification-node        = zif_ci_epda_appln_bo=>sc_node-root.
      ls_modification-root_key    = ls_appln-parent_key.
      ls_modification-key         = ls_appln-key.
      ls_modification-source_node = zif_ci_epda_appln_bo=>sc_node-root.
      ls_modification-source_key  = ls_appln-key.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING lt_changedfields TO ls_modification-changed_fields.
      ls_modification-data        = NEW zst_cs_ega_epda_appln_root( CORRESPONDING #( ls_appln ) ).
      APPEND ls_modification TO lt_modification.

      zcl_ega_epda_appln_case_create=>mv_skip = abap_true.

      lo_srv_mgr->modify(
      EXPORTING
        it_modification = lt_modification    " Changes
      IMPORTING
        eo_change       =  DATA(lt_change)   " Interface of Change Object
        eo_message      =  DATA(lt_message)   " Interface of Message Object
    ).

      lo_tra_mgr->save(
        EXPORTING
          iv_transaction_pattern = /bobf/if_tra_c=>gc_tp_save_and_continue    " Data element for a transaction pattern
        IMPORTING
          eo_message             =  lt_message   " Interface of Message Object
*         et_rejecting_bo_key    =     " Key table
      ).

    ENDIF.

    REFRESH: input_data-fees_table, input_data-sub_activities_tab.
    LOOP AT wa_data-feesset INTO DATA(ls_fees_data).
      ls_input_fees-amount = ls_fees_data-amount.
      ls_input_fees-currency = ls_fees_data-currency.
      ls_input_fees-discount = ls_fees_data-discount.
      ls_input_fees-duration_editable = ls_fees_data-duration_editable.
      ls_input_fees-editable_on_ui = ls_fees_data-editable_on_ui.
      ls_input_fees-fee_type = ls_fees_data-fee_type.
      ls_input_fees-months_duration = ls_fees_data-months_duration.
      ls_input_fees-waived_amount = ls_fees_data-waived_amount.
      ls_input_fees-waived_off = ls_fees_data-waived_off.
      ls_input_fees-years_duration = ls_fees_data-years_duration.
      APPEND ls_input_fees TO input_data-fees_table.
    ENDLOOP.

    READ TABLE lt_data INTO DATA(ls_caseid) INDEX 1.
    input_data-case_id =  |{ ls_caseid-case_id ALPHA = IN }|.
    lv_caseid =  |{ ls_caseid-case_id ALPHA = IN }|.
    lv_case_type =  |{ ls_caseid-case_type ALPHA = IN }|.

    IF wa_data-subactivityset IS INITIAL.
      input_data-pay_range = wa_data-pay_range.
    ENDIF.
*  IF input_data-fees_table IS INITIAL.
*    zcl_epda_fees_handler=>get_fees(
*        EXPORTING
*          is_fee_parameter =  input_data
*          iv_case_type     =   'ZE01'
*        IMPORTING
*          et_computed_fees =   input_data-fees_table ).
*  ENDIF.

    DATA lt_final TYPE ztt_epda_fees.

    REFRESH input_data-sub_activities_tab.

    LOOP AT wa_data-subactivityset INTO DATA(ls_subact).
      ls_final_subact-subactivity_id = ls_subact-activity.
      ls_final_subact-pay_range = ls_subact-payrange.
      APPEND ls_final_subact TO  lt_final_subact.
      ls_input_sub_activity-subactivity_id = ls_subact-activity.
      ls_input_sub_activity-pay_range = ls_subact-payrange.
      APPEND ls_input_sub_activity TO input_data-sub_activities_tab.
    ENDLOOP.

    zcl_epda_fees_handler=>get_fees(
        EXPORTING
          is_fee_parameter =  input_data
          iv_case_type     =   lv_case_type "'ZE01'
        IMPORTING
          et_computed_fees =   DATA(it_ffees) ).

    MOVE-CORRESPONDING it_ffees TO lt_final_fees.

    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>update_fee_and_subactivity(
      EXPORTING
        iv_case_id        =  lv_caseid
        it_fee_details    = lt_final_fees
        it_sub_activities = lt_final_subact
      IMPORTING
        et_return         = DATA(lt_return)
    ).

  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->/IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~EXECUTE_ACTION
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ACTION_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IT_PARAMETER                   TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_FUNC_IMPORT(optional)
* | [<---] ER_DATA                        TYPE REF TO DATA
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
METHOD /iwbep/if_mgw_appl_srv_runtime~execute_action.

  DATA: ls_data       TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>message,
        lt_data       TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>users,
        ls_user       TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>users,
        ls_appl       TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>application_fee,
        ls_permitform TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>permitform_output,
        lt_return     TYPE bapiret2_t.
  DATA workitem_id             TYPE swr_struct-workitemid.
  DATA user_id                 TYPE sy-uname.
  DATA language                TYPE sylangu.
  DATA do_commit               TYPE xfeld.
  DATA current_user            TYPE syuname.
  DATA check_inbox_restriction TYPE xfeld.
  DATA return_code             TYPE sy-subrc.
  DATA new_status              TYPE swr_wistat.
  DATA message_lines           TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF swr_messag.
  DATA message_struct          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF swr_mstruc.
  DATA user_ids                TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF swragent.
  DATA: ls_address TYPE bapiaddr3, lv_name TYPE xubname.
  DATA lv_caseid_tmp  TYPE scmg_ext_key.
  DATA lv_call_flg    TYPE char1.

  IF iv_action_name EQ 'GetInspector'.
    DATA lv_assignedinsp TYPE zde_ega_location_id.
    TRY.
        lv_assignedinsp = it_parameter[ name = 'AssignedInspector' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
    SELECT * FROM zdt_ega_loca_epd INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_location) WHERE inspector_id NE @lv_assignedinsp.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SELECT * FROM zdt_epda_locat_t INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_locat_t)
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_location
          WHERE language = @sy-langu
            AND location = @lt_location-location.
    ENDIF.
    LOOP AT lt_location ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_loc>).
      ls_user-inspectorid = <fs_loc>-inspector_id.
**Inspector Name
      CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_USER_GET_DETAIL'
        EXPORTING
          username = <fs_loc>-inspector_id
        IMPORTING
          address  = ls_address
        TABLES
          return   = lt_return.
      IF NOT ls_address-fullname IS INITIAL.
        ls_user-inspectorname =  ls_address-fullname.
      ENDIF.
      ls_user-locationid = <fs_loc>-location.
      READ TABLE lt_locat_t INTO DATA(ls_locat_t) WITH KEY location = <fs_loc>-location.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        ls_user-locationname = ls_locat_t-location_description.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND ls_user TO lt_data.
      CLEAR:ls_address,ls_user.
    ENDLOOP.
    copy_data_to_ref(
      EXPORTING
        is_data = lt_data
      CHANGING
        cr_data = er_data
    ).
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  IF iv_action_name EQ 'AssignInspector'.

    WAIT UP TO 5 SECONDS.

    DATA lv_caseid  TYPE scmg_ext_key.
    TRY.
        lv_caseid = it_parameter[ name = 'CaseId' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
    lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
    TRY.
        user_id = it_parameter[ name = 'InspectorId' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    SELECT SINGLE case_guid INTO @DATA(lv_guid) FROM scmg_t_case_attr WHERE ext_key = @lv_caseid.

    SELECT case_guid, wfpthid FROM scmg_t_case INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_case) WHERE case_guid EQ @lv_guid.

    IF lt_case IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT posid_wiid, pathid, agent, activity, creadate, creatime, enddate, endtime FROM srmwfpthps
       INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_srmwfpthps)
       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_case
         WHERE pathid = @lt_case-wfpthid AND enddate EQ 00000000 AND endtime EQ 00000000.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE lt_srmwfpthps INTO DATA(ls_wfpth) INDEX 1.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    workitem_id = ls_wfpth-posid_wiid.
    IF workitem_id IS INITIAL.
      ls_data-msg = 'There is no workitem assigned'.
    ELSE.
      CALL FUNCTION 'SAP_WAPI_FORWARD_WORKITEM'
        EXPORTING
          workitem_id    = workitem_id
          user_id        = user_id
          language       = sy-langu
          do_commit      = 'X'
          current_user   = sy-uname
        IMPORTING
          return_code    = return_code
          new_status     = new_status
        TABLES
          message_lines  = message_lines
          message_struct = message_struct.

      READ TABLE message_lines INTO DATA(ls_msg) WITH KEY msg_type = 'E'.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        ls_data-msg = 'Task forwarded to' && user_id.
      ELSE.
        ls_data-msg = ls_msg-line.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    copy_data_to_ref(
      EXPORTING
        is_data = ls_data
      CHANGING
        cr_data = er_data
    ).
  ENDIF.
  IF iv_action_name EQ 'DownloadToExcel'.
    DATA : lv_applicationtype TYPE string,
           lv_fromtodate      TYPE string,
           gv_fromdate        TYPE zde_ega_case_creation_date,
           gv_fromdate_n      TYPE zde_ega_case_creation_date,
           gv_todate          TYPE zde_ega_case_creation_date,
           gv_todate_n        TYPE zde_ega_case_creation_date..
    TRY.
        lv_applicationtype = it_parameter[ name = 'ApplicationType' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
    TRY.
        lv_fromtodate  = it_parameter[ name = 'FromToDate' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
    SPLIT lv_fromtodate AT ',' INTO gv_fromdate gv_todate.

    gv_fromdate_n =  COND string( WHEN gv_fromdate IS NOT INITIAL
                                  THEN |{ gv_fromdate+4(4) }| && |{ gv_fromdate+2(2) }| &&
                                  |{ gv_fromdate+0(2) }|  ELSE '' ).
    gv_todate_n =  COND string( WHEN gv_todate IS NOT INITIAL
                              THEN |{ gv_todate+4(4) }| && |{ gv_todate+2(2) }| &&
                              |{ gv_todate+0(2) }| ELSE '' ).
    CALL METHOD zcl_ega_epda_excel_download=>download_to_excel
      EXPORTING
        iv_from_date        = gv_fromdate_n
        iv_to_date          = gv_todate_n
        iv_application_type = lv_applicationtype.
  ENDIF.
  IF iv_action_name EQ 'GetApplicationFee'.
    DATA:lv_netwr  TYPE vbap-netwr.
    TRY.
        lv_caseid = it_parameter[ name = 'CaseId' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
    lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
    SELECT vbeln,netwr FROM vbap
           INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_appl_fee) WHERE matnr       = 'EPDA APPL_FEE'
*                                         AND   case_type   = 'ZE01'
                                         AND   vbap~ext_key     = @lv_caseid.
    IF NOT lt_appl_fee IS INITIAL.
      LOOP AT lt_appl_fee INTO DATA(ls_appl_fee).
        IF NOT ls_appl_fee-netwr IS INITIAL.
          lv_netwr = lv_netwr + ls_appl_fee-netwr.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
    ls_appl-applicationfee = lv_netwr.
    copy_data_to_ref(
      EXPORTING
        is_data = ls_appl
      CHANGING
        cr_data = er_data
    ).
  ENDIF.
  IF iv_action_name EQ 'GetPermitForm'.
    DATA:lv_appl_no  TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no.
    TRY.
        lv_caseid = it_parameter[ name = 'CaseId' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        lv_appl_no = it_parameter[ name = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.


    CLEAR lv_caseid_tmp.
    CLEAR lv_call_flg.
    IF lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL OR lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF NOT lv_appl_no IS INITIAL.
        SELECT SINGLE *  FROM zdt_epda_appln_r INTO @DATA(ls_epda_appln_r) WHERE application_number = @lv_appl_no.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
*          SELECT SINGLE * FROM scmg_t_case_attr INTO @DATA(ls_case_attr) WHERE ext_key = @ls_epda_appln_r-case_id.
*          IF sy-subrc = 0.
*            SELECT SINGLE * FROM scmgstatprofst INTO @DATA(ls_case_text) WHERE langu        = @sy-langu
*                                                                         AND   profile_id   = 'ZE01'
*                                                                         AND   stat_orderno = @ls_case_attr-stat_orderno.
*          ENDIF.
          lv_caseid_tmp = ls_epda_appln_r-case_id.
          lv_call_flg = 1.
        ELSE.

          SELECT SINGLE * FROM zdt_epda_noc_r INTO @DATA(ls_epda_noc_r) WHERE application_number = @lv_appl_no.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            lv_caseid_tmp = ls_epda_noc_r-case_id.
            lv_call_flg = 2.
          ELSE.
            SELECT SINGLE * FROM zdt_epda_fisry_r INTO @DATA(ls_epda_fisry_r) WHERE application_number = @lv_appl_no.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              lv_caseid_tmp = ls_epda_fisry_r-case_id.
              lv_call_flg = 3.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        IF lv_caseid_tmp IS INITIAL AND lv_call_flg IS INITIAL.
          ls_permitform-message   = 'Please Enter Valid Application Number'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      IF NOT lv_caseid IS INITIAL.
        lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
        SELECT SINGLE *  FROM zdt_epda_appln_r INTO @ls_epda_appln_r WHERE case_id = @lv_caseid.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
*          SELECT SINGLE * FROM scmg_t_case_attr INTO @ls_case_attr WHERE ext_key = @ls_epda_appln_r-case_id.
*          IF sy-subrc = 0.
*            SELECT SINGLE * FROM scmgstatprofst INTO @ls_case_text WHERE langu        = @sy-langu
*                                                                   AND   profile_id   = 'ZE01'
*                                                                   AND   stat_orderno = @ls_case_attr-stat_orderno.
*          ENDIF.
          lv_caseid_tmp = ls_epda_appln_r-case_id.
          lv_call_flg = 1.
        ELSE.

          SELECT SINGLE * FROM zdt_epda_noc_r INTO @ls_epda_noc_r WHERE case_id = @lv_caseid.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            lv_caseid_tmp = ls_epda_noc_r-case_id.
            lv_call_flg = 2.
          ELSE.
            SELECT SINGLE * FROM zdt_epda_fisry_r INTO @ls_epda_fisry_r WHERE case_id = @lv_caseid.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              lv_caseid_tmp = ls_epda_fisry_r-case_id.
              lv_call_flg = 3.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.


        IF lv_caseid_tmp IS INITIAL AND lv_call_flg IS INITIAL.
          ls_permitform-message   = 'Please Enter Valid Case Id'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      SELECT SINGLE * FROM scmg_t_case_attr INTO @DATA(ls_case_attr) WHERE ext_key = @lv_caseid_tmp."@ls_epda_appln_r-case_id.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          SELECT SINGLE * FROM scmgstatprofst INTO @DATA(ls_case_text) WHERE langu        = @sy-langu
                                                                       AND   profile_id   = @ls_case_attr-case_type
                                                                       AND   stat_orderno = @ls_case_attr-stat_orderno.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      IF ls_permitform-message IS INITIAL AND ls_case_attr-stat_orderno = '75'. "ls_case_text-stat_orderno = '75'.
        CASE lv_call_flg.
          WHEN 1.

            zcl_epda_contract_handler=>get_form_data(
              EXPORTING
                iv_caseid     = lv_caseid   " RAK EGA: Case ID
                iv_refno      = lv_appl_no " RAK EGA EPDA Application Number
              IMPORTING
                ts_final      = DATA(ts_final)      " Structure Permit type Form
                ev_pdf_permit = DATA(ev_pdf_permit) " Form Processing: Content from XFT, XFD, PDF, and so on
            ).

            IF NOT ev_pdf_permit IS INITIAL.
              ls_permitform-file_data = ev_pdf_permit.
              ls_permitform-message   = '  '.
            ENDIF.
          WHEN 2 OR 3.
            NEW zcl_scase_gen_attachments( )->get_case_attachments(
              EXPORTING
                spsids      = VALUE #( ( 'ZZRM_SPS_AL_PERMIT/LICENSE' ) )
                case_guid   = ls_case_attr-case_guid
              RECEIVING
                attachments = DATA(attachments)
            ).
            READ TABLE attachments ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_att>) INDEX 1.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              DATA: attachment TYPE zst_att_gen_srv.
              NEW zcl_scase_gen_attachments( )->zif_attachmet_gen~get_attachment(
                EXPORTING
                  key        = <fs_att>-gen_key
                CHANGING
                  attachment = attachment
              ).
              IF attachment-data IS NOT INITIAL.
                ls_permitform-file_data = attachment-data .
                ls_permitform-message   = '  '.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          WHEN OTHERS.
        ENDCASE.
      ELSE.
        IF ls_permitform-message IS INITIAL.
          IF lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL.
            lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = OUT }|.
            CONCATENATE 'The Status of the Case id' lv_caseid 'is' ls_case_text-stat_ordno_descr INTO ls_permitform-message  SEPARATED BY space.
          ENDIF.
          IF lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL.
            CONCATENATE 'The Status of the Application no' lv_appl_no 'is' ls_case_text-stat_ordno_descr INTO ls_permitform-message  SEPARATED BY space.
          ENDIF.
*          ls_permitform-message   = ls_case_text-stat_ordno_descr.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      copy_data_to_ref(
        EXPORTING
          is_data = ls_permitform
        CHANGING
          cr_data = er_data
      ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF iv_action_name EQ 'FeeChange'.
    DATA lv_application_no  TYPE  zde_ega_epda_application_no.
    DATA lv_fee_type        TYPE  zde_epda_fee_type.
    DATA lv_amount          TYPE  zde_ega_amount.
    DATA lv_discount        TYPE  zde_ega_discount_percentage.
    TRY.
        lv_application_no = it_parameter[ name = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
        lv_fee_type = it_parameter[ name = 'FeeType' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
        lv_amount = it_parameter[ name = 'Amount' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
        lv_discount = it_parameter[ name = 'Discount' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    CALL METHOD zcl_epda_fees_handler=>fee_modify
      EXPORTING
        iv_application_no = lv_application_no
        iv_fee_type       = lv_fee_type
        iv_amount         = lv_amount
        iv_discount       = lv_discount.

  ENDIF.


  IF iv_action_name EQ 'BlockPermit'.

    DATA lv_permit_id TYPE  zde_ega_permit_ref_number.
    DATA lv_blocktype TYPE  zde_ega_epda_permit_block.
    DATA lv_comments TYPE  string.
    DATA lv_attachment TYPE  string.


    TRY.
        lv_permit_id = it_parameter[ name = 'PermitNumber' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
        lv_blocktype = it_parameter[ name = 'Blocktype' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
        lv_comments = it_parameter[ name = 'Comments' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
        lv_attachment = it_parameter[ name = 'Attachment' ]-value.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    CLEAR ls_data.
    NEW zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api( )->permit_block_unblock_raise(
      EXPORTING
        iv_permit_id    = lv_permit_id
        iv_blocktype    = lv_blocktype
        iv_comments     = lv_comments
        iv_attachment   = lv_attachment
      IMPORTING
        ev_message_type = DATA(lv_mtype)
        ev_message      = ls_data-msg ).

    IF lv_mtype = 'E'.


      DATA(lv_message) = VALUE scx_t100key( msgid = 'ZMSG'
                                          msgno = '000'
                                          attr1 = ls_data-msg ).


*DATA lv_message TYPE BAPI_MSG .
*        lv_message = ls_data-msg .
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
        EXPORTING
*          message = lv_message
          textid = lv_message
          .

    ELSE.
      copy_data_to_ref(
        EXPORTING
          is_data = ls_data
        CHANGING
          cr_data = er_data
      ).
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.
ENDMETHOD.

* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD /iwbep/if_mgw_appl_srv_runtime~get_expanded_entityset.

    TRY .
        DATA(searchkey) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'SearchKey' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.



    IF searchkey EQ '1' OR searchkey EQ '2'.
      me->get_expanded_fishing(
        EXPORTING
          iv_entity_name           = iv_entity_name
          iv_entity_set_name       = iv_entity_set_name
          iv_source_name           = iv_source_name
          it_filter_select_options = it_filter_select_options
          it_order                 = it_order
          is_paging                = is_paging
          it_navigation_path       = it_navigation_path
          it_key_tab               = it_key_tab
          iv_filter_string         = iv_filter_string
          iv_search_string         = iv_search_string
          io_expand                = io_expand
          io_tech_request_context  = io_tech_request_context
        IMPORTING
          er_entityset             = er_entityset
          et_expanded_clauses      = et_expanded_clauses
          et_expanded_tech_clauses = et_expanded_tech_clauses
      ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    IF searchkey EQ '3'.
      me->get_expanded_noc_consultant(
        EXPORTING
          iv_entity_name           = iv_entity_name
          iv_entity_set_name       = iv_entity_set_name
          iv_source_name           = iv_source_name
          it_filter_select_options = it_filter_select_options
          it_order                 = it_order
          is_paging                = is_paging
          it_navigation_path       = it_navigation_path
          it_key_tab               = it_key_tab
          iv_filter_string         = iv_filter_string
          iv_search_string         = iv_search_string
          io_expand                = io_expand
          io_tech_request_context  = io_tech_request_context
        IMPORTING
          er_entityset             = er_entityset
          et_expanded_clauses      = et_expanded_clauses
          et_expanded_tech_clauses = et_expanded_tech_clauses
      ).

      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    IF iv_entity_name EQ zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>gc_application.
      TYPES:
        BEGIN OF ty_application,
          activitynatureset     TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_activitynature WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          additionalactivityset TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_additionalactivity WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          airpollutionset       TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_airpollution WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          employeecategoryset   TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_employeecategory WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          energyinletset        TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_energyinlet WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          hazardwasteset        TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_hazardwaste WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          liquidwasteset        TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_liquidwaste WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          partnerset            TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_partner WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          productlistset        TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_productlist WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          rawmaterialset        TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_rawmaterial WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          solidwasteset         TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_solidwaste WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          waterinletset         TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_waterinlet WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          subactivityset        TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_subactivity WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          feesset               TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_fees WITH DEFAULT KEY.
          INCLUDE TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_application.
    TYPES END OF ty_application.

      TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_sub_final,
                created_on                 TYPE datum,
                created_time               TYPE uzeit,
                created_by                 TYPE uname,
                created_type               TYPE zde_epda_creat_type,
                subactivity_id             TYPE zde_epda_busin_subactivity_id,
                pay_range                  TYPE zde_epda_pay_range,
                subactivity_description_en TYPE zde_epda_desc_en,
              END OF ty_sub_final ,
              BEGIN OF ty_add_final,
                created_on               TYPE datum,
                created_time             TYPE uzeit,
                created_by               TYPE uname,
                created_type             TYPE zde_epda_creat_type,
                add_activity             TYPE zde_epda_additional_activity,
                add_activity_description TYPE zde_epda_additional_acti_descr,
              END OF ty_add_final.
      TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_subact,
                subactset TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_subactivity WITH DEFAULT KEY.
      TYPES END OF ty_subact.
      TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_addact,
                addactset TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_additionalactivity WITH DEFAULT KEY.
      TYPES END OF ty_addact.
      TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_feeset,
                feesset TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_fees WITH DEFAULT KEY.
      TYPES END OF ty_feeset.
**Nature of Activity
      TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_natureact,
                natureset TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_activitynature WITH DEFAULT KEY.
      TYPES END OF ty_natureact.
      DATA : lt_natureset TYPE TABLE OF ty_natureact,
             ls_natureset TYPE ty_natureact.
**Nature of Activity
      DATA:lt_feeset      TYPE TABLE OF ty_feeset WITH DEFAULT KEY,
           ls_feeset      TYPE ty_feeset,
           lt_subactset   TYPE TABLE OF ty_subact WITH DEFAULT KEY,
           ls_subactset   TYPE ty_subact,
           ls_addactset   TYPE ty_addact,
           lt_subactivn   TYPE TABLE OF zdt_sub_activ,
           ls_subactiv    TYPE zdt_sub_activ,
           lt_subactiv_c  TYPE TABLE OF zdt_sub_activ,
           lt_subactiv_i  TYPE TABLE OF zdt_sub_activ,
           lt_subactiv_f  TYPE TABLE OF ty_sub_final,
           ls_subactiv_f  TYPE ty_sub_final,
           lt_addactvn    TYPE TABLE OF zdt_epda_appl_aa,
           ls_addactv     TYPE  zdt_epda_appl_aa,
           lt_addactiv_c  TYPE TABLE OF zdt_epda_appl_aa,
           lt_addactiv_i  TYPE TABLE OF zdt_epda_appl_aa,
           lt_addactiv_f  TYPE TABLE OF ty_add_final,
           ls_addactiv_f  TYPE ty_add_final,
           ls_application TYPE ty_application,
           lt_application TYPE TABLE OF ty_application WITH DEFAULT KEY,
           lv_comp_en     TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
           lv_comp_ar     TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
           lv_caseid      TYPE scmg_ext_key,
           lv_licno       TYPE zde_ega_fshry_lic_no,
           lv_createdon   TYPE sy-datum,
           lv_createdon_h TYPE sy-datum,
           lv_sign        TYPE char2,
           lv_from_date   TYPE datum,
           lv_to_date     TYPE datum.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_freetext) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'FreeSearch' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_appl_no) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_comp_en = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameEn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_comp_ar = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameAr' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_permitno) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PermitNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_caseid = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CaseId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_createdon   = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CreatedOn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
          lv_sign        = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CreatedOn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-option.
          lv_createdon_h = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CreatedOn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-high.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.
**Getting one week dates
      CLEAR:lv_from_date,lv_to_date.
      IF lv_sign = 'EQ'.
        IF NOT lv_createdon IS INITIAL.
          CALL FUNCTION 'HR_GBSSP_GET_WEEK_DATES'
            EXPORTING
              p_pdate    = lv_createdon "sy-datum
            IMPORTING
              p_sunday   = lv_from_date
              p_saturday = lv_to_date.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        lv_from_date = lv_createdon.
        lv_to_date   = lv_createdon_h.
      ENDIF.
**Getting one week dates
      DATA(lt_children) = io_expand->get_children( ).
      zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
        EXPORTING
          iv_read_type    = 'C'
          iv_freetext     = lv_freetext
*         iv_get_all      = abap_true
          iv_get_sel_node = VALUE zst_epda_bopf_node_read_flg(
                           owners = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'PARTNERSET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                           additional_activities = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'ADDITIONALACTIVITYSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                           air_pollution = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'AIRPOLLUTIONSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                           employee_category = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'EMPLOYEECATEGORYSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                           energy_inlet = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'ENERGYINLETSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                           hazard_waste = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'HAZARDWASTESET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                           liquid_waste = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'LIQUIDWASTESET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                           product_list = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'PRODUCTLISTSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                           raw_materials = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'RAWMATERIALSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                           solid_waste = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'SOLIDWASTESET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                           water_inlet = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'WATERINLETSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )

          iv_from_date    = lv_from_date

          ir_permit_id    = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )

        RECEIVING

***Sub Activity
          IF NOT <ls_data>-sub_activities IS INITIAL.
            SELECT * FROM zdt_sub_activ INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_subactiv)
                     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @<ls_data>-sub_activities
                     WHERE db_key     = @<ls_data>-sub_activities-key
                     AND   parent_key = @<ls_data>-sub_activities-parent_key.
            SELECT * FROM zdt_epda_subactv INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_subtext).
            SORT lt_subactiv DESCENDING BY created_on created_time.
            lt_subactivn = lt_subactiv.
            DELETE lt_subactiv     WHERE created_type NE 'I'.
            DELETE lt_subactivn    WHERE created_type NE 'C'.
**Time Validating
            READ TABLE lt_subactiv INTO ls_subactiv INDEX 1.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              DATA(lv_time_i) = ls_subactiv-created_time.
              DELETE lt_subactiv WHERE created_time NE lv_time_i.
            ENDIF.
            READ TABLE lt_subactivn INTO DATA(ls_subactivn) INDEX 1.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              DATA(lv_time_c) = ls_subactivn-created_time.
              DELETE lt_subactivn WHERE created_time NE lv_time_c.
            ENDIF.
**Time Validating
            SORT lt_subactiv BY subactivity_id.
            DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_subactiv COMPARING subactivity_id.
            SORT lt_subactivn BY subactivity_id.
            DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_subactivn COMPARING subactivity_id.
            LOOP AT lt_subactivn INTO ls_subactivn.
              MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_subactivn TO ls_subactiv.
              APPEND ls_subactiv TO lt_subactiv.
              CLEAR:ls_subactiv.
            ENDLOOP.
            LOOP AT lt_subactiv INTO ls_subactivn.
              MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_subactivn TO ls_subactiv_f.
              READ TABLE lt_subtext INTO DATA(ls_subtext) WITH KEY subactivity_id = ls_subactivn-subactivity_id.
              IF sy-subrc = 0.
                ls_subactiv_f-subactivity_description_en = ls_subtext-subactivity_description_en.
              ENDIF.
              APPEND ls_subactiv_f TO lt_subactiv_f.
              CLEAR:ls_subactiv_f,ls_subtext.
            ENDLOOP.
**Final table prepation
            LOOP AT lt_subactiv_f INTO ls_subactiv_f.
              DATA(ls_sub)    =   VALUE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_subactivity(     application_number = <ls_data>-application_number
                                                                                  activitytext      = ls_subactiv_f-subactivity_description_en
                                                                                  activity          = ls_subactiv_f-subactivity_id
                                                                                  payrange          = ls_subactiv_f-pay_range
                                                                                  created_type       = ls_subactiv_f-created_type ).
              APPEND ls_sub TO ls_subactset-subactset.
            ENDLOOP.
          ENDIF.

* Noc Search
      DATA ls_chiren LIKE LINE OF lt_children.
      ls_chiren-tech_nav_prop_name = 'COMPANY'.

      DATA(lt_noc_data) =    NEW zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api( )->get_noc_cases(
              iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
              iv_from_odata     = abap_true
              iv_get_sel_node   = VALUE zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api=>ty_noc_bopf_node_read_flg(
                                 partner = abap_true"COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'PARTNERSET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 attachment = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'ATTACHMENTSET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 fees = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'FEESSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                                 company = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'COMPANY' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                                 consultant  = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'CONSULTANT' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 )
              iv_from_date      = lv_from_date
              iv_to_date        = lv_to_date
              ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
              ir_permit_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
              ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
          ).

      LOOP AT lt_noc_data INTO DATA(ls_noc_data).
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_noc_data TO ls_application.
        ls_application-searchkey = '3'.
        ls_application-permit_id = ls_noc_data-permit.
        ls_application-dedapplnno = ls_noc_data-ded_appln_no.
        ls_application-dedstatus = ls_noc_data-ded_status_tx.
        ls_application-dednote  = ls_noc_data-ded_note.
        ls_application-dedblocktyp  = ls_noc_data-block_type_tx.
        READ TABLE ls_noc_data-partner INTO DATA(ls_partner) WITH KEY bp_type = '2'. "Company
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          IF ls_noc_data-case_type = 'ZE33'.
            READ TABLE ls_noc_data-company INTO DATA(ls_company) INDEX 1.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              ls_application-comp_name_en = ls_company-company_name_en.
              ls_application-comp_name_ar = ls_company-company_name_ar.
              sy-subrc = 4.
            ENDIF.
          ELSE.
            READ TABLE ls_noc_data-partner INTO ls_partner WITH KEY bp_type = '1'.  "Owner
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          ls_application-companybpid  = ls_partner-owner_id.
          ls_application-comp_name_en = ls_partner-bp_name_en.
          ls_application-comp_name_ar = ls_partner-bp_name.
        ENDIF.

        IF ls_noc_data-permit_issue_date IS INITIAL.
          CLEAR: ls_application-permit_issue_date.
        ENDIF.
        IF ls_noc_data-permit_expiry_date IS INITIAL.
          CLEAR: ls_application-permit_expiry_date.
        ENDIF.
        SHIFT ls_application-case_id LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.
        LOOP AT ls_noc_data-fees INTO DATA(ls_noc_fee) WHERE fee_type NE '000'.
          APPEND INITIAL LINE TO ls_application-feesset ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<feeset>).
          MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_noc_fee TO <feeset>.

          MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_noc_fee TO <feeset>.
        ENDLOOP.

        APPEND ls_application TO lt_application.

*                                                                 |{ <fs_labour>-card_exp_on+4(2) }| &&
*                                                                 |{ <fs_labour>-card_exp_on+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
*                  IF <fs_labour>-worker_status =  abap_true.
*                    ls_labour-status          = 'Active'.
*                  ELSEIF <fs_labour>-worker_status = abap_false.
*                    ls_labour-status          = 'Inactive'.
*                  ENDIF.
*                  IF <fs_labour>-transfer = abap_true.
*                    ls_labour-transfer    = 'Transfer'.
*                  ENDIF.
*                  IF <fs_labour>-transferred = abap_true.
*                    ls_labour-transferred  = 'Transferred'.
*                  ENDIF.
*
*                  ls_labour-transferbegin   = COND string( WHEN <fs_labour>-transfer_beg IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                                          THEN |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_beg+6(2) }| &&
*                                                                               |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_beg+4(2) }| &&
*                                                                               |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_beg+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
*                  ls_labour-transferend     = COND string( WHEN <fs_labour>-transfer_end IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                                          THEN |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_end+6(2) }| &&
*                                                                               |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_end+4(2) }| &&
*                                                                               |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_end+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
*
*                  ls_labour-transferfrom   =  <fs_labour>-transfer_from.
*                  ls_labour-transferto     =  <fs_labour>-transfer_to.
*
*                  APPEND ls_labour TO ls_boat-tolabour.
*                  CLEAR:ls_labour.
*                ENDLOOP.
*              ENDIF.
***Added by Uday
*              IF <fs_boat>-boat_status =  abap_true.
*                ls_boat-status          = 'Active'.
*              ELSEIF <fs_boat>-boat_status = abap_false.
*                ls_boat-status          = 'Inactive'.
*              ENDIF.
*              DELETE  ls_labour_n-boat WHERE transfer NE 'X'.
*              READ TABLE ls_labour_n-boat INTO DATA(ls_boat_new) WITH KEY boat_id = <fs_boat>-boat_id.
*              IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*                IF ls_boat_new-transfer EQ 'X'.
*                  ls_boat-transfer = 'Transferred'.
*                ENDIF.
*              ENDIF.
****Reef Balls
*              LOOP AT lt_reefballs INTO DATA(ls_reefballs).
*                CONDENSE:ls_reefballs-coordinates_n_x,
*                         ls_reefballs-coordinates_e_y.
*                ls_fishing_area-coordinatesnx = ls_reefballs-coordinates_n_x.
*                ls_fishing_area-coordinatesey = ls_reefballs-coordinates_e_y.
*                ls_fishing_area-depth         = ls_reefballs-depth.
*                ls_fishing_area-gps           = ls_reefballs-gps.
*                APPEND ls_fishing_area TO ls_boat-tofishingarea_n.
*              ENDLOOP.
*              APPEND ls_boat TO lt_boat.
*              CLEAR:ls_boat.
*            ENDLOOP.
*          ENDIF.
*          copy_data_to_ref(
*            EXPORTING
*              is_data = lt_boat
*            CHANGING
*              cr_data = er_entityset
*          ).
*          APPEND 'TOLABOUR'        TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
*          APPEND 'TOFISHINGAREA_N' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
*        ENDIF.
        DATA lv_boat_flg TYPE boolean.

        LOOP AT lt_boat_data INTO DATA(ls_boat_data).
          lv_boat_flg = abap_true.
          LOOP AT ls_boat_data-boat INTO DATA(ls_bo_boat).
            CLEAR ls_boat.
            CLEAR lv_boat_flg.

            ls_boat-licenseno = ls_boat_data-lic_no.
            ls_boat-portname = ls_boat_data-port_tx.

            ls_boat-number  = ls_bo_boat-boat_id..
            ls_boat-name  = ls_bo_boat-boat_name.
            ls_boat-type = ls_bo_boat-boat_type_tx." boattype[ domvalue_l = <fs_boat>-boat_type ]-ddtext..
            ls_boat-expirydate = ls_bo_boat-boat_lic_exp_on .
            ls_boat-length  = ls_bo_boat-length.
            ls_boat-enginenumber  = ls_bo_boat-engine_no.
            ls_boat-enginepower  = ls_bo_boat-engine_power.
            ls_boat-numberofcrew  = ls_bo_boat-max_crew_cnt.
            IF ls_bo_boat-transfer EQ abap_true.
              ls_boat-transfer = 'Transferred'.
            ENDIF.
            ls_boat-typeoffishing  = ls_bo_boat-fishing_type_tx.
            ls_boat-status  = ls_bo_boat-boat_status_tx.
            LOOP AT ls_boat_data-workers ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_labour>) WHERE boat_id  =  ls_bo_boat-boat_id.
              ls_labour-licenseno        =   ls_boat_data-lic_no.
              ls_labour-number           =  <fs_labour>-worker_id.
              ls_labour-name             = <fs_labour>-bp_name.
              ls_labour-nationality      = <fs_labour>-nationality.
              ls_labour-nationality_tx   = <fs_labour>-nationality_tx.
              ls_labour-visastatus       = <fs_labour>-visa_status_tx.
              ls_labour-roomno           = <fs_labour>-room_no.
              ls_labour-eid              = <fs_labour>-emirates_id.
              ls_labour-status           = <fs_labour>-worker_status_tx.
              ls_labour-uid              = <fs_labour>-visa_id.
              ls_labour-phoneno          = <fs_labour>-mobile_number.
              ls_labour-boatnumber       = <fs_labour>-boat_id.
              ls_labour-visaexpirydate   = COND string( WHEN <fs_labour>-card_exp_on IS NOT INITIAL
                                                        THEN |{ <fs_labour>-card_exp_on+6(2) }| &&
                                                             |{ <fs_labour>-card_exp_on+4(2) }| &&
                                                             |{ <fs_labour>-card_exp_on+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
              ls_labour-status          = <fs_labour>-worker_status_tx.
              IF <fs_labour>-transfer = abap_true.
                ls_labour-transfer    = 'Transfer'.
              ENDIF.
              IF <fs_labour>-transferred = abap_true.
                ls_labour-transferred  = 'Transferred'.
              ENDIF.

              ls_labour-transferbegin   = COND string( WHEN <fs_labour>-transfer_beg IS NOT INITIAL
                                                                      THEN |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_beg+6(2) }| &&
                                                                           |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_beg+4(2) }| &&
                                                                           |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_beg+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
              ls_labour-transferend     = COND string( WHEN <fs_labour>-transfer_end IS NOT INITIAL
                                                                      THEN |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_end+6(2) }| &&
                                                                           |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_end+4(2) }| &&
                                                                           |{ <fs_labour>-transfer_end+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).

              ls_labour-transferfrom   =  <fs_labour>-transfer_from.
              ls_labour-transferto     =  <fs_labour>-transfer_to.

              APPEND ls_labour TO ls_boat-tolabour.
              CLEAR:ls_labour.
            ENDLOOP.
***Reef Balls
            LOOP AT ls_boat_data-fishing_area INTO DATA(ls_reefballs).
              CONDENSE:ls_reefballs-coordinates_n_x,
                       ls_reefballs-coordinates_e_y.
              ls_fishing_area-coordinatesnx = ls_reefballs-coordinates_n_x.
              ls_fishing_area-coordinatesey = ls_reefballs-coordinates_e_y.
              ls_fishing_area-depth         = ls_reefballs-depth.
              ls_fishing_area-gps           = ls_reefballs-gps.
              APPEND ls_fishing_area TO ls_boat-tofishingarea_n.
            ENDLOOP.
            APPEND ls_boat TO lt_boat.
          ENDLOOP.
          IF lv_boat_flg = abap_true.
            CLEAR ls_boat.
            ls_boat-licenseno = ls_boat_data-lic_no.
            ls_boat-portname = ls_boat_data-port_tx.
            CONDENSE ls_boat-licenseno.
            CONDENSE ls_boat-portname.
            IF ls_boat-licenseno IS NOT INITIAL OR ls_boat-portname IS NOT INITIAL.
              APPEND ls_boat TO lt_boat.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.

        copy_data_to_ref(
          EXPORTING
            is_data = lt_boat
          CHANGING
            cr_data = er_entityset
        ).
        APPEND 'TOLABOUR'        TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'TOFISHINGAREA_N' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.

      ENDIF.



    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->APPLICATIONSET_UPDATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_U(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TS_APPLICATION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD applicationset_update_entity.
    DATA: ls_data TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_application,
          it_data TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_application.
    DATA lt_final TYPE zst_ct_ega_epda_appln_root.
    DATA wa_final TYPE zst_ct_ega_epda_appln_root.
    DATA: lo_message_container TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_message_container.

    io_data_provider->read_entry_data( IMPORTING
        es_data   = ls_data
    ).

    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
      EXPORTING
        iv_read_type      = 'C'    " EPDA Case Read Type
        ir_applic_id      = VALUE #( ( option = 'EQ' sign = 'I' low = ls_data-application_number ) )
      RECEIVING
        et_data           = DATA(lt_data)
    ).
    DATA:  ls_header  TYPE ihttpnvp.

    TRY .
        DATA(ls_appln) = lt_data[ 1 ].
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    IF ls_data-contact_pers_name  IS NOT INITIAL  OR
      ls_data-contact_pers_email  IS NOT INITIAL  OR
      ls_data-contact_pers_mobile IS NOT INITIAL  OR
      ls_data-comp_email          IS NOT INITIAL  OR
      ls_data-comp_phone_no       IS NOT INITIAL.

      IF ls_appln-status EQ '75'.
*        me->/iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_message_container( receiving ro_message_container = lo_message_container ).
*        ls_header-name = 'my-custom-message' .
*        ls_header-value = '{Data status is not completed}'.
*        /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~set_header( ls_header ).

        SELECT application_number
               FROM zdt_epda_appln_r INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_appl) WHERE permit_id EQ @ls_appln-permit_id.
        IF ls_appln-comp_bp_id IS NOT INITIAL.
          zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
            EXPORTING
              iv_read_type      = 'C'    " EPDA Case Read Type
              ir_applic_id      = VALUE #( FOR wa_n IN lt_appl ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa_n-application_number ) )
            RECEIVING
              et_data           = DATA(lt_data_n) ).
          DELETE lt_data_n WHERE comp_bp_id EQ ' '.
          REFRESH lt_appl.
*          DELETE lt_data WHERE status EQ 75.
          DATA ls_applic_n LIKE LINE OF lt_data.
          LOOP AT lt_data_n  INTO DATA(ls_data_n).
            ls_applic_n-application_number = ls_data_n-application_number.
            APPEND ls_applic_n TO lt_data.
            MOVE-CORRESPONDING lt_data TO lt_appl.
          ENDLOOP.
          SORT lt_appl BY application_number.
          DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_appl COMPARING application_number.
        ELSE.
          DELETE lt_appl WHERE application_number NE ls_data-application_number.
          zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
            EXPORTING
              iv_read_type      = 'C'    " EPDA Case Read Type
              ir_applic_id      = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_appl ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-application_number ) )
            RECEIVING
              et_data           = lt_data
            ).

          REFRESH lt_appl.
          DELETE lt_data WHERE status EQ 75.
          DATA ls_applic LIKE LINE OF lt_data.
          ls_applic-application_number = ls_data-application_number.
          APPEND ls_applic TO lt_data.
          MOVE-CORRESPONDING lt_data TO lt_appl.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      APPEND ls_data TO  it_data.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING it_data TO lt_final.


      IF lt_appl IS INITIAL.
        DATA ls_appl LIKE LINE OF lt_appl.
        ls_appl-application_number = ls_data-application_number.
        APPEND ls_appl TO lt_appl.
      ENDIF.

      zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>update_contact(
        EXPORTING
          ir_applnno         = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_appl ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-application_number ) )
          it_appl_details    = lt_final    " EPDA Application Details - #GENERATED#
        IMPORTING
          et_return          = DATA(lt_return)    " Return parameter table
      ).

    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->ATTACHMENTSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_ATTACHMENT
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD attachmentset_get_entityset.
    DATA: lv_wiid   TYPE sww_wiid,
          lv_caseid TYPE scmg_ext_key,
          lv_appln  TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no.

    lv_appln = it_key_tab[ name = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-value.
    IF lv_appln IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE case_id INTO @lv_caseid FROM zdt_epda_appln_r WHERE application_number EQ @lv_appln.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        SELECT SINGLE case_id INTO @lv_caseid FROM zdt_epda_fisry_r WHERE application_number EQ @lv_appln.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      lv_caseid = it_key_tab[ name = 'CaseId' ]-value.
    ENDIF.

    lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
    SELECT SINGLE case_guid,case_type  FROM scmg_t_case_attr INTO @DATA(case) WHERE ext_key EQ @lv_caseid.
    IF case-case_type EQ 'ZE03' OR case-case_type EQ 'ZE04' OR case-case_type EQ 'ZE05' OR case-case_type EQ 'ZE06' OR case-case_type EQ 'ZE07' OR
       case-case_type EQ 'ZE08' OR case-case_type EQ 'ZE09' OR case-case_type EQ 'ZE10' OR case-case_type EQ 'ZE11' OR case-case_type EQ 'ZE12'.
      NEW zcl_scase_gen_attachments( )->get_case_attachments(
        EXPORTING
          spsids      = VALUE #( ( 'ZZRM_SPS_AL_PERMIT/LICENSE_QR' ) )
          case_guid   = case-case_guid
        RECEIVING
          attachments = DATA(attachments)
      ).
      READ TABLE attachments ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_att>) INDEX 1.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        DATA: attachment TYPE zst_att_gen_srv.
        NEW zcl_scase_gen_attachments( )->zif_attachmet_gen~get_attachment(
          EXPORTING
            key        = <fs_att>-gen_key
          CHANGING
            attachment = attachment
        ).
        DATA:lv_length TYPE sapb-length,
             data_x    TYPE xstring.
        lv_length = xstrlen( attachment-data ).
        et_entityset = VALUE #( ( file_data = attachment-data
                                file_descr = attachment-file_descr
                                file_mime_type = attachment-file_mime_type
                                doc_type_desc = attachment-doc_type_desc
                                attachment_key = <fs_att>-gen_key ) ).
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_case_attachment(
        EXPORTING
          iv_case_id        =  lv_caseid   " Case ID
        RECEIVING
          et_attachment     = DATA(lt_data)
      ).
      et_entityset = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data ( CORRESPONDING #( wa ) ) ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->BILLINGDOCUMENTS_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_BILLINGDOCUMENT
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD billingdocuments_get_entityset.
    DATA: lv_comp_en    TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
          lv_comp_ar    TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
          rt_case_id    TYPE RANGE OF scmg_ext_key,
          rt_partner_id TYPE RANGE OF bu_partner,
          lv_caseid     TYPE scmg_ext_key,
          lr_status     TYPE RANGE OF scmgstatusonr,
          ls_entityset  TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_billingdocument.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_freetext) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'FreeSearch' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_appl_no) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        lv_comp_en = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameEn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        lv_comp_ar = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameAr' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_permitno) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PermitNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        lv_caseid = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CaseId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
**Begin of changes by Uday.y
    CALL METHOD zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_user_section
      EXPORTING
        iv_user       = sy-uname
      RECEIVING
        rv_section_id = DATA(lv_sectionid).
**End of changes by Uday.y
    lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
**Begin of changes by Uday.y
    CASE lv_sectionid.
      WHEN '00' OR '03'."All OR "EPDA&FISHERIES
        zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
          EXPORTING
            iv_read_type      = 'C'
            iv_comp_name_en   = lv_comp_en
            iv_comp_name_ar   = lv_comp_ar
            iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
            ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
            ir_permit_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
            ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
          RECEIVING
            et_data           = DATA(lt_data)
        ).

        DATA(lt_fish_data)   = NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_fshry_cases(
          iv_freetext        = lv_freetext
          ir_case_id         = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN  VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
          ir_lic_id          = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
          ir_applic_id       = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
          ).

        DATA(lt_consultant_data)   = NEW zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api( )->get_noc_cases(
              iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
              iv_from_date      = '18000101'
              iv_to_date        = '99991231'
              ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
              ir_permit_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
              ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
              ).
      WHEN '01'. "EPDA
        zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
          EXPORTING
            iv_read_type      = 'C'
            iv_comp_name_en   = lv_comp_en
            iv_comp_name_ar   = lv_comp_ar
            iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
            ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
            ir_permit_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
            ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
          RECEIVING
            et_data           = lt_data
        ).
      WHEN '02'. "FISHERIES
        lt_fish_data         = NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_fshry_cases(
           iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
           ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN  VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
           ir_lic_id         = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
           ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
           ).
      WHEN OTHERS.
        RETURN.
    ENDCASE.
**End of changes by Uday.y
*    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
*      EXPORTING
*        iv_read_type      = 'C'
*        iv_comp_name_en   = lv_comp_en
*        iv_comp_name_ar   = lv_comp_ar
*        iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
*        ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
*        ir_permit_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
*        ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
*      RECEIVING
*        et_data           = DATA(lt_data)
*    ).
*
*    DATA(lt_fish_data) =    NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_fshry_cases(
*      iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
*      ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN  VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
*      ir_lic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
*      ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
*      ).

    LOOP AT lt_data INTO DATA(ls_data_n) WHERE case_type = 'ZE02'.
      ls_data_n-status = '56'.
      MODIFY lt_data FROM ls_data_n TRANSPORTING status.
    ENDLOOP.
    lr_status = VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 20  ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 21  ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 55  ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 56  ) ).

    DELETE lt_data WHERE status NOT IN lr_status.

    rt_case_id = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-case_id ) ) .

    rt_partner_id = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-created_by_bp ) ).

    rt_case_id = VALUE #( BASE rt_case_id FOR wa1 IN lt_fish_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa1-case_id ) ) .

    rt_partner_id = VALUE #( BASE rt_partner_id FOR wa1 IN lt_fish_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa1-created_by ) ).

    rt_case_id = VALUE #( BASE rt_case_id FOR consultant IN lt_consultant_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = consultant-case_id ) ) .

    rt_partner_id = VALUE #( BASE rt_partner_id FOR consultant IN lt_consultant_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = consultant-created_by ) ).

    IF rt_case_id IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT DISTINCT bill_h~vbeln,
                  acct_h~opbel,
                  acct_h~budat,
                  bill_h~kunrg,
                  bill_h~xblnr,
                  bill_h~rfbsk,
                  bill_h~fkart,
                  bill_h~netwr,
                  bill_h~waerk,
                  sale_i~ext_key,
                  cs_attr~case_title,
                  cs_attr~case_type
                  FROM  vbrk              AS bill_h                                        INNER JOIN
                        vbap              AS sale_i   ON bill_h~xblnr = sale_i~vbeln       INNER JOIN
                        scmg_t_case_attr  AS cs_attr  ON sale_i~ext_key = cs_attr~ext_key  INNER JOIN
                        dfkkko            AS acct_h   ON bill_h~vbeln = acct_h~awkey
                        INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_billing_docs)
                        WHERE  sale_i~ext_key    IN @rt_case_id
                        AND ( cs_attr~case_type LIKE 'ZE%' )
*        OR
*                              cs_attr~case_type EQ 'ZE02' )
                        AND sale_i~ext_key IS NOT NULL
*                        AND bill_h~bukrs = 'REPA'
                        AND ( bill_h~fkart = 'ZBV' OR
                              bill_h~fkart = 'YBV' OR
                              bill_h~fkart = 'YQCR' )
                        AND (
                              bill_h~rfbsk = 'C' AND
                              NOT EXISTS ( SELECT *
                                           FROM   dfkkko AS acc_h            INNER JOIN
                                                  dfkkop AS acc_i            ON acc_h~opbel = acc_i~opbel
                                           WHERE  acc_h~awkey = bill_h~vbeln AND
                                                  acc_i~augst = '9' )        AND
                              EXISTS ( SELECT *
                                       FROM   dfkkko AS acc_h2               INNER JOIN
                                              dfkkop AS acc_i2               ON acc_h2~opbel = acc_i2~opbel
                                       WHERE  acc_h2~awkey = bill_h~vbeln    AND
                                              acc_i2~augst = '' )
                             ).
      IF lt_billing_docs IS NOT INITIAL.

        SELECT a~name_text , b~partner FROM adrp AS a LEFT OUTER JOIN but000 AS b ON a~persnumber = b~persnumber
                                                                       INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_adrp)
                                                                       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_billing_docs
                                                                       WHERE b~partner EQ @lt_billing_docs-kunrg.

        LOOP AT lt_billing_docs ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_bd>).
          READ TABLE lt_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_data>) WITH KEY case_id = <fs_bd>-ext_key.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            ls_entityset-applicationnumber = <fs_data>-application_number.
            ls_entityset-applicationtype = <fs_data>-application_type_tx.
            ls_entityset-billingdocid = <fs_bd>-opbel.
            ls_entityset-caseid = |{ <fs_data>-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|.
            ls_entityset-casetitle = <fs_data>-case_title.
            ls_entityset-casetype = <fs_data>-case_type.
            ls_entityset-compnamear = <fs_data>-comp_name_ar.
            ls_entityset-compnameen = <fs_data>-comp_name_en.
            ls_entityset-documentid = <fs_bd>-opbel.
            ls_entityset-payer = <fs_bd>-kunrg.
            ls_entityset-referencedocid = <fs_bd>-xblnr.
            ls_entityset-submissiondate = <fs_data>-created_on.
            ls_entityset-permitnumber = <fs_data>-permit_id.
            TRY .
                ls_entityset-payername = lt_adrp[ partner = <fs_bd>-kunrg ]-name_text.       "name_last.
              CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
            ENDTRY.
            ls_entityset-completeddate = <fs_bd>-budat.
            ls_entityset-netvalue = <fs_bd>-netwr.
            APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
          ENDIF.

          READ TABLE lt_fish_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_fdata>) WITH KEY case_id = <fs_bd>-ext_key.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            ls_entityset-applicationnumber = <fs_fdata>-application_number.
            ls_entityset-applicationtype = <fs_fdata>-lic_type_tx.
            ls_entityset-billingdocid = <fs_bd>-opbel.
            ls_entityset-caseid = |{ <fs_fdata>-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|.
            ls_entityset-casetitle = <fs_fdata>-case_title.
            ls_entityset-casetype = <fs_fdata>-case_type.
            ls_entityset-compnamear = <fs_fdata>-owner_name_ar.
            ls_entityset-compnameen = <fs_fdata>-owner_name.
            ls_entityset-documentid = <fs_bd>-opbel.
            ls_entityset-payer = <fs_bd>-kunrg.
            ls_entityset-referencedocid = <fs_bd>-xblnr.
            ls_entityset-submissiondate = <fs_fdata>-created_on.
            ls_entityset-permitnumber = <fs_fdata>-lic_no.
            TRY .
                ls_entityset-payername = lt_adrp[ partner = <fs_bd>-kunrg ]-name_text.       "name_last.
              CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
            ENDTRY.
            ls_entityset-completeddate = <fs_bd>-budat.
            ls_entityset-netvalue = <fs_bd>-netwr.
            APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
          ENDIF.
          READ TABLE lt_consultant_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_consultant>) WITH KEY case_id = <fs_bd>-ext_key.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            ls_entityset-applicationnumber = <fs_consultant>-application_number.
            ls_entityset-applicationtype = <fs_consultant>-application_type_tx.
            ls_entityset-billingdocid = <fs_bd>-opbel.
            ls_entityset-caseid = |{ <fs_consultant>-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|.
            ls_entityset-casetitle = <fs_consultant>-case_title.
            ls_entityset-casetype = <fs_consultant>-case_type.
            READ TABLE <fs_consultant>-partner INTO DATA(ls_partner) WITH KEY own_type = '2'.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              ls_entityset-compnamear = ls_partner-bp_name.
