              ls_entityset-compnameen = ls_partner-bp_name_en.
            ENDIF.
            ls_entityset-documentid = <fs_bd>-opbel.
            ls_entityset-payer = <fs_bd>-kunrg.
            ls_entityset-referencedocid = <fs_bd>-xblnr.
            ls_entityset-submissiondate = <fs_consultant>-created_on.
            ls_entityset-permitnumber = <fs_consultant>-permit.
            TRY .
                ls_entityset-payername = lt_adrp[ partner = <fs_bd>-kunrg ]-name_text.       "name_last.
              CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
            ENDTRY.
            ls_entityset-completeddate = <fs_bd>-budat.
            ls_entityset-netvalue = <fs_bd>-netwr.
            APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
        SORT et_entityset DESCENDING BY submissiondate.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->CHECK_PAYMENT_FOR_PERMIT
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_PERMIT_ID                   TYPE        ZDE_EGA_PERMIT_REF_NUMBER
* | [<---] EV_BALANCE_TO_PAY              TYPE        BOOLEAN
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD check_payment_for_permit.

    DATA ls_data_n TYPE zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>ty_appl_output_struc. "TT_APPL_OUTPUT_STRUC.
    DATA : rt_case_id TYPE RANGE OF scmg_ext_key,
*           rt_partner_id TYPE RANGE OF bu_partner,
           lr_status  TYPE RANGE OF scmgstatusonr.


    ev_balance_to_pay = abap_false.
    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
        EXPORTING
          iv_read_type      = 'C'
          ir_permit_id      = COND #( WHEN iv_permit_id IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = iv_permit_id ) ) )
        RECEIVING
          et_data           = DATA(lt_data)
      ).

    ls_data_n-status = '56'.
    MODIFY lt_data FROM ls_data_n TRANSPORTING status WHERE case_type = 'ZE02'.

    lr_status = VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 20  ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 21  )
                         ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 55  ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 56  ) ).
    DELETE lt_data WHERE status NOT IN lr_status.
    rt_case_id = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data WHERE ( case_id IS NOT INITIAL ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-case_id ) ) .

    IF rt_case_id IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT DISTINCT bill_h~vbeln,
                  acct_h~opbel,
                  acct_h~budat,
                  bill_h~kunrg,
                  bill_h~xblnr,
                  bill_h~rfbsk,
                  bill_h~fkart,
                  bill_h~netwr,
                  bill_h~waerk,
                  sale_i~ext_key,
                  cs_attr~case_title,
                  cs_attr~case_type
                  FROM  vbrk              AS bill_h                                        INNER JOIN
                        vbap              AS sale_i   ON bill_h~xblnr = sale_i~vbeln       INNER JOIN
                        scmg_t_case_attr  AS cs_attr  ON sale_i~ext_key = cs_attr~ext_key  INNER JOIN
                        dfkkko            AS acct_h   ON bill_h~vbeln = acct_h~awkey
                        INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_billing_docs)
                        WHERE  sale_i~ext_key    IN @rt_case_id
                        AND ( cs_attr~case_type EQ 'ZE01' OR
                              cs_attr~case_type EQ 'ZE02' )
                        AND sale_i~ext_key IS NOT NULL
*                        AND bill_h~bukrs = 'REPA'
                        AND ( bill_h~fkart = 'ZBV' OR
                              bill_h~fkart = 'YBV' OR
                              bill_h~fkart = 'YQCR' )
                        AND (
                              bill_h~rfbsk = 'C' AND
                              NOT EXISTS ( SELECT *
                                           FROM   dfkkko AS acc_h            INNER JOIN
                                                  dfkkop AS acc_i            ON acc_h~opbel = acc_i~opbel
                                           WHERE  acc_h~awkey = bill_h~vbeln AND
                                                  acc_i~augst = '9' )        AND
                              EXISTS ( SELECT *
                                       FROM   dfkkko AS acc_h2               INNER JOIN
                                              dfkkop AS acc_i2               ON acc_h2~opbel = acc_i2~opbel
                                       WHERE  acc_h2~awkey = bill_h~vbeln    AND
                                              acc_i2~augst = '' )
                             ).


      IF lt_billing_docs IS NOT INITIAL.
        ev_balance_to_pay = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->DECISIONSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_DECISION
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD decisionset_get_entityset.
    DATA:lv_wiid        TYPE sww_wiid,
         lr_backendcase TYPE REF TO if_scmg_case,
         lv_apptype     TYPE zde_ega_is_diw_app_type.
    DATA: lo_case_wa TYPE swr_object, lt_actfunc TYPE ztt_scase_act_func,
          lv_return  TYPE sy-subrc,   lv_exerr   TYPE bapi_msg,lv_error TYPE string.
    CLEAR: lv_wiid,lo_case_wa.

    lv_wiid = it_key_tab[ name = 'WiId' ]-value.

    CALL FUNCTION 'SAP_WAPI_GET_OBJECTS'
      EXPORTING
        workitem_id    = lv_wiid
      IMPORTING
        leading_object = lo_case_wa
        return_code    = lv_return.

    CALL FUNCTION 'ZFM_READ_CONTAINER_CASE'
      EXPORTING
        im_wiid         = lv_wiid
        im_object       = lo_case_wa
      IMPORTING
        er_case_backend = lr_backendcase.

    IF  lr_backendcase IS BOUND.
      REFRESH:lt_actfunc[].
      CALL METHOD me->get_activity_function
        EXPORTING
          ir_case    = lr_backendcase
          im_wiid    = lv_wiid
        IMPORTING
          lt_actfunc = lt_actfunc.
      IF lt_actfunc[] IS NOT INITIAL.

        DATA(lv_case_type) = lr_backendcase->if_scmg_case_read~get_case_type( ).
        SELECT * FROM zdt_ega_caprc3 INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_cap)
          WHERE casety EQ @lv_case_type
          ORDER BY activity, actfunc.

        LOOP AT lt_actfunc INTO DATA(ls_actfunc).
          APPEND INITIAL LINE TO et_entityset  ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<entityset>).
          READ TABLE lt_cap INTO DATA(ls_cap)
          WITH KEY activity = ls_actfunc-activity
                   actfunc  = ls_actfunc-actfunc BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0 AND ls_cap-fdval EQ 'A'.
            <entityset>-final_decision = ls_cap-fdset.
          ENDIF.
*          ls_actfunc-activity = ls_actfunc-actfcdc.
          MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_actfunc TO <entityset>.
        ENDLOOP.

      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Private Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->GET_ACTIVITY_FUNCTION
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IR_CASE                        TYPE REF TO IF_SCMG_CASE
* | [--->] IM_WIID                        TYPE        SWW_WIID
* | [<---] LT_ACTFUNC                     TYPE        ZTT_SCASE_ACT_FUNC
* | [<---] EX_POS_ID                      TYPE        SRMWFPOSID
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD get_activity_function.
    DATA:lr_wf_path   TYPE REF TO cl_srm_wf_path,lt_positions TYPE srmwfppost,  lt_actfuncd TYPE  srmactfnctt.
    CHECK ir_case IS BOUND.
    REFRESH: lt_actfunc[].
    CALL METHOD zcl_ega_cahdl_pr=>get_wf_path
      EXPORTING
        ir_case    = ir_case
      IMPORTING
        er_wf_path = lr_wf_path
      EXCEPTIONS
        error      = 1
        OTHERS     = 2.
*  get current position
    CALL METHOD lr_wf_path->get_positions
      RECEIVING
        re_positions = lt_positions.
    READ TABLE  lt_positions ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_pos>) WITH KEY posid_wiid =  im_wiid.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF ex_pos_id IS SUPPLIED.
        ex_pos_id =  <fs_pos>-posid.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
      CALL METHOD lr_wf_path->get_actfunc_desc_by_activity
        EXPORTING
          im_activity         = <fs_pos>-activity
        RECEIVING
          re_tab_actfunc_desc = lt_actfuncd.

      MOVE-CORRESPONDING lt_actfuncd[]  TO lt_actfunc[].
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Private Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->GET_CASE_DETAILS
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_WIID                        TYPE        SWW_WIID
* | [<---] RV_CASE_GUID                   TYPE        SCMG_CASE_GUID
* | [<---] RV_CASE_TYPE                   TYPE        SCMGCASE_TYPE
* | [<---] RV_APPLICATION_TYPE            TYPE        ZDE_EGA_EPDA_APPLICATION_TYPE
* | [<---] RV_ACTIVITY                    TYPE        SRMACTIV
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD get_case_details.

    SELECT SINGLE posid_wiid,pathid,activity FROM srmwfpthps
                      INTO @DATA(ls_srmwfpthps)
                     WHERE posid_wiid = @iv_wiid.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    " get Case Details
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    SELECT SINGLE case_guid,
           wfpthid FROM scmg_t_case
                   INTO @DATA(ls_case)
                  WHERE wfpthid = @ls_srmwfpthps-pathid.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE  ext_key, case_type FROM scmg_t_case_attr INTO ( @DATA(lv_case_id),  @rv_case_type )
      WHERE case_guid = @ls_case-case_guid.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*      SELECT SINGLE case_type application_type  FROM zdt_epda_appln_r INTO ( rv_case_type, rv_application_type )
*        WHERE case_id = lv_case_id.
*      IF sy-subrc = 0.
*      ENDIF.

      SELECT SINGLE application_type  FROM zdt_ega_caat_epd INTO (  rv_application_type )
        WHERE case_guid = ls_case-case_guid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

    rv_case_guid = ls_case-case_guid.
    rv_activity = ls_srmwfpthps-activity.

  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->INSEPECTIONHISTO_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_INSEPECTIONHISTORY
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
METHOD insepectionhisto_get_entityset.
  DATA: lv_comp_en           TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
        lv_comp_ar           TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
        rt_case_id           TYPE RANGE OF scmg_ext_key,
        rt_applno            TYPE RANGE OF zde_ega_epda_application_no,
        rt_partner_id        TYPE RANGE OF bu_partner,
        lv_caseid            TYPE scmg_ext_key,
        lv_appl_no           TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no,
        ls_entity            LIKE LINE OF et_entityset,
        lv_scenario          TYPE char1,
        lt_entity            TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>tt_insepectionhistory,
        lt_entity_temp       TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>tt_insepectionhistory,
        lt_entity_temp1      TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>tt_insepectionhistory,
        lt_inspection_temp   TYPE zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api=>tt_inspection,
        ls_inspection_temp   TYPE zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api=>ty_inspection,
        lt_inspection_temp_n TYPE zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api=>tt_inspection.

  TYPES:
    BEGIN OF ty_inspect,
      checklist  TYPE TABLE OF zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api=>ty_checklist WITH DEFAULT KEY,
      attachment TYPE TABLE OF zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api=>ty_attachment WITH DEFAULT KEY.
      INCLUDE TYPE zst_ega_epda_insp_root.
      INCLUDE TYPE zst_ega_epda_insp_visit.
  TYPES: END OF ty_inspect.

  DATA lt_inspect TYPE TABLE OF ty_inspect.

  DATA lcl_inspe TYPE REF TO zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api.
  CREATE OBJECT lcl_inspe.

  TRY .
      DATA(lv_freetext) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'FreeSearch' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
  ENDTRY.

  TRY .
      lv_appl_no = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
  ENDTRY.

  TRY .
      lv_comp_en = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameEn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
  ENDTRY.

  TRY .
      lv_comp_ar = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameAr' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
  ENDTRY.

  TRY .
      DATA(lv_permitno) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PermitNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
  ENDTRY.

  TRY .
      lv_caseid = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CaseId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
  ENDTRY.

  lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.

  TRY .
      lv_scenario = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'Scenario' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
  ENDTRY.

  IF lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR: lv_caseid,lv_appl_no.
  ENDIF.

  zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
    EXPORTING
      iv_read_type    = 'C'
      iv_comp_name_en = lv_comp_en
      iv_comp_name_ar = lv_comp_ar
      iv_freetext     = lv_freetext
      ir_case_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
      ir_permit_id    = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
      ir_applic_id    = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
    RECEIVING
      et_data         = DATA(lt_data)
  ).

  rt_case_id = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data WHERE ( case_id IS NOT INITIAL ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-case_id ) ).
  rt_applno  = VALUE #( FOR wa1 IN lt_data WHERE ( application_number IS NOT INITIAL )  ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa1-application_number ) ).


  DATA(lt_fish_data)  = NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_fshry_cases(
*iv_get_sel_node     = ls_sel_node
                      iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
                      ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN  VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
                      ir_lic_id         = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
                      ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
                      ).
  IF NOT lt_fish_data IS INITIAL.
    rt_case_id = VALUE #( FOR wa2 IN lt_fish_data WHERE ( case_id IS NOT INITIAL ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa2-case_id ) ).
    rt_applno  = VALUE #( FOR wa3 IN lt_fish_data WHERE ( application_number IS NOT INITIAL )  ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa3-application_number ) ).
  ENDIF.

  lcl_inspe->get_inspection(
    EXPORTING
      ir_case_id            = rt_case_id   " Case ID
      ir_application_number = rt_applno   " RAK EGA EPDA Application Number
    IMPORTING
      et_inspection         = DATA(lt_inspection)
      et_return             = DATA(lt_return)    " Return parameter table
  ).
  IF  lv_freetext IS INITIAL AND lv_appl_no IS INITIAL AND
      lv_comp_en  IS INITIAL AND lv_comp_ar IS INITIAL AND
      lv_permitno IS INITIAL AND lv_caseid  IS INITIAL AND
      lv_scenario EQ '1'.
*    SORT lt_inspection BY case_id DESCENDING.
*    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_inspection COMPARING inspection_id.
    SORT lt_inspection BY inspection_id ASCENDING.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_inspection COMPARING inspection_id.
    SORT lt_inspection BY inspect_actual_date  DESCENDING inspect_actual_time DESCENDING.
    lt_inspection_temp    = lt_inspection.
    lt_inspection_temp_n  = lt_inspection.
    DELETE lt_inspection_temp WHERE permitid = ' '.
    IF NOT lt_inspection_temp IS INITIAL.
      SORT lt_inspection_temp BY permitid DESCENDING.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_inspection_temp COMPARING permitid.
    ENDIF.
    LOOP AT lt_inspection_temp_n INTO DATA(ls_inspection_temp_n).
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_inspection_temp_n TO ls_inspection_temp.
      APPEND ls_inspection_temp TO lt_inspection_temp.
    ENDLOOP.
    SORT lt_data BY created_on DESCENDING.
  ELSE.
    SORT lt_inspection BY inspection_id ASCENDING seqno DESCENDING.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_inspection COMPARING inspection_id.
  ENDIF.
*    SORT lt_inspection BY inspection_id ASCENDING seqno DESCENDING.
*    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_inspection COMPARING inspection_id.
  IF NOT lt_inspection_temp IS INITIAL.
    LOOP AT lt_inspection INTO DATA(ls_inspection).
      READ TABLE lt_inspection_temp INTO ls_inspection_temp WITH KEY inspection_id = ls_inspection-inspection_id.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        ls_entity-caseid = |{ ls_inspection-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|.
        ls_entity-applicationnumber = ls_inspection-application_number.

        READ TABLE lt_data INTO DATA(ls_data) WITH KEY application_number = ls_inspection-application_number.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          ls_entity-applicationtype = ls_data-application_type.
          ls_entity-applicationtypetx = ls_data-application_type_tx.
          ls_entity-businesstype = ls_data-business_type.
          ls_entity-businesstypetx = ls_data-businees_type_description.
          ls_entity-companybpid = ls_data-comp_bp_id.
          ls_entity-companyname = ls_data-comp_name_en.
          ls_entity-expirydate = COND string( WHEN ls_data-permit_expiry_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                             THEN |{ ls_data-permit_expiry_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_data-permit_expiry_date+4(2) }| &&
                                             |{ ls_data-permit_expiry_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' ).
          ls_entity-issuedate =  COND string( WHEN ls_data-permit_issue_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                             THEN |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+4(2) }| &&
                                             |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' ).
          ls_entity-issuedate = COND string( WHEN ls_data-permit_issue_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                     THEN |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+4(2) }| &&
                                     |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' )..
          ls_entity-permitnumber = ls_data-permit_id.
        ENDIF.

        READ TABLE lt_fish_data INTO DATA(ls_fish_data) WITH KEY application_number = ls_inspection-application_number.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          ls_entity-applicationtype = ls_fish_data-lic_type.
          ls_entity-applicationtypetx = ls_fish_data-lic_type_tx.
          ls_entity-businesstype = ls_inspection-business_type.
          ls_entity-businesstypetx = ls_inspection-businees_type_description.
          ls_entity-companybpid = ls_fish_data-owner_id.
          ls_entity-companyname = ls_fish_data-owner_name.
          ls_entity-permitnumber = ls_fish_data-lic_no.
          ls_entity-expirydate = COND string( WHEN ls_fish_data-expire_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                               THEN |{ ls_fish_data-expire_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_fish_data-expire_date+4(2) }| &&
                                               |{ ls_fish_data-expire_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' ).
          ls_entity-issuedate =  COND string( WHEN ls_fish_data-issue_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                             THEN |{ ls_fish_data-issue_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_fish_data-issue_date+4(2) }| &&
                                             |{ ls_fish_data-issue_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' ).
        ENDIF.

        ls_entity-inspectiondate    = COND string( WHEN ls_inspection-completeddate IS NOT INITIAL
                                        THEN |{ ls_inspection-completeddate+6(2) }| &&
                                        |{ ls_inspection-completeddate+4(2) }| &&
                                        |{ ls_inspection-completeddate+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
        ls_entity-inspectionid = ls_inspection-inspection_id.
        ls_entity-inspectorname = ls_inspection-inspected_by_name.
        ls_entity-previousstatus = ls_inspection-pre_inspection_status_tx.
        ls_entity-submissiondate = COND string( WHEN ls_inspection-inspect_submit_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                           THEN |{ ls_inspection-inspect_submit_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_inspection-inspect_submit_date+4(2) }| &&
                                           |{ ls_inspection-inspect_submit_date+0(4) }| &&
                                    |{ ls_inspection-inspect_submit_time }| ELSE '' ).
        ls_entity-currentinspectioresult = ls_inspection-status.
        ls_entity-currentstatus = ls_inspection-status_tx.
        APPEND ls_entity TO lt_entity.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSE.
    LOOP AT lt_inspection INTO ls_inspection.
      ls_entity-caseid = |{ ls_inspection-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|.
      ls_entity-applicationnumber = ls_inspection-application_number.
      READ TABLE lt_data INTO ls_data WITH KEY application_number = ls_inspection-application_number.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        ls_entity-applicationtype = ls_data-application_type.
        ls_entity-applicationtypetx = ls_data-application_type_tx.
        ls_entity-businesstype = ls_data-business_type.
        ls_entity-businesstypetx = ls_data-businees_type_description.
        ls_entity-companybpid = ls_data-comp_bp_id.
        ls_entity-companyname = ls_data-comp_name_en.
        ls_entity-expirydate = COND string( WHEN ls_data-permit_expiry_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                           THEN |{ ls_data-permit_expiry_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_data-permit_expiry_date+4(2) }| &&
                                           |{ ls_data-permit_expiry_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' ).
        ls_entity-issuedate =  COND string( WHEN ls_data-permit_issue_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                           THEN |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+4(2) }| &&
                                           |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' ).
        ls_entity-issuedate = COND string( WHEN ls_data-permit_issue_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                     THEN |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+4(2) }| &&
                                     |{ ls_data-permit_issue_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' )..
        ls_entity-permitnumber = ls_data-permit_id.
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE lt_fish_data INTO ls_fish_data WITH KEY application_number = ls_inspection-application_number.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        ls_entity-applicationtype = ls_fish_data-lic_type.
        ls_entity-applicationtypetx = ls_fish_data-lic_type_tx.
        ls_entity-businesstype = ls_inspection-business_type.
        ls_entity-businesstypetx = ls_inspection-businees_type_description.
        ls_entity-companybpid = ls_fish_data-owner_id.
        ls_entity-companyname = ls_fish_data-owner_name.
        ls_entity-permitnumber = ls_fish_data-lic_no.
        ls_entity-expirydate = COND string( WHEN ls_fish_data-expire_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                             THEN |{ ls_fish_data-expire_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_fish_data-expire_date+4(2) }| &&
                                             |{ ls_fish_data-expire_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' ).
        ls_entity-issuedate =  COND string( WHEN ls_fish_data-issue_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                           THEN |{ ls_fish_data-issue_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_fish_data-issue_date+4(2) }| &&
                                           |{ ls_fish_data-issue_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' ).
      ENDIF.

      ls_entity-currentinspectioresult = ls_inspection-status.
      ls_entity-currentstatus = ls_inspection-status_tx.

      ls_entity-inspectiondate    = COND string( WHEN ls_inspection-completeddate IS NOT INITIAL
                                      THEN |{ ls_inspection-completeddate+6(2) }| &&
                                      |{ ls_inspection-completeddate+4(2) }| &&
                                      |{ ls_inspection-completeddate+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
      ls_entity-inspectionid = ls_inspection-inspection_id.
      ls_entity-inspectorname = ls_inspection-inspected_by_name.

      ls_entity-previousstatus = ls_inspection-pre_inspection_status_tx.
      ls_entity-submissiondate = COND string( WHEN ls_inspection-inspect_submit_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                         THEN |{ ls_inspection-inspect_submit_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_inspection-inspect_submit_date+4(2) }| &&
                                         |{ ls_inspection-inspect_submit_date+0(4) }| &&
                                  |{ ls_inspection-inspect_submit_time }| ELSE '' ).

      APPEND ls_entity TO lt_entity.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
  IF lv_scenario EQ '1'.
    "Only these changes are needed for header so we introduced a parameter to differentiate
    SELECT * FROM zdt_epda_conhist INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_permits)
                                   FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_data WHERE contract_no EQ @lt_data-permit_id.
    SORT lt_permits ASCENDING BY issue_date.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_permits COMPARING contract_no.

    LOOP AT lt_entity ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_temp>).
      IF <fs_temp>-permitnumber IS INITIAL.
        APPEND <fs_temp> TO lt_entity_temp.
      ELSE.
        APPEND <fs_temp> TO lt_entity_temp1.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_entity_temp1 COMPARING permitnumber.
    APPEND LINES OF lt_entity_temp1 TO lt_entity_temp.

    LOOP AT lt_entity ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_last>).
      READ TABLE lt_entity_temp INTO ls_entity WITH KEY applicationnumber = <fs_last>-applicationnumber caseid = <fs_last>-caseid.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        READ TABLE lt_permits INTO DATA(ls_permit) WITH KEY contract_no = <fs_last>-permitnumber.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          IF <fs_last>-permitnumber IS NOT INITIAL.
            ls_entity-issuedate = COND string( WHEN ls_permit-issue_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                         THEN |{ ls_permit-issue_date+6(2) }| && |{ ls_permit-issue_date+4(2) }| &&
                                         |{ ls_permit-issue_date+0(4) }|  ELSE '' ).
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        APPEND ls_entity TO et_entityset.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSE.
    APPEND LINES OF lt_entity TO et_entityset.
  ENDIF.
*    SORT et_entityset BY submissiondate DESCENDING.
ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->INSPECTIONHIST01_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_INSPECTIONHISTLTWO
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD inspectionhist01_get_entityset.
    DATA: lv_caseid TYPE scmg_ext_key,
          lv_app_no TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no,
          lv_insp   TYPE zde_epda_inspection,
          ls_entity LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.

    DATA:  lv_comp_en  TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
           lv_comp_ar  TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
*              rt_applno  TYPE RANGE OF zde_ega_epda_application_no,
           rt_busintyp TYPE RANGE OF zde_ega_business_type.

    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ty_inspect,
        checklist TYPE TABLE OF zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api=>ty_checklist WITH DEFAULT KEY,
        images    TYPE TABLE OF zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api=>ty_attachment WITH DEFAULT KEY.
            INCLUDE TYPE zst_ega_epda_insp_root.
            INCLUDE TYPE zst_ega_epda_insp_visit.
    TYPES: END OF ty_inspect.

    DATA:  ls_inspect TYPE ty_inspect,
           lt_inspect TYPE TABLE OF ty_inspect.

    DATA:  lcl_inspe TYPE REF TO zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api.
    CREATE OBJECT lcl_inspe.

    lv_caseid = it_key_tab[ name = 'CaseId' ]-value.
    lv_app_no = it_key_tab[ name = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-value.
    lv_insp = it_key_tab[ name = 'InspectionId' ]-value.

    lcl_inspe->get_inspection(
      EXPORTING
        ir_inspection_id      =  COND #( WHEN lv_insp IS NOT INITIAL THEN  VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_insp ) ) )   " Inspection ID
      IMPORTING
        et_inspection      = DATA(lt_data)
        et_return          = DATA(lt_return)    " Return parameter table
    ).

      zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
        EXPORTING
          iv_read_type      = 'C'    " EPDA Case Read Type
          ir_applic_id      =  COND #( WHEN lv_insp IS NOT INITIAL THEN  VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_insp ) ) )   " I
          ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN  VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
        RECEIVING
          et_data           = DATA(lt_csdata)
      ).

    LOOP AT lt_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_data>).
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data> TO ls_inspect.
      READ TABLE lt_csdata INTO DATA(ls_csdata) WITH KEY application_number = <fs_data>-application_number.
      ls_inspect-images = VALUE #( BASE ls_inspect-images FOR wa1 IN <fs_data>-attachment
                                                          ( CORRESPONDING #( BASE ( VALUE #( inspection_id = ls_inspect-inspection_id ) )
                                                           wa1 ) ) ).
      ls_inspect-application_number =  <fs_data>-application_number.
      ls_inspect-inspection_id = <fs_data>-inspection_id.

      APPEND ls_entity TO et_entityset.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->INSPECTIONHISTLO_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_INSPECTIONHISTLONE
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD inspectionhistlo_get_entityset.
    DATA: lv_caseid TYPE scmg_ext_key,
          lv_app_no TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no,
          lv_insp   TYPE zde_epda_inspection,
          ls_entity LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.

    DATA:  lv_comp_en TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
           lv_comp_ar TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
           rt_applno  TYPE RANGE OF zde_ega_epda_application_no,
           rt_case_id TYPE RANGE OF scmg_ext_key.


    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ty_inspect,
        checklist  TYPE TABLE OF zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api=>ty_checklist WITH DEFAULT KEY,
        attachment TYPE TABLE OF zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api=>ty_attachment WITH DEFAULT KEY.
            INCLUDE TYPE zst_ega_epda_insp_root.
            INCLUDE TYPE zst_ega_epda_insp_visit.
    TYPES: END OF ty_inspect.

    DATA lt_inspect TYPE TABLE OF ty_inspect.
    DATA lt_inspect_temp TYPE zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api=>tt_inspection.

    DATA  lcl_inspe TYPE REF TO zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api.
    CREATE OBJECT lcl_inspe.


    IF iv_source_name EQ 'InsepectionHistory'.
      lv_caseid = it_key_tab[ name = 'CaseId' ]-value.
      lv_app_no = it_key_tab[ name = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-value.
      lv_insp = it_key_tab[ name = 'InspectionId' ]-value.

    ELSE.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_freetext) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'FreeSearch' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_app_no = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_comp_en = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameEn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_comp_ar = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameAr' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_permitno) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PermitNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_caseid = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CaseId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_status) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CurrentStatus' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.


      TRY .
          lv_insp = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'InspectionId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.
    ENDIF.

    lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.

    IF lv_insp IS INITIAL.
      zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
        EXPORTING
          iv_read_type      = 'C'
          iv_comp_name_en   = lv_comp_en
          iv_comp_name_ar   = lv_comp_ar
          iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
          ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN  VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
          ir_permit_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
          ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_app_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_app_no ) ) )
        RECEIVING
          et_data           = DATA(lt_data)
      ).
      rt_case_id = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-case_id ) ).
      rt_applno  = VALUE #( FOR wa1 IN lt_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa1-application_number ) ).
    ENDIF.

    lcl_inspe->get_inspection(
      EXPORTING
        ir_inspection_id      =  COND #( WHEN lv_insp IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_insp ) ) )   " Inspection ID
        ir_case_id            =  rt_case_id  " Case ID
        ir_application_number =  rt_applno
             IMPORTING
        et_inspection      = DATA(lt_inspection)
        et_return          = DATA(lt_return)    " Return parameter table
    ).
*

    IF lv_status IS NOT INITIAL.
      SORT lt_inspection DESCENDING BY inspect_actual_date inspect_actual_time.
*    delete ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_inspection COMPARING inspection_id.
      IF lv_insp IS NOT INITIAL.
        DELETE lt_inspection WHERE inspection_id NE lv_insp.
      ENDIF.
      IF lv_status EQ 'C'.
        DELETE lt_inspection WHERE inspection_status NE '01'.
      ELSEIF lv_status EQ 'NC'.
        DELETE lt_inspection WHERE status EQ '01'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF NOT lv_insp IS INITIAL.
      REFRESH:rt_case_id,rt_applno.
      lt_inspect_temp[] = lt_inspection[].
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_inspect_temp[] COMPARING case_id.
      rt_case_id = VALUE #( FOR wa_n  IN lt_inspect_temp ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa_n-case_id ) ).
      rt_applno  = VALUE #( FOR wa_n1 IN lt_inspect_temp ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa_n1-application_number ) ).
      zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
        EXPORTING
          iv_read_type      = 'C'
          ir_case_id        = rt_case_id
          ir_applic_id      = rt_applno
        RECEIVING
          et_data           = lt_data ).
    ENDIF.

    LOOP AT lt_inspection INTO DATA(ls_inspection).
      READ TABLE lt_data INTO DATA(ls_data) WITH KEY application_number = ls_inspection-application_number.
      ls_entity-caseid = |{ ls_inspection-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|.
      ls_entity-applicationnumber = ls_inspection-application_number.
      ls_entity-applicationtype = ls_data-application_type.
      ls_entity-applicationtypetx = ls_data-application_type_tx.
      ls_entity-currentstatus = ls_inspection-status_tx.
      ls_entity-seqno = ls_inspection-seqno.
      ls_entity-inspectiondate = COND string( WHEN ls_inspection-inspect_actual_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                         THEN |{ ls_inspection-inspect_actual_date+6(2) }| &&
                                         |{ ls_inspection-inspect_actual_date+4(2) }| &&
                                         |{ ls_inspection-inspect_actual_date+0(4) }| &&
                                  |{ ls_inspection-inspect_actual_time }| ELSE '' ).
      ls_entity-inspectionid = ls_inspection-inspection_id.
      ls_entity-inspectorname = ls_inspection-inspected_by_name.
*            ls_entity-issuedate = ls_data-permit_issue_date.
*            ls_entity-pendingby = ls_inspection-
***      ls_entity-permitnumber = ls_data-permit_id.
      ls_entity-previousstatus = ls_inspection-inspection_status_tx.
*            ls_entity-submissiondate = |{ ls_inspection-inspect_submit_date }| &&
*                                        |{ ls_inspection-inspect_submit_time }|.
      ls_entity-operat_status    = ls_inspection-operat_status.
      ls_entity-operat_status_tx = ls_inspection-operat_status_tx.
      ls_entity-violation        = ls_inspection-violation.
      ls_entity-violation_tx     = ls_inspection-violation_tx.
      ls_entity-remarks          = ls_inspection-remarks.
      APPEND ls_entity TO et_entityset.
    ENDLOOP.

    SORT et_entityset DESCENDING BY seqno." inspectiondate.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->NOTESET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_NOTE
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD noteset_get_entityset.

    TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_lines,
              tag   TYPE string,
              notes TYPE string,
            END OF ty_lines.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS : <fs_filter> TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option,
                    <fs_opt>    TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.

    DATA :       lv_wid          TYPE swwwihead-wi_id,
                 lo_case_wa      TYPE swr_object,
                 lv_return       TYPE sy-subrc,
                 lr_case_backend TYPE REF TO if_scmg_case,
                 lt_lines        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_lines,
                 ls_lines        TYPE ty_lines,
                 lv_error        TYPE string,
                 lv_exerr        TYPE bapi_msg,
                 ls_return       TYPE bapiret2,
                 lt_headers      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapinoteheader,
                 ls_headers      TYPE bapinoteheader,
                 lt_contents     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapinote,
                 ls_contents     TYPE bapinote,
                 lv_tag          TYPE string,
                 lv_result_notes TYPE string,
                 lv_first        TYPE xfeld,
                 lv_uname        TYPE bapibname-bapibname,
                 ls_address      TYPE bapiaddr3,
                 lt_return       TYPE TABLE OF bapiret2.


    lv_wid = it_key_tab[ name = 'WiId' ]-value.

* Get Case ID from Workitem ID
    CALL FUNCTION 'SAP_WAPI_GET_OBJECTS'
      EXPORTING
        workitem_id    = lv_wid
      IMPORTING
        leading_object = lo_case_wa
        return_code    = lv_return.

    CALL FUNCTION 'ZFM_READ_CONTAINER_CASE'
      EXPORTING
        im_wiid         = lv_wid
        im_object       = lo_case_wa
      IMPORTING
        er_case_backend = lr_case_backend.

    DATA(lv_case_guid) = lr_case_backend->if_scmg_case_read~get_guid( ).

* Get note text
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_CASE_GETNOTES'
      EXPORTING
        guid          = lv_case_guid
*       text_id       = 'ZDWI'
      IMPORTING
        return        = ls_return
      TABLES
        note_headers  = lt_headers
        note_contents = lt_contents.

    SORT lt_headers DESCENDING BY elem_no.

    IF lt_headers IS NOT INITIAL.
      LOOP AT lt_headers INTO ls_headers.
        LOOP AT lt_contents INTO ls_contents WHERE elem_no = ls_headers-elem_no.
          IF lv_first NE 'X'.
* #001           ls_lines-tag = ls_contents-tdline+30(50).
            lv_uname = ls_contents-tdline+30(20).     " #001
            DATA(lv_timestamp) = ls_contents-tdline+50(20). " #001

            CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_USER_GET_DETAIL'
              EXPORTING
                username = lv_uname
              IMPORTING
                address  = ls_address
              TABLES
                return   = lt_return.
            IF ls_address IS NOT INITIAL.
              CASE sy-langu.
                WHEN 'E'.
                  DATA(lv_notefrom) = 'Note From'.
                WHEN OTHERS.
                  lv_notefrom = 'ملاحظة من'.
              ENDCASE.

              CONCATENATE lv_notefrom ls_address-fullname lv_timestamp INTO ls_lines-tag
                              SEPARATED BY space.
            ENDIF.
* #003 - end of changes

            lv_tag = ls_lines-tag.lv_first = 'X'.
            CONTINUE.
          ENDIF.
          CONCATENATE lv_result_notes ls_contents-tdline INTO lv_result_notes SEPARATED BY space.
          CLEAR : ls_address, lv_notefrom, lv_timestamp. "#003
        ENDLOOP.

        ls_lines-tag = lv_tag.
        ls_lines-notes = lv_result_notes.
        APPEND ls_lines TO lt_lines.
        CLEAR: lv_first,lv_tag,lv_result_notes.
      ENDLOOP.

      MOVE-CORRESPONDING lt_lines[] TO et_entityset[].
    ELSE.
      CALL METHOD cl_wd_utilities=>get_otr_text_by_alias
        EXPORTING
          alias      = 'Z_ABAP/ZEGA_COURT_NODATA_FOUND'
          language   = sy-langu
        RECEIVING
          alias_text = lv_error.

      lv_exerr =  lv_error.

      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
        EXPORTING
          textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
          message = lv_exerr.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->OLDADDITIONALACT_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_OLDADDITIONALACT
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD oldadditionalact_get_entityset.
    TYPES: BEGIN OF ts_oldcomp,
             count     TYPE char05,
             case_id   TYPE scmg_ext_key,
             applic_no TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no,
           END OF ts_oldcomp.

    DATA: ls_oldadd TYPE ts_oldcomp,
          lt_oldadd TYPE TABLE OF ts_oldcomp,
          lv_count  TYPE char05.

    DATA:rt_applno TYPE RANGE OF zde_ega_epda_application_no.
    DATA: lv_permitno TYPE recnnr,
          lv_caseid   TYPE  zde_ega_case_id,
          lv_applnos  TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no,
          ls_entity   LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.

    DATA(lv_applno) = it_key_tab[ name = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-value.

    SELECT SINGLE application_number,
                  permit_id,
                  amend_add_actv FROM zdt_epda_appln_r INTO @DATA(ls_permit) WHERE application_number EQ @lv_applno.

*    rt_applno = value #( for wa in lt_permit ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-application_number  ) ).
    lv_permitno = ls_permit-permit_id.

    IF lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL AND ls_permit-amend_add_actv IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT case_id,
             application_number FROM zdt_epda_appln_r INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_appl) WHERE permit_id EQ @lv_permitno.

      SORT lt_appl ASCENDING BY case_id.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        LOOP AT lt_appl INTO DATA(ls_appl).
          ls_oldadd-count = ls_oldadd-count + 1.
          ls_oldadd-case_id = ls_appl-case_id.
          ls_oldadd-applic_no = ls_appl-application_number.
          APPEND ls_oldadd TO lt_oldadd.
        ENDLOOP.

        READ TABLE lt_oldadd INTO ls_oldadd  WITH KEY applic_no = lv_applno.
        lv_count = ls_oldadd-count - 1.
        READ TABLE lt_oldadd INTO ls_oldadd WITH KEY count = lv_count.

        lv_applnos = ls_oldadd-applic_no.

        zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
          EXPORTING
            iv_read_type    = 'C'
            iv_get_sel_node = VALUE zst_epda_bopf_node_read_flg(
                                                        additional_activities = abap_true
                                                         )
            ir_applic_id    = COND #( WHEN lv_applnos IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_applnos ) ) )
          RECEIVING
            et_data         = DATA(lt_data)
        ).

        LOOP AT lt_data INTO DATA(ls_act).
          SORT ls_act-additional_activities DESCENDING BY created_on created_time created_type.
          READ TABLE ls_act-additional_activities INTO DATA(ls_oldact) INDEX 1.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            DELETE ls_act-additional_activities
                             WHERE NOT ( created_on = ls_oldact-created_on
                               AND created_time = ls_oldact-created_time ).
          ENDIF.
          CLEAR ls_oldact.

          LOOP AT ls_act-additional_activities INTO ls_oldact.
            ls_entity-applicationnumber = ls_act-application_number.
            ls_entity-oldactivity = ls_oldact-add_activity.
            ls_entity-oldactivitytext = ls_oldact-main_activity_text.

            APPEND ls_entity TO et_entityset.
          ENDLOOP.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->OVERVIEWSET_CREATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_C(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TS_OVERVIEW
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD overviewset_create_entity.

    DATA: ls_deep_entity TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc_ext=>ts_overview,
          lo_case_wa     TYPE swr_object,
          lv_case_guid   TYPE scmg_case_guid,
          lv_wid         TYPE sww_wiid,
          lv_execfunc    TYPE srmactfc,
          lv_return      TYPE sy-subrc,
          lr_backendcase TYPE REF TO if_scmg_case,
          lv_exerr       TYPE bapi_msg,
          lv_error       TYPE string,
          lr_capr        TYPE REF TO zif_ega_capr,
          lv_posid       TYPE srmwfpthps-posid,
          lv_offset      TYPE i VALUE 72,
          lv_substring   TYPE string,
          lv_length      TYPE i,
          ls_return      TYPE bapiret2,
          lv_tdid        TYPE bapitdid.
    DATA:lr_case               TYPE REF TO zcl_ega_rmps_case,
         lr_srm                TYPE REF TO         if_srm,
         lt_messages           TYPE scmg_t_attr_return_value,
         lr_case_poid          TYPE REF TO if_srm_poid,
         lr_crmroot            TYPE  REF TO  if_srm_root,
         lr_cahdl              TYPE REF TO zif_ega_cahdl,
         lr_factory            TYPE REF TO if_srm_sps_factory,
         lr_case_visualization TYPE REF TO zcl_ega_rmps_case_vis_win.

    DATA lv_md_skip       TYPE boolean_flg.

    gv_call_from_fiori = abap_true.

    io_data_provider->read_entry_data( IMPORTING es_data = ls_deep_entity ) .


    lv_wid      = ls_deep_entity-wi_id.
    lv_execfunc = ls_deep_entity-actfunc.
    lv_md_skip  = ls_deep_entity-md_skip.


    DATA lv_case_type TYPE scmgcase_type.
    DATA lv_application_type TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_type.
    DATA lv_activity TYPE srmactiv.

    CALL METHOD get_case_details
      EXPORTING
        iv_wiid             = lv_wid
      IMPORTING
        rv_case_guid        = lv_case_guid
        rv_application_type = lv_application_type
        rv_case_type        = lv_case_type
        rv_activity         = lv_activity.

    IF lv_case_type = 'ZE01' AND lv_case_type = 'ZE02'.
      CALL METHOD validate_on_user_action
        EXPORTING
          iv_wiid     = lv_wid
          iv_activity = lv_activity
          iv_execfunc = lv_execfunc
          iv_md_skip  = lv_md_skip
        IMPORTING
          et_return   = DATA(lt_return).
      IF lt_return IS NOT INITIAL.
        READ TABLE lt_return INTO ls_return INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          DATA lv_msg_code TYPE string.
          CLEAR lv_msg_code.
          IF ls_return-parameter IS NOT INITIAL.
            lv_msg_code = ls_return-parameter.
          ENDIF.

          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
            EXPORTING
              textid   = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
              message  = ls_return-message
              msg_code = lv_msg_code.
          RETURN.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_activity = 'ZE01MOD'.
      SELECT SINGLE tdid FROM zdt_epda_map_act INTO lv_tdid
        WHERE case_type = lv_case_type
          AND application_type =  lv_application_type
          AND activity = lv_activity
          AND funcresult =  lv_execfunc.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.

      IF lv_case_type = 'ZE01' or lv_case_type = 'ZE02' .
        SELECT SINGLE tdid FROM zdt_epda_map_act INTO lv_tdid
          WHERE case_type = lv_case_type
            AND application_type =  lv_application_type
            AND activity = lv_activity.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
        ENDIF.

      ELSE.
        IF lv_execfunc IS NOT INITIAL.
          SELECT SINGLE tdid FROM zdt_epda_map_act INTO lv_tdid
            WHERE case_type = lv_case_type
              AND application_type =  lv_application_type
              AND activity = lv_activity
              AND funcresult =  lv_execfunc.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
            SELECT SINGLE tdid FROM zdt_epda_map_act INTO lv_tdid
              WHERE case_type = lv_case_type
                AND application_type =  lv_application_type
                AND activity = lv_activity.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*  lv_tdid = COND #( WHEN lv_execfunc EQ 'EFKT02' THEN 'ZREJ' ELSE 'ZAPR' ).

    CALL FUNCTION 'SAP_WAPI_GET_OBJECTS'
      EXPORTING
        workitem_id    = lv_wid
      IMPORTING
        leading_object = lo_case_wa
        return_code    = lv_return.

    CALL FUNCTION 'ZFM_READ_CONTAINER_CASE'
      EXPORTING
        im_wiid         = lv_wid
        im_object       = lo_case_wa
      IMPORTING
        er_case_backend = lr_backendcase.
    IF  lr_backendcase IS BOUND.
      IF ls_deep_entity-comments IS NOT INITIAL.
        DATA: lv_comment TYPE string.
        lv_comment = ls_deep_entity-comments.
        post_notes(
          EXPORTING
            iv_tdid      = lv_tdid
            iv_notes     = lv_comment
            iv_case_guid = lv_case_guid
          IMPORTING
            es_return    = ls_return
        ).
        IF ls_return IS NOT INITIAL.
          CALL FUNCTION 'MESSAGE_TEXT_BUILD'
            EXPORTING
              msgid               = ls_return-id
              msgnr               = ls_return-number
              msgv1               = ls_return-message_v1
              msgv2               = ls_return-message_v2
            IMPORTING
              message_text_output = ls_return-message.

          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
            EXPORTING
              textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
              message = ls_return-message.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      TRY.
          CALL METHOD me->get_activity_function
            EXPORTING
              ir_case   = lr_backendcase
              im_wiid   = lv_wid
            IMPORTING
              ex_pos_id = lv_posid.
          lr_srm  ?= lr_backendcase.
          lr_case ?= lr_srm.
          lr_case_poid  = lr_case->if_srm_sp_object~get_poid( ).
          lr_crmroot    = lr_srm->get_root( ).
          lr_factory    = lr_crmroot->get_sps_factory_by_poid( lr_case_poid ).
          lr_case_visualization ?= lr_factory->connect_object( im_class_role = 'IS_SP_VISUALIZATION_WIN_CLASS' im_poid = lr_case_poid ).
          lr_case_visualization->g_backend =  lr_backendcase.
          lr_case_visualization->init_frontend( ).

          CALL METHOD zcl_ega_cahdl=>get_case_handler
            EXPORTING
              ir_case   = lr_case_visualization->g_backend
              iv_hdlty  = zcl_ega_cacutil=>cv_hdlty_process_route
            RECEIVING
              er_result = lr_cahdl
            EXCEPTIONS
              error     = 1
              not_found = 2
              OTHERS    = 3.

          lr_capr ?= lr_cahdl.
          REFRESH: lt_messages[].

          SELECT SINGLE * FROM srmwfpthps
                          INTO @DATA(ls_srmwfpthps)
                          WHERE posid_wiid = @lv_wid.

          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.

          CALL METHOD lr_capr->case_activity_execute_function
            EXPORTING
              im_case              = lr_case_visualization
              iv_wf_path_posid     = ls_srmwfpthps-posid
              iv_activity_function = lv_execfunc  "im_activity_function
            CHANGING
              ct_messages          = lt_messages
            EXCEPTIONS
              error                = 1
              OTHERS               = 2.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          ENDIF.

*          CALL METHOD /c4pdpr/cl_cm_wf=>execute_function
*            EXPORTING
*              iv_actarea           = ls_srmwfpthps-actarea
*              iv_pathid            = ls_srmwfpthps-pathid
*              iv_posid             = ls_srmwfpthps-posid
*              ir_case              = lr_case_visualization
*              iv_activity_function = lv_execfunc
*            IMPORTING
*              et_return            = DATA(lt_return)
*            EXCEPTIONS
*              error                = 1
*              OTHERS               = 2.
*          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
**          MOVE-CORRESPONDING sy TO ls_syst.
*          ENDIF.

*          lv_exerr = text-001.
*          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
*            EXPORTING
*              textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
*              message = lv_exerr.
*      ENDTRY.
          READ TABLE lt_messages ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_msg>) WITH KEY type = 'E'.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*        TRY.
*            WAIT UP TO 3 SECONDS.
            DATA ls_srmwfpthfields TYPE srmwfpthfields.
            ls_srmwfpthfields-actarea = 'SCMG'.
            ls_srmwfpthfields-pathid  = ls_srmwfpthps-pathid.
            ls_srmwfpthfields-posid   = ls_srmwfpthps-posid.
            ls_srmwfpthfields-funcresult = lv_execfunc.
            MODIFY srmwfpthfields FROM ls_srmwfpthfields.

            CALL METHOD lr_case_visualization->g_backend->store
              EXPORTING
                im_do_validation = ' '
                im_update_task   = ' '
                im_new_version   = ' '.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              CALL FUNCTION 'SAP_WAPI_WORKITEM_COMPLETE'
                EXPORTING
                  workitem_id = lv_wid.

              CALL FUNCTION 'SAP_WAPI_WORKITEM_CONFIRM'
                EXPORTING
                  workitem_id = lv_wid.
*              WAIT UP TO 3 SECONDS.
            ELSE.
              lv_exerr = text-002.
              RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
                EXPORTING
                  textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
                  message = lv_exerr.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        CATCH cx_root.
          lv_exerr = text-001.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
            EXPORTING
              textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
              message = lv_exerr.
*        CATCH cx_srm_framework .
*        CATCH cx_scmg .
*        CATCH cx_srm_sp_client .


      ENDTRY.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->OVERVIEWSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_OVERVIEW
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD overviewset_get_entityset.
    DATA: lv_wiid   TYPE sww_wiid,
          lv_caseid TYPE scmg_ext_key,
          lv_applno TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no.

    IF it_key_tab[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      READ TABLE it_key_tab INTO DATA(ls_key) WITH KEY name = 'WiId'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        lv_wiid   = ls_key-value.
      ENDIF.
      READ TABLE it_key_tab INTO ls_key WITH KEY name = 'CaseId'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        lv_caseid   = ls_key-value.
        lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
      ENDIF.
      READ TABLE it_key_tab INTO ls_key WITH KEY name = 'ApplicationNumber'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        lv_applno   = ls_key-value.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF it_filter_select_options[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      READ TABLE it_filter_select_options INTO DATA(ls_filter) WITH KEY property = 'WiId'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        READ TABLE ls_filter-select_options INTO DATA(ls_select) INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          lv_wiid   = ls_select-low.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      READ TABLE it_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter WITH KEY property = 'CaseId'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        READ TABLE ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_select INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          lv_caseid   = ls_select-low.
          lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      READ TABLE it_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter WITH KEY property = 'ApplicationNumber'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        READ TABLE ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_select INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          lv_applno   = ls_select-low.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE case_type INTO @DATA(casetype) FROM scmg_t_case_attr WHERE ext_key EQ @lv_caseid.
    IF casetype EQ 'ZE03'.
      overviewset_fish_get_entityset(
        EXPORTING
          iv_entity_name               = iv_entity_name
          iv_entity_set_name           = iv_entity_set_name
          iv_source_name               = iv_source_name
          it_filter_select_options     = it_filter_select_options
          is_paging                    = is_paging
          it_key_tab                   = it_key_tab
          it_navigation_path           = it_navigation_path
          it_order                     = it_order
          iv_filter_string             = iv_filter_string
          iv_search_string             = iv_search_string
          io_tech_request_context      = io_tech_request_context
        IMPORTING
          et_entityset                 = et_entityset
          es_response_context          = es_response_context
      ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    IF NOT lv_applno IS INITIAL.
      zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
        EXPORTING
          iv_read_type      =  'C'   " EPDA Case Read Type
          ir_applic_id      =  COND #( WHEN lv_applno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_applno ) ) )
        RECEIVING
          et_data           = DATA(lt_appl_data)
      ).
      READ TABLE lt_appl_data INTO DATA(ls_appl_data) INDEX 1.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lv_caseid = ls_appl_data-case_id.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
*    DATA(lt_data) = zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_wi_overview( im_wi_id = lv_wiid ).
    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_wi_overview(
      EXPORTING
        im_wi_id     = lv_wiid    " Work item ID
        im_case_id   = lv_caseid   " Case ID
      RECEIVING
        rt_data      = DATA(lt_data)    " EPDA - My Case Workitem Overview Tabletype
    ).
****
    SELECT SINGLE *  FROM zdt_epda_appln_r INTO @DATA(ls_epda_appln_r) WHERE case_id = @lv_caseid .
******
    SORT lt_data DESCENDING BY assign_date assign_time.
    et_entityset = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data
                  (   wi_id = wa-wi_id
                      case_id = |{ wa-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|
                      assign_date = |{ wa-assign_date+6(2) }| && |{ wa-assign_date+4(2) }| &&
                                     |{ wa-assign_date+0(4) }| && |{ wa-assign_time }|
                      completion_date = COND string( WHEN wa-completion_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                         THEN |{ wa-completion_date+6(2) }| && |{ wa-completion_date+4(2) }| &&
                                         |{ wa-completion_date+0(4) }| && |{ wa-completion_time }| ELSE '' )
                      application_number = wa-application_number
                      wi_rh_task = wa-wi_rh_task
                      task = wa-task
                      wi_aagent = wa-wi_aagent
                      assign_user_name = wa-assign_user_name
                      wi_stat = wa-wi_stat
                      comments = wa-comments
                      permit_id =  ls_epda_appln_r-permit_id
                      comp_name_en = ls_epda_appln_r-comp_name_en
                      comp_name_ar = ls_epda_appln_r-comp_name_ar
                      locationid = ls_appl_data-establish_location
                      locationdesc = ls_appl_data-establish_location_tx
                       ) ).

  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->PARTNERRELATIONS_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_PARTNERRELATION
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
METHOD partnerrelations_get_entityset.
  DATA: ls_return TYPE bapiret2,
        lt_return TYPE bapiret2_t.
  DATA: ls_bp_relationship TYPE zst_ega_bp_new_relationship.
  DATA: lt_relations TYPE TABLE OF bapibus1006_relations,
        ls_relations LIKE LINE OF lt_relations.
  DATA lv_bp TYPE bu_partner.
  DATA:ls_entity TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc_ext=>ts_partnerrelation.
*
*  TRY.
*      lv_bp = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PartnerId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
*    CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
*  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      DATA lv_permit TYPE recnnr.
      lv_permit = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PermitId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
  ENDTRY.

  CALL METHOD zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>permit_partner_list
    EXPORTING
      im_permit_id    = lv_permit
    IMPORTING
      et_partner_list = DATA(lt_partner_list).



*  CALL FUNCTION 'BUPA_RELATIONSHIPS_READ'
*    EXPORTING
*      iv_partner            = lv_bp
**     iv_reltyp             = is_bp_relationship-relation
*    TABLES
*      et_relationships      = lt_relations
*    EXCEPTIONS
*      no_partner_specified  = 1
*      no_valid_record_found = 2.

  IF lt_partner_list IS INITIAL.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.
  SELECT relkind,text30 FROM tb905t INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_relname) WHERE spras EQ @sy-langu.
  SELECT reltyp,rtitl FROM tbz9a INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_relname1) WHERE spras EQ @sy-langu.
  SELECT role, rltxt FROM tb003t INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_rolename) WHERE spras EQ @sy-langu.
  SELECT type, text FROM tb039b INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_idtext) WHERE spras EQ @sy-langu.

  LOOP AT lt_partner_list ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_partner_list>).
    CLEAR:ls_entity.
    ls_entity-permitid          = lv_permit.
    ls_entity-partnerid         = <ls_partner_list>-owner_id.
    TRY.
        ls_entity-relationshiptype = lt_relname[ relkind = <ls_partner_list>-relkind ]-text30.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
    IF ls_entity-relationshiptype IS INITIAL.
      TRY.
          ls_entity-relationshiptype = lt_relname1[ reltyp = <ls_partner_list>-bp_role ]-rtitl.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.
    ENDIF.
    IF ls_entity-relationshiptype IS INITIAL.
      TRY.
          ls_entity-relationshiptype = lt_rolename[ role = <ls_partner_list>-bp_role ]-rltxt.
          IF <ls_partner_list>-bp_role = 'MKK'.
            CONTINUE.
          ENDIF.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.
    ENDIF.


*    ls_entity-relationshiptype  = <ls_partner_list>-rltxt.
    ls_entity-partnername       = <ls_partner_list>-bp_name.
*    ls_entity-idtype = <ls_partner_list>-.
    TRY.
        ls_entity-idtype = lt_idtext[ type = 'YFS002' ]-text.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
    ls_entity-idnumber          = <ls_partner_list>-emirates_id.

    ls_entity-validfrom         = <ls_partner_list>-validfrom.
    ls_entity-validto           = <ls_partner_list>-validto.
    APPEND ls_entity TO et_entityset.

  ENDLOOP.
ENDMETHOD.
