* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->PARTNERSET_UPDATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_U(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TS_PARTNER
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD partnerset_update_entity.
    DATA: ls_data  TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_partner,
          wa_new   TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_partner,
          it_data  TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_partner,
          lt_final TYPE zst_ct_ega_epda_appln_bp.


    io_data_provider->read_entry_data( IMPORTING es_data   = ls_data ).
    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
      EXPORTING
        iv_read_type      = 'C'    " EPDA Case Read Type
        iv_get_sel_node = VALUE zst_epda_bopf_node_read_flg(
                                                   owners = abap_true
                                                    )

        ir_applic_id      = VALUE #( ( option = 'EQ' sign = 'I' low = ls_data-application_number ) )
      RECEIVING
        et_data           = DATA(lt_data)
    ).
    READ TABLE lt_data INTO DATA(it_dat) INDEX 1.
    READ TABLE it_dat-owners INTO DATA(ls_owners) INDEX 1.

    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>update_owner_details(
      EXPORTING
        iv_applnno         =  ls_data-application_number
        it_partner_details =  VALUE #( ( owner_id = ls_owners-owner_id email_address = ls_data-email_address mobile_number = ls_data-mobile_number ) )
      IMPORTING
        et_return          = DATA(lt_return)
    ).

  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->PERMITITEMSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_PERMITITEM
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD permititemset_get_entityset.
    DATA:rt_applno TYPE RANGE OF zde_ega_epda_application_no.
*    DATA lt_fish_data_n TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api=>ts_permit_history."Added by Uday
*    DATA ls_fish_data_n TYPE zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api=>ts_permit_history."Added by Uday
    DATA: lv_permitno TYPE recnnr,
          lv_caseid   TYPE  zde_ega_case_id,
          lv_applnos  TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no.

    IF iv_source_name EQ 'Overview'.
      DATA(lv_wiid) = it_key_tab[ name = 'WiId' ]-value.
      lv_caseid = it_key_tab[ name = 'CaseId' ]-value.
      DATA(lv_applno) = it_key_tab[ name = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-value.
    ELSE.
      lv_permitno = it_key_tab[ name = 'PermitNumber' ]-value.
      lv_applnos = it_key_tab[ name = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-value.
      lv_caseid = it_key_tab[ name = 'CaseId' ]-value.
    ENDIF.
**Begin of changes by Uday.y
    CALL METHOD zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_user_section
      EXPORTING
        iv_user       = sy-uname
      RECEIVING
        rv_section_id = DATA(lv_sectionid).
**End of changes by Uday.y
    lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
**Begin of changes by Uday.y
    CASE lv_sectionid.
      WHEN '00' OR '03'."All OR "EPDA&FISHERIES
        zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_permit_history(
         EXPORTING
           im_permit_id      = lv_permitno    " Real Estate Contract Number
           im_case_id        = lv_caseid    " RAK EGA: Case ID
           im_application_no =  lv_applnos   " RAK EGA EPDA Application Number
         IMPORTING
           et_permit_history = DATA(lt_data)
        ).

        NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_permit_history(
          EXPORTING
            im_permit_id      = lv_permitno    " Real Estate Contract Number
            im_case_id        = lv_caseid    " RAK EGA: Case ID
            im_application_no =  lv_applnos   " RAK EGA EPDA Application Number
          IMPORTING
            et_permit_history = DATA(lt_fsh_data)
          ).

        DATA: ls_noc_node TYPE zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api=>ty_noc_bopf_node_read_flg.
        ls_noc_node-partner = abap_true.
        DATA(lt_noc_data) = NEW zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api( )->get_noc_cases(
        iv_get_sel_node = ls_noc_node
        iv_permit_history = abap_true
        ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN  VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
        ir_permit_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
        ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_applnos IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_applnos ) ) )
        ).
      WHEN '01'. "EPDA
        zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_permit_history(
         EXPORTING
           im_permit_id      = lv_permitno    " Real Estate Contract Number
           im_case_id        = lv_caseid    " RAK EGA: Case ID
           im_application_no = lv_applnos   " RAK EGA EPDA Application Number
         IMPORTING
           et_permit_history = lt_data
        ).
      WHEN '02'. "FISHERIES
        NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_permit_history(
         EXPORTING
           im_permit_id      = lv_permitno    " Real Estate Contract Number
           im_case_id        = lv_caseid    " RAK EGA: Case ID
           im_application_no = lv_applnos   " RAK EGA EPDA Application Number
         IMPORTING
           et_permit_history = lt_fsh_data
        ).
      WHEN OTHERS.
        RETURN.
    ENDCASE.
**End of changes by Uday.y
*    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_permit_history(
*      EXPORTING
*        im_permit_id      = lv_permitno    " Real Estate Contract Number
*        im_case_id        = lv_caseid    " RAK EGA: Case ID
*        im_application_no =  lv_applnos   " RAK EGA EPDA Application Number
*      IMPORTING
*        et_permit_history = DATA(lt_data)
*    ).
*
*    NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_permit_history(
*      EXPORTING
*        im_permit_id      = lv_permitno    " Real Estate Contract Number
*        im_case_id        = lv_caseid    " RAK EGA: Case ID
*        im_application_no =  lv_applnos   " RAK EGA EPDA Application Number
*      IMPORTING
*        et_permit_history = DATA(lt_fsh_data)
*      ).
**Added by Uday
    IF NOT lt_fsh_data IS INITIAL.
*      LOOP AT lt_fsh_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_fish_data>) .
*        MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_fish_data> TO ls_fish_data_n.
*        IF <fs_fish_data>-issue_date IS INITIAL.
*          IF NOT <fs_fish_data>-permit_id IS INITIAL.
*            NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_lic_details(
*              EXPORTING
*                iv_lic_no       = <fs_fish_data>-permit_id    " Contract Number
*              IMPORTING
*                es_lic_details  =  DATA(ls_lic_details)   " Real Estate Contract
*            ).
*            IF NOT ls_lic_details-contract_start_date IS INITIAL.
*              ls_fish_data_n-issue_date = ls_lic_details-contract_start_date.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
*        ENDIF.
*        APPEND ls_fish_data_n TO lt_fish_data_n.
*      ENDLOOP.
*      REFRESH :lt_fsh_data.
*      lt_fsh_data = lt_fish_data_n.
**Added By Uday
      APPEND LINES OF lt_fsh_data TO lt_data.
    ENDIF.
    LOOP AT lt_noc_data INTO DATA(ls_noc_data).
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<data>).
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_noc_data TO <data>.
      <data>-permit_id = ls_noc_data-permit.
      <data>-issue_date = ls_noc_data-permit_issue_date.
      <data>-expiry_date = ls_noc_data-permit_expiry_date.
      READ TABLE ls_noc_data-partner INTO DATA(ls_company) WITH KEY bp_type = '2'.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        READ TABLE ls_noc_data-partner INTO ls_company WITH KEY bp_type = '1'.
      ENDIF.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        <data>-comp_bp_id   = ls_company-owner_id.
        <data>-comp_name_ar = ls_company-bp_name.
        <data>-comp_name_en = ls_company-bp_name_en.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF iv_source_name = 'Permit'.
      DELETE lt_data WHERE application_type = '05'.
*      SORT   lt_data BY created_on DESCENDING.
      SORT lt_data DESCENDING BY created_on created_time .
    ENDIF.
    DELETE lt_data WHERE case_id IS INITIAL.
    IF lt_data IS NOT INITIAL.

      et_entityset = VALUE #( FOR wa1 IN lt_data
                                  ( submissiondate = |{ wa1-created_on+6(2) }| && |{ wa1-created_on+4(2) }| &&
                                                  |{ wa1-created_on+0(4) }|
                                    caseid = |{ wa1-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|
                                    applicationnumber = wa1-application_number
                                    comp_name_ar = wa1-comp_name_ar
                                    comp_name_en = wa1-comp_name_en
                                    permitnumber = wa1-permit_id
                                    applicationtype = wa1-application_type
                                    status = wa1-status
                                    issuedate = COND string( WHEN wa1-issue_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                                  THEN |{ wa1-issue_date+6(2) }| &&
                                                       |{ wa1-issue_date+4(2) }| &&
                                                       |{ wa1-issue_date+0(4) }| ELSE '' )
                                    expirydate = COND string( WHEN wa1-expiry_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                                  THEN |{ wa1-expiry_date+6(2) }| &&
                                                  |{ wa1-expiry_date+4(2) }| &&
                                                  |{ wa1-expiry_date+0(4) }| ELSE '' )
                                    companybpid = wa1-comp_bp_id
                                    applicationtypetx = wa1-application_type_tx
                                    currentpermitstatustx = wa1-status_tx ) ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD permitset_get_entityset.
    DATA: lv_comp_en  TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
          lv_comp_ar  TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
          rt_case_id  TYPE RANGE OF scmg_ext_key,
          lv_caseid   TYPE scmg_ext_key,

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_freetext) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'FreeSearch' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_appl_no) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        lv_comp_en = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameEn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        lv_comp_ar = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameAr' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_permitno) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PermitNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        lv_caseid = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CaseId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    IF lt_data IS NOT INITIAL.
      DELETE lt_data WHERE application_type EQ '05'.

                                    expirydate = COND string( WHEN wa2-expire_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                                THEN |{ wa2-expire_date+6(2) }| &&
                                                     |{ wa2-expire_date+4(2) }| &&
                                                     |{ wa2-expire_date+0(4) }| ELSE '' )
                                    companybpid = wa2-owner_id
                                    currentpermitstatus = wa2-status
                                    appliicationtypetx = wa2-lic_type_tx
                                    currentpermitstatustx = wa2-status_tx
                                    businesstypetx = wa2-fishing_type_tx
                                     ) )  .
    ENDIF.


    IF lt_noc_data[] IS NOT INITIAL.

      et_entityset =  VALUE #( FOR wa3 IN lt_noc_data
*                                 FOR wa2 IN lt_date WHERE ( case_id = wa1-case_id )
                                  ( permitnumber = wa3-permit
                                    applicationnumber = wa3-application_number
                                    caseid = |{ wa3-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|

                                    issuedate = COND string( WHEN wa3-permit_issue_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                                THEN |{ wa3-permit_issue_date+6(2) }| &&
                                                     |{ wa3-permit_issue_date+4(2) }| &&
                                                     |{ wa3-permit_issue_date+0(4) }| ELSE '' )
                                    expirydate = COND string( WHEN wa3-permit_expiry_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                                THEN |{ wa3-permit_expiry_date+6(2) }| &&
                                                     |{ wa3-permit_expiry_date+4(2) }| &&
                                                     |{ wa3-permit_expiry_date+0(4) }| ELSE '' )
*                                    companybpid = wa3-comp_bp_id

                                    currentpermitstatustx = wa3-status_tx
*                                    businesstypetx = wa1-businees_type_description
                                    ) )  .
      LOOP AT et_entityset ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<entity>).
        READ TABLE lt_noc_data INTO DATA(ls_noc_data) INDEX sy-tabix.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          READ TABLE ls_noc_data-partner INTO DATA(ls_partner) WITH KEY bp_type = '2'.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            READ TABLE ls_noc_data-partner INTO ls_partner WITH KEY bp_type = '1'.
          ENDIF.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            <entity>-companybpid  = ls_partner-owner_id.
            <entity>-comp_name_ar = ls_partner-bp_name.
            <entity>-comp_name_en = ls_partner-bp_name_en.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      DELETE et_entityset WHERE permitnumber IS INITIAL.
    ENDIF.


  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Private Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->POST_NOTES
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_TDID                        TYPE        BAPITDID
* | [--->] IV_NOTES                       TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_CASE_GUID                   TYPE        SCMG_CASE_GUID
* | [<---] ES_RETURN                      TYPE        BAPIRET2
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD post_notes.
    DATA: lt_note_ids TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapinoteid,
          ls_note_ids TYPE bapinoteid,
          lt_tline    TYPE TABLE OF bapitdline,
          lt_notes    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bapinote,
          ls_notes    TYPE bapinote.
    ls_note_ids-elem_no = '1'.
    ls_note_ids-tdid = iv_tdid.
    APPEND ls_note_ids TO lt_note_ids.
    ls_notes-elem_no = '1'.
    CALL FUNCTION 'SOTR_SERV_STRING_TO_TABLE'
      EXPORTING
        text        = iv_notes
        line_length = '72'
      TABLES
        text_tab    = lt_tline.
    LOOP AT lt_tline ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_tline>) .
      ls_notes-tdline = <fs_tline>.
      APPEND ls_notes TO lt_notes.
    ENDLOOP.
* Add notes
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_CASE_ADDNOTES'
      EXPORTING
        guid          = iv_case_guid
      IMPORTING
        return        = es_return
      TABLES
        case_note_ids = lt_note_ids
        case_notes    = lt_notes.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Private Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->REMOVE
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ID                          TYPE        ANY
* | [<-()] RV_ID                          TYPE        CHAR20
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD remove.

    rv_id = iv_id.
    SHIFT rv_id LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->SUBACTIVITYSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_SUBACTIVITY
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD subactivityset_get_entityset.
*    DATA: lv_application_number TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no,
*          ls_application        TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_application,
*          ls_entity             TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_subactivity.
*
*    lv_application_number = it_key_tab[ name = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-value.
*
*    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
*            EXPORTING
*              iv_read_type      = 'C'
*              ir_applic_id      = VALUE #( ( option = 'EQ' sign = 'I' low = lv_application_number ) )
*              iv_get_sel_node   = VALUE zst_epda_bopf_node_read_flg( sub_act = abap_true )
*            RECEIVING
*              et_data           = DATA(lt_data) ).
*
*    READ TABLE lt_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_data>) INDEX 1.
*    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*      RETURN.
*    ENDIF.
*    MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data> TO ls_application.
*    DATA(lt_subact) = <fs_data>-sub_activities.
*
*    SELECT * FROM zdt_epda_actsuat INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_sa) WHERE main_activity_key EQ @ls_application-main_activity.
*    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*      RETURN.
*    ENDIF.
*
*    SELECT * FROM zdt_epda_subactv INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_st) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_sa WHERE subactivity_id EQ @lt_sa-subactivity_id.
*    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*      RETURN.
*    ENDIF.
*
*    LOOP AT lt_sa ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_sa>).
*      READ TABLE lt_subact INTO DATA(ls_subact) WITH KEY subactivity_id = <fs_sa>-subactivity_id.
*      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*        ls_entity-flag = abap_true.
*      ENDIF.
*      READ TABLE lt_st INTO DATA(ls_st) WITH KEY subactivity_id = <fs_sa>-subactivity_id.
*      ls_entity-activity = <fs_sa>-subactivity_id.
*      ls_entity-activitytext = COND #( WHEN sy-langu EQ 'EN' THEN ls_st-subactivity_description_en ELSE ls_st-subactivity_description_ar ).
*      APPEND ls_entity TO et_entityset.
*      CLEAR ls_entity.
*    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->TASKSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_TASK
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD taskset_get_entityset.
    TYPES :BEGIN OF ty_attr_wf,
             case_id TYPE zst_ega_case_attr_wf-ext_key,
             agent   TYPE zst_ega_case_attr_wf-agent,
             count   TYPE i,
           END OF ty_attr_wf.
    DATA: rt_appln_no       TYPE RANGE OF zde_ega_epda_application_no,
          rt_case_id        TYPE RANGE OF scmg_ext_key,
          rt_case_id_fshry  TYPE RANGE OF scmg_ext_key,
          lt_case_attr_wf   TYPE TABLE OF zst_ega_case_attr_wf,
          lt_case_attr_wf_t TYPE TABLE OF zst_ega_case_attr_wf,
          lt_case_attr_n    TYPE TABLE OF ty_attr_wf,
          ls_case_attr_n    TYPE ty_attr_wf,
          lt_case_attr_f    TYPE TABLE OF ty_attr_wf,
          dt_dts            TYPE tzntstmpsl,
          lr_status         TYPE RANGE OF scmgstatusonr. "timestamp.

    DATA ls_entityset     TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_task.
    DATA lv_fcount        TYPE char5.
    DATA lv_rcount        TYPE char5.
    DATA lv_fullname      TYPE string.
    DATA ls_read_flg      TYPE zst_fshry_bopf_node_read_flg.
    DATA ls_noc_read_flg TYPE zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api=>ty_noc_bopf_node_read_flg.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_pending) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'Pending' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_search) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'FreeSearch' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_completed) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'Completed' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
**Begin of changes by Uday.y
    CALL METHOD zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_user_section
      EXPORTING
        iv_user       = sy-uname
      RECEIVING
        rv_section_id = DATA(lv_sectionid).
    " 00 - ALL
    " 01 - EPDA
    " 02 - Fisheries
    " 03 - EPDA & Fisheries
    IF lv_pending EQ abap_false AND lv_completed EQ abap_false.
      DATA(lt_data) = zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_inbox_workitems( im_login_user = sy-uname ).
      DELETE lt_data WHERE application_number = ''.
      rt_appln_no = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-application_number ) ).
    ELSE.
      lr_status = VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 75  ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 80  ) ).
    ENDIF.



    IF rt_appln_no[] IS INITIAL AND lv_search IS INITIAL.
      DATA(lv_get_changed_on) = abap_true.
      DATA(lv_begda) = sy-datum.
      DATA(lv_endda) = cl_reca_date=>sub_months_from_date( id_months = 1 id_date = lv_begda ).
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_sectionid EQ '00' OR lv_sectionid EQ '01' OR lv_sectionid EQ '03'.
      DATA(lt_case) = NEW zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api( )->get_epda_cases(
          iv_read_type      = 'C'
          ir_applic_id      = rt_appln_no
          iv_freetext       = lv_search
          iv_from_date      = lv_begda
          iv_to_date        = lv_endda
          iv_get_changed_on = lv_get_changed_on ).

      IF lv_pending EQ abap_true AND lv_completed EQ abap_false.
        DELETE lt_case WHERE status IN lr_status.
      ELSEIF lv_pending EQ abap_true AND lv_completed EQ abap_true.
        DELETE lt_case WHERE status NOT IN lr_status.
      ENDIF.

      rt_case_id = VALUE #( FOR ls IN lt_case ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = ls-case_id ) ).
    ENDIF.
    IF lv_sectionid EQ '00' OR lv_sectionid EQ '02' OR lv_sectionid EQ '03'.
      CLEAR ls_read_flg.
      ls_read_flg-partner = abap_true.

      DATA(lt_fshry_case) = NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_fshry_cases(
          iv_get_sel_node   = ls_read_flg
          ir_applic_id      = rt_appln_no
          iv_freetext       = lv_search
          iv_from_date      = lv_begda
          iv_to_date        = lv_endda
          iv_get_changed_on = lv_get_changed_on ).

      IF lv_pending EQ abap_true AND lv_completed EQ abap_false.
        DELETE lt_fshry_case WHERE status IN lr_status.
      ELSEIF lv_pending EQ abap_true AND lv_completed EQ abap_true.
        DELETE lt_fshry_case WHERE status NOT IN lr_status.
      ENDIF.

      rt_case_id_fshry = VALUE #( FOR ls1 IN lt_fshry_case ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = ls1-case_id ) ).
      APPEND LINES OF rt_case_id_fshry[] TO rt_case_id[].
      CLEAR: rt_case_id_fshry[].
    ENDIF.
    IF lv_sectionid EQ '00' OR lv_sectionid EQ '01' OR lv_sectionid EQ '03'.
      CLEAR ls_noc_read_flg.
      ls_noc_read_flg-company = abap_true.
      ls_noc_read_flg-partner = abap_true.

      DATA(lt_noc_case) = NEW zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api( )->get_noc_cases(
          iv_get_sel_node   = ls_noc_read_flg
          ir_applic_id      = rt_appln_no
          iv_freetext       = lv_search
          iv_from_date      = lv_begda
          iv_to_date        = lv_endda
          iv_get_changed_on = lv_get_changed_on ).

      IF lv_pending EQ abap_true AND lv_completed EQ abap_false.
        DELETE lt_noc_case WHERE status IN lr_status.
      ELSEIF lv_pending EQ abap_true AND lv_completed EQ abap_true.
        DELETE lt_noc_case WHERE status NOT IN lr_status.
      ENDIF.

      rt_case_id_fshry = VALUE #( FOR ls2 IN lt_noc_case ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = ls2-case_id ) ).
      APPEND LINES OF rt_case_id_fshry[] TO rt_case_id[].
      CLEAR: rt_case_id_fshry[].
    ENDIF.

    IF rt_case_id[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT * FROM zdt_epda_insp_r INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_insp_root)
        WHERE case_id IN @rt_case_id
        ORDER BY case_id.

      DATA: lt_domvalues TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF dd07v.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DDIF_DOMA_GET'
        EXPORTING
          name          = 'ZDO_EPDA_INSPECTION_STATUS'
          langu         = sy-langu
        TABLES
          dd07v_tab     = lt_domvalues
        EXCEPTIONS
          illegal_input = 1
          OTHERS        = 2.
**Fetching for assigned User

      SELECT scmg_t_case_attr~case_guid, scmg_t_case_attr~stat_orderno, scmg_t_case_attr~ext_key,
        srmwfpthps~agent, swwuserwi~user_id, swwuserwi~no_sel, swwuserwi~forward
        FROM scmg_t_case_attr
        INNER JOIN scmg_t_case
        ON  scmg_t_case~case_guid EQ scmg_t_case_attr~case_guid
        INNER JOIN srmwfpthps
        ON  srmwfpthps~pathid EQ scmg_t_case~wfpthid
        INNER JOIN swwuserwi
        ON  swwuserwi~wi_id EQ srmwfpthps~posid_wiid
        INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_cases)
        WHERE scmg_t_case_attr~ext_key IN @rt_case_id
        ORDER BY scmg_t_case_attr~ext_key, swwuserwi~forward, swwuserwi~no_sel.

      LOOP AT lt_cases INTO DATA(ls_case_attr).
        IF ls_case_attr-no_sel NE abap_true.
          ls_case_attr_n-case_id = ls_case_attr-ext_key.
          ls_case_attr_n-agent   = ls_case_attr-agent+2(12).
          ls_case_attr_n-count   = 1.
          COLLECT ls_case_attr_n INTO lt_case_attr_f.
          CLEAR: ls_case_attr_n.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      SELECT positionid, positioniddescription FROM zdt_epda_rolemat
      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_rolemat)
        ORDER BY positionid.

    ENDIF.
    SORT lt_case BY application_number.
    SORT lt_fshry_case BY application_number.
    SORT lt_noc_case BY application_number.
    IF lv_pending EQ abap_true OR lv_completed EQ abap_true.
      LOOP AT lt_case INTO DATA(ls_case).
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<data>).
        <data>-application_number = ls_case-application_number.
      ENDLOOP.
      LOOP AT lt_fshry_case INTO DATA(ls_fshry_case).
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_data ASSIGNING <data>.
        <data>-application_number = ls_fshry_case-application_number.
      ENDLOOP.
      LOOP AT lt_noc_case INTO DATA(ls_noc_case).
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_data ASSIGNING <data>.
        <data>-application_number = ls_noc_case-application_number.
      ENDLOOP.
      SORT lt_data BY application_number.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_data COMPARING application_number.
    ENDIF.


    LOOP AT lt_data INTO DATA(ls_data).
      READ TABLE lt_case INTO ls_case WITH KEY application_number = ls_data-application_number.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND ls_case-renewal_type EQ 2.
        lv_fcount = lv_fcount + 1.
      ELSE.
        lv_rcount = lv_rcount + 1.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    SORT lt_data DESCENDING BY assign_date assign_time.
    LOOP AT lt_data INTO ls_data.
      READ TABLE lt_case INTO ls_case WITH KEY application_number = ls_data-application_number BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        CLEAR ls_entityset.
        ls_entityset-wi_id               = ls_data-wi_id.
        ls_entityset-case_id             = ls_case-case_id.
        ls_entityset-assign_date         = ls_data-assign_date && ls_data-assign_time.
        ls_entityset-submission_date     = ls_case-created_on && ls_case-created_time.
        ls_entityset-application_number  = ls_case-application_number.
        ls_entityset-busiinesstype       = ls_case-business_type.
        ls_entityset-busineestypedesc    = ls_case-businees_type_description.
        ls_entityset-companybpid         = ls_case-comp_bp_id.
        ls_entityset-comp_name           = ls_case-comp_name_en.
        ls_entityset-application_type    = ls_data-application_type.
        ls_entityset-application_type_tx = ls_data-application_type_tx.
        ls_entityset-casetype            = ls_case-case_type.
        ls_entityset-casetypedesc        = ls_case-case_type_desc.
        ls_entityset-renewaltype         = ls_case-renewal_type.
        ls_entityset-fastcount           = lv_fcount.
        ls_entityset-regcount            = lv_rcount.
        ls_entityset-permitnumber        = ls_case-permit_id.
        ls_entityset-statuscode          = ls_case-status.
        ls_entityset-currentstatus       = ls_case-status_tx.

        ls_entityset-sectionid           = '1'.
        APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
      ENDIF.

* Fisheries Cases
      READ TABLE lt_fshry_case INTO ls_fshry_case WITH KEY application_number = ls_data-application_number BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        CLEAR ls_entityset.
        ls_entityset-wi_id                = ls_data-wi_id.
        ls_entityset-case_id              = ls_fshry_case-case_id.
        ls_entityset-assign_date          = ls_data-assign_date && ls_data-assign_time.
        ls_entityset-submission_date      = ls_fshry_case-created_on && ls_fshry_case-created_time.
        ls_entityset-application_number   = ls_fshry_case-application_number.
        ls_entityset-companybpid          = ls_fshry_case-owner_id.
        ls_entityset-comp_name            = ls_fshry_case-owner_name.
        ls_entityset-application_type     = ls_fshry_case-lic_type.
        ls_entityset-application_type_tx  = ls_fshry_case-lic_type_tx.
        ls_entityset-casetype             = ls_fshry_case-case_type.
        ls_entityset-casetypedesc         = ls_fshry_case-case_type_tx.
        IF ls_fshry_case-case_type EQ 'ZE13'.
          ls_entityset-renewaltype        = ' '.
        ELSE.
          ls_entityset-renewaltype        = '1'.
        ENDIF.
        ls_entityset-fastcount            = lv_fcount.
        ls_entityset-regcount             = lv_rcount.
        ls_entityset-permitnumber         = ls_fshry_case-lic_no.
        ls_entityset-statuscode           = ls_fshry_case-status.
        ls_entityset-currentstatus        = ls_fshry_case-status_tx.

        ls_entityset-sectionid            = '2'.
        APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
      ENDIF.


* NOC
      READ TABLE lt_noc_case INTO ls_noc_case WITH KEY application_number = ls_data-application_number BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.

        CLEAR ls_entityset.
        ls_entityset-wi_id                = ls_data-wi_id.
        ls_entityset-case_id              = ls_noc_case-case_id.
        ls_entityset-assign_date          = ls_data-assign_date && ls_data-assign_time.
        ls_entityset-submission_date      = ls_noc_case-created_on && ls_noc_case-created_time.
        ls_entityset-application_number   = ls_noc_case-application_number.
        ls_entityset-companybpid          = ls_noc_case-comp_bp_id.
        READ TABLE ls_noc_case-company INTO DATA(ls_noc_company) INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          IF sy-langu = 'A'.
            ls_entityset-comp_name        = ls_noc_company-company_name_ar.
          ELSE.
            ls_entityset-comp_name        = ls_noc_company-company_name_en.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        ls_entityset-application_type     = ls_noc_case-application_type.
        ls_entityset-application_type_tx  = ls_noc_case-application_type_tx.
        ls_entityset-casetype             = ls_noc_case-case_type.
        ls_entityset-casetypedesc         = ls_noc_case-case_type_desc.
        ls_entityset-fastcount            = lv_fcount.
        ls_entityset-regcount             = lv_rcount.
        ls_entityset-permitnumber         = ls_noc_case-permit.
        ls_entityset-renewaltype          = '1'.
        ls_entityset-statuscode           = ls_noc_case-status.
        ls_entityset-currentstatus        = ls_noc_case-status_tx.

        ls_entityset-sectionid            = '3'.
        APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
      ENDIF.


    ENDLOOP.

    LOOP AT et_entityset ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<entityset>).

      READ TABLE lt_insp_root ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_insp_root>) WITH KEY case_id = <entityset>-case_id BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <fs_insp_root>-status IS NOT INITIAL.
        READ TABLE lt_domvalues ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_domval>) WITH KEY domvalue_l = <fs_insp_root>-status.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          <entityset>-inspectionstatus      = <fs_domval>-ddtext.
          <entityset>-inspectionstatuscode  = <fs_insp_root>-status.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      DATA: lt_cases_temp LIKE lt_cases[].
      CLEAR: lt_cases_temp[].
      READ TABLE lt_cases INTO ls_case_attr WITH KEY ext_key = <entityset>-case_id BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        DATA(lv_tabix) = sy-tabix.
        DO.
          APPEND ls_case_attr TO lt_cases_temp.
          ADD 1 TO lv_tabix.
          READ TABLE lt_cases INTO ls_case_attr INDEX lv_tabix.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0 AND ls_case_attr-ext_key EQ <entityset>-case_id.
          ELSE.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
        ENDDO.

        READ TABLE lt_case_attr_f INTO DATA(ls_case_attr_f) WITH KEY case_id = <entityset>-case_id BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          IF ls_case_attr_f-count = 1.
            READ TABLE lt_cases_temp INTO ls_case_attr WITH KEY no_sel = abap_false.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              <entityset>-assigneduser = ls_case_attr-user_id.
            ENDIF.
          ELSEIF ls_case_attr_f-count GT 1.
            READ TABLE lt_cases_temp INTO ls_case_attr WITH KEY forward = 'X'.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              <entityset>-assigneduser = ls_case_attr-user_id.
            ELSE.
              READ TABLE lt_rolemat INTO DATA(ls_rolemat) WITH KEY positionid = ls_case_attr_f-agent BINARY SEARCH.
              IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
                <entityset>-assigneduser = ls_rolemat-positioniddescription.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF <entityset>-assigneduser IS INITIAL.
          IF <entityset>-statuscode = '20' OR <entityset>-statuscode = '21' OR
             <entityset>-statuscode = '26' OR <entityset>-statuscode = '55'.
            <entityset>-assigneduser = 'Pending with Customer'.
            <entityset>-statuscode   = ls_case_attr-stat_orderno.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.



      <entityset>-case_id = |{ <entityset>-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|.
    ENDLOOP.

***End of changes by Uday.y
*    IF lv_pending NE abap_true AND lv_completed NE abap_true.

*            IF ls_case_attr_f-count = 1.
*              ls_entityset-assigneduser = ls_case_attr_wf-user_id.
*            ELSEIF ls_case_attr_f-count GT 1.
*              READ TABLE lt_case_attr_wf_t INTO DATA(ls_case_attr) WITH KEY ext_key = ls_epdata-case_id
*                                                                            forward = 'X'.
*              IF sy-subrc = 0.
*                ls_entityset-assigneduser = ls_case_attr-user_id.
*              ELSE.
*                READ TABLE lt_rolemat INTO DATA(ls_rolemat) WITH KEY positionid = ls_case_attr_f-agent.
*                IF sy-subrc = 0.
*                  ls_entityset-assigneduser = ls_rolemat-positioniddescription.
*                ENDIF.
*              ENDIF.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
**        ENDIF.
*          ls_entityset-statuscode   = ls_case_attr_wf-stat_orderno.
*        ENDIF.
*        IF ls_entityset-assigneduser IS INITIAL.
*          IF ls_epdata-status = '20' OR ls_epdata-status = '21' OR
*             ls_epdata-status = '26' OR ls_epdata-status = '55'.
*            ls_entityset-assigneduser = 'Pending with Customer'.
*            READ TABLE lt_guid INTO DATA(ls_guid) WITH KEY ext_key = ls_epdata-case_id.
*            IF sy-subrc = 0.
*              ls_entityset-statuscode   = ls_guid-stat_orderno.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
*        ENDIF.
*        ls_entityset-sectionid = '1'.
*        APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
*        CLEAR:ls_case_attr_wf,ls_entityset,ls_guid.
*      ENDLOOP.
*
** Fishery Data
*      LOOP AT lt_fshrydata INTO DATA(ls_fshrydata).
*
*        ls_entityset-case_id = |{ ls_fshrydata-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|.
*        IF NOT ls_fshrydata-created_time IS INITIAL.
*          CONCATENATE ls_fshrydata-created_on ls_fshrydata-created_time INTO dt_dts.
*          ls_entityset-submission_date = dt_dts.
*        ELSE.
*          CONCATENATE ls_fshrydata-created_on '000000' INTO dt_dts.
*          ls_entityset-submission_date = dt_dts.
*        ENDIF.
**        ls_entityset-submission_date = ls_epdata-created_on.
*        ls_entityset-application_number = ls_fshrydata-application_number.
**        ls_entityset-busiinesstype = ls_epdata-business_type.
**        ls_entityset-busineestypedesc = ls_epdata-businees_type_description.
*        ls_entityset-permitnumber = ls_fshrydata-lic_no.
*        ls_entityset-application_type = ls_fshrydata-lic_type.
*        ls_entityset-application_type_tx = ls_fshrydata-lic_type_tx.
*        ls_entityset-casetype = ls_fshrydata-case_type.
*        ls_entityset-casetypedesc = ls_fshrydata-case_type_tx.
*        ls_entityset-currentstatus = ls_fshrydata-status_tx.
*        ls_entityset-companybpid = ls_fshrydata-owner_id.
*        ls_entityset-comp_name   = ls_fshrydata-owner_name.
*
*        READ TABLE lt_case_attr_wf INTO ls_case_attr_wf WITH KEY ext_key = ls_fshrydata-case_id.
*        IF sy-subrc = 0.
*          READ TABLE lt_case_attr_f INTO ls_case_attr_f WITH KEY case_id = ls_fshrydata-case_id.
*          IF sy-subrc = 0.
*            IF ls_case_attr_f-count = 1.
*              ls_entityset-assigneduser = ls_case_attr_wf-user_id.
*            ELSEIF ls_case_attr_f-count GT 1.
*              READ TABLE lt_case_attr_wf_t INTO ls_case_attr WITH KEY ext_key = ls_fshrydata-case_id
*                                                                            forward = 'X'.
*              IF sy-subrc = 0.
*                ls_entityset-assigneduser = ls_case_attr-user_id.
*              ELSE.
*                READ TABLE lt_rolemat INTO ls_rolemat WITH KEY positionid = ls_case_attr_f-agent.
*                IF sy-subrc = 0.
*                  ls_entityset-assigneduser = ls_rolemat-positioniddescription.
*                ENDIF.
*              ENDIF.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
**        ENDIF.
*          ls_entityset-statuscode   = ls_case_attr_wf-stat_orderno.
*        ENDIF.
*        IF ls_entityset-assigneduser IS INITIAL.
*          IF ls_fshrydata-status = '20' OR ls_fshrydata-status = '21' OR
*             ls_fshrydata-status = '26' OR ls_fshrydata-status = '55'.
*            ls_entityset-assigneduser = 'Pending with Customer'.
*            READ TABLE lt_guid INTO ls_guid WITH KEY ext_key = ls_fshrydata-case_id.
*            IF sy-subrc = 0.
*              ls_entityset-statuscode   = ls_guid-stat_orderno.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
*        ENDIF.
*        ls_entityset-sectionid = '2'.
*        APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
*        CLEAR:ls_case_attr_wf,ls_entityset,ls_guid.
*      ENDLOOP.
*
*      LOOP AT lt_noc_data INTO DATA(ls_noc_data).
*        ls_entityset-case_id = |{ ls_noc_data-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|.
*        IF NOT ls_noc_data-created_time IS INITIAL.
*          CONCATENATE ls_noc_data-created_on ls_noc_data-created_time INTO dt_dts.
*          ls_entityset-submission_date = dt_dts.
*        ELSE.
*          CONCATENATE ls_noc_data-created_on '000000' INTO dt_dts.
*          ls_entityset-submission_date = dt_dts.
*        ENDIF.
**        ls_entityset-submission_date = ls_noc_data-created_on.
*        ls_entityset-application_number = ls_noc_data-application_number.
**        ls_entityset-busiinesstype = ls_epdata-business_type.
**        ls_entityset-busineestypedesc = ls_epdata-businees_type_description.
*        ls_entityset-permitnumber = ls_noc_data-permit.
*        ls_entityset-application_type = ls_noc_data-application_type.
*        ls_entityset-application_type_tx = ls_noc_data-application_type_tx.
*        ls_entityset-casetype = ls_noc_data-case_type.
*        ls_entityset-casetypedesc = ls_noc_data-case_type_desc.
**        ls_entityset-currentstatus = ls_noc_data-status_tx.
**        ls_entityset-companybpid = ls_noc_data-owner_id.
**        ls_entityset-comp_name   = ls_noc_data-owner_name.
*
*        READ TABLE lt_case_attr_wf INTO ls_case_attr_wf WITH KEY ext_key = ls_noc_data-case_id.
*        IF sy-subrc = 0.
*          READ TABLE lt_case_attr_f INTO ls_case_attr_f WITH KEY case_id = ls_noc_data-case_id.
*          IF sy-subrc = 0.
*            IF ls_case_attr_f-count = 1.
*              ls_entityset-assigneduser = ls_case_attr_wf-user_id.
*            ELSEIF ls_case_attr_f-count GT 1.
*              READ TABLE lt_case_attr_wf_t INTO ls_case_attr WITH KEY ext_key = ls_noc_data-case_id
*                                                                            forward = 'X'.
*              IF sy-subrc = 0.
*                ls_entityset-assigneduser = ls_case_attr-user_id.
*              ELSE.
*                READ TABLE lt_rolemat INTO ls_rolemat WITH KEY positionid = ls_case_attr_f-agent.
*                IF sy-subrc = 0.
*                  ls_entityset-assigneduser = ls_rolemat-positioniddescription.
*                ENDIF.
*              ENDIF.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
**        ENDIF.
*          ls_entityset-statuscode   = ls_case_attr_wf-stat_orderno.
*        ENDIF.
*        IF ls_entityset-assigneduser IS INITIAL.
*          IF ls_noc_data-status = '20' OR ls_noc_data-status = '21' OR
*             ls_noc_data-status = '26' OR ls_noc_data-status = '55'.
*            ls_entityset-assigneduser = 'Pending with Customer'.
*            READ TABLE lt_guid INTO ls_guid WITH KEY ext_key = ls_fshrydata-case_id.
*            IF sy-subrc = 0.
*              ls_entityset-statuscode   = ls_guid-stat_orderno.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
*        ENDIF.
*        ls_entityset-sectionid = '3'.
*        APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
*        CLEAR:ls_case_attr_wf,ls_entityset,ls_guid.
*      ENDLOOP.
*
*    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->VALIDATE_ON_USER_ACTION
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_WIID                        TYPE        SWW_WIID
* | [--->] IV_CASE_ID                     TYPE        SCMG_EXT_KEY(optional)
* | [--->] IV_ACTIVITY                    TYPE        SRMACTIV(optional)
* | [--->] IV_EXECFUNC                    TYPE        SRMACTFC(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MD_SKIP                     TYPE        BOOLEAN_FLG(optional)
* | [<---] ET_RETURN                      TYPE        BAPIRET2_T
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD validate_on_user_action.

    DATA lv_case_id   TYPE scmg_ext_key.
    DATA lv_activity  TYPE srmactiv.
    DATA ls_return    TYPE bapiret2.
    DATA lv_exerr     TYPE bapi_msg.
    DATA lv_sel_node  TYPE zst_epda_bopf_node_read_flg.

    IF iv_wiid IS INITIAL.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

* From Workitem get the path, activity and case id
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM srmwfpthps INTO @DATA(ls_srmwfpthps)
            WHERE posid_wiid = @iv_wiid.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      RETURN.
    ELSE.
      lv_activity = ls_srmwfpthps-activity.
    ENDIF.


* Get case ID
    SELECT SINGLE case_guid, wfpthid FROM scmg_t_case INTO @DATA(ls_t_case)
      WHERE wfpthid = @ls_srmwfpthps-pathid.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    IF iv_case_id IS NOT INITIAL.
      lv_case_id = iv_case_id.

    ELSEIF ls_t_case-case_guid IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE  ext_key FROM scmg_t_case_attr INTO lv_case_id
         WHERE case_guid = ls_t_case-case_guid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

* Get Full workflow
    SELECT * FROM srmwfpthps INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_srmwfpthps)
      WHERE pathid = @ls_srmwfpthps-pathid.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_activity IS INITIAL AND lv_case_id IS INITIAL.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    DATA lv_get_sel_node  TYPE zst_epda_bopf_node_read_flg.
    lv_get_sel_node-fee = abap_true.

    CALL METHOD zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases
      EXPORTING
        iv_read_type    = 'C'
        ir_case_id      = VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_case_id ) )
        iv_get_sel_node = lv_get_sel_node
      RECEIVING
        et_data         = DATA(lt_case_read).
    IF lt_case_read IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT * FROM zdt_epda_mainacc INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_epda_mainacc)
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_case_read
            WHERE main_activity_key = @lt_case_read-main_activity.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR ls_return.
    READ TABLE lt_case_read INTO DATA(ls_case_read) INDEX 1.
    CASE lv_activity.
      WHEN 'ZE01MOD'.
        IF ls_case_read-application_type = '01' AND iv_execfunc <> 'FKT04'.
          READ TABLE lt_epda_mainacc ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_epda_mainacc>)  WITH KEY main_activity_key = ls_case_read-main_activity.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <fs_epda_mainacc>-major_industry_indicator = abap_true.
            READ TABLE lt_srmwfpthps INTO ls_srmwfpthps WITH KEY activity = 'ZE01GMA'.
            IF sy-subrc <> 0.
              lv_exerr = text-002.
              ls_return-type = 'E'.
              ls_return-message = lv_exerr.
              APPEND ls_return TO et_return.
*              RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
*                EXPORTING
*                  textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
*                  message = lv_exerr.
              RETURN.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
***Begin of Changes by Y.REDDY dated on 14/02/2020
*        IF iv_execfunc = 'FKT12'.
*          DATA lv_esr_valid TYPE boolean.
*          SORT lt_srmwfpthps DESCENDING BY posid.
*          READ TABLE lt_srmwfpthps INTO ls_srmwfpthps WITH KEY activity = 'ZE01GMA'.
*          IF sy-subrc = 0.
*            lv_esr_valid = abap_true.
*            SELECT SINGLE funcresult FROM srmwfpthfields INTO @DATA(lv_funcresult_esr)
*              WHERE pathid = @ls_srmwfpthps-pathid
*                AND posid = @ls_srmwfpthps-posid.
*            IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*              CLEAR lv_funcresult_esr.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
***Industry
*          IF ls_case_read-business_type = '001'. "Industry
*            IF lv_esr_valid = abap_false.
*              lv_exerr = text-004.
*              ls_return-type = 'E'.
*              ls_return-message = lv_exerr.
*              APPEND ls_return TO et_return.
*              RETURN.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
*        ENDIF.
***End of changes by Y.REDDY dated on 14/02/2020
* on EMD Approve button
        IF iv_execfunc = 'FKT05'. " Approve
          DATA lv_gma_act TYPE boolean.
          SORT lt_srmwfpthps DESCENDING BY posid.
          READ TABLE lt_srmwfpthps INTO ls_srmwfpthps WITH KEY activity = 'ZE01GMA'.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            lv_gma_act = abap_true.
            SELECT SINGLE funcresult FROM srmwfpthfields INTO @DATA(lv_funcresult)
              WHERE pathid = @ls_srmwfpthps-pathid
                AND posid = @ls_srmwfpthps-posid.
            IF sy-subrc <> 0.
              CLEAR lv_funcresult.
            ENDIF.

* On GM Rejected
            IF lv_funcresult = 'FKT06'. " Rejected
              lv_exerr = text-003.
              ls_return-type = 'E'.
              ls_return-message = lv_exerr.
              APPEND ls_return TO et_return.
              RETURN.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

* On new, Renew(without fast track) and Amendment( Address and activity Change ) - Inspection is needed
          IF ls_case_read-application_type = '01'
            OR ( ls_case_read-application_type = '02' AND ls_case_read-renewal_type = '01' )
            OR ( ls_case_read-application_type = '03' AND ( ls_case_read-amend_add_actv = abap_true OR ls_case_read-amend_addr_chg = abap_true  ) ) .

            NEW zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api( )->get_inspection(
              EXPORTING
                ir_case_id      = VALUE #(  ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = ls_case_read-case_id ) )
                iv_last_visit         = abap_true
              IMPORTING
                et_inspection         = DATA(lt_inspection) ).
            IF lt_inspection[] IS INITIAL.
              lv_exerr = text-005.
              ls_return-type = 'E'.
              ls_return-message = lv_exerr.
              APPEND ls_return TO et_return.
              RETURN.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

* On new, Renew and Amendment( Address and activity Change ) - Need GM Appproval
          IF ls_case_read-business_type = '001'. "Industry
            IF ls_case_read-application_type = '01' OR ls_case_read-application_type = '02' OR
              ( ls_case_read-application_type = '03' AND
                    ( ls_case_read-amend_add_actv = abap_true OR ls_case_read-amend_addr_chg = abap_true  ) ) .

              IF lv_gma_act = abap_false.
                lv_exerr = text-004.
                ls_return-type = 'E'.
                ls_return-message = lv_exerr.
                APPEND ls_return TO et_return.
                RETURN.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

          IF ls_case_read-application_type = '02' OR ls_case_read-application_type = '03' OR ls_case_read-application_type = '04'.

*            DATA lv_trad_lic_chk TYPE boolean.
*
*            IF iv_md_skip <> abap_true.
*
*              CLEAR lv_get_sel_node.
*              CLEAR lv_trad_lic_chk.
*
*              CALL METHOD zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases
*                EXPORTING
*                  iv_read_type    = 'C'
*                  ir_permit_id    = VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = ls_case_read-permit_id ) )
*                  iv_get_sel_node = lv_get_sel_node
*                RECEIVING
*                  et_data         = DATA(lt_permit_list).
*              IF lt_permit_list[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*
*                DELETE lt_permit_list WHERE ( application_type = '01' OR application_type = '05' ).
*                READ TABLE lt_permit_list TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY status = '75'.
*                IF sy-subrc = 0.
*                  lv_trad_lic_chk = abap_true.
*                ENDIF.
*
*              ENDIF.
*
*              IF lv_trad_lic_chk = abap_false.
*                lv_exerr = text-009.
*                ls_return-type = 'I'.
*                ls_return-message = lv_exerr.
*                ls_return-parameter = 'I009'.
*                APPEND ls_return TO et_return.
*                RETURN.
*              ENDIF.
*            ENDIF.

            CALL METHOD check_payment_for_permit
              EXPORTING
                iv_permit_id      = ls_case_read-permit_id
              IMPORTING
                ev_balance_to_pay = DATA(lv_flag).
            IF lv_flag = abap_true.
              lv_exerr = text-007.
              ls_return-type = 'E'.
              ls_return-message = lv_exerr.
              APPEND ls_return TO et_return.
              RETURN.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

        ENDIF. " End of Approve Button

      WHEN 'ZE02MOD'.

        IF iv_execfunc = 'FKT05'. " Approve
          SORT lt_srmwfpthps DESCENDING BY posid.
          READ TABLE lt_srmwfpthps INTO ls_srmwfpthps WITH KEY activity = 'ZE02GMA'.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            lv_gma_act = abap_true.
          ENDIF.

          IF ls_case_read-application_type = '05'.
            READ TABLE ls_case_read-fee_details ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_fee>) WITH KEY fee_type = '007'.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <fs_fee>-amount GT 0.
              IF lv_gma_act = abap_false.
                lv_exerr = text-004.
                ls_return-type = 'E'.
                ls_return-message = lv_exerr.
                APPEND ls_return TO et_return.
                RETURN.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

      WHEN 'ZE01INSP'. "Evaluation & Inspection  Review

        NEW zcl_ega_epda_inspection_api( )->get_inspection(
            EXPORTING
              ir_case_id      = VALUE #(  ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = ls_case_read-case_id ) )
              iv_last_visit   = abap_true
            IMPORTING
            et_inspection         = lt_inspection  ).
        IF lt_inspection[] IS NOT INITIAL AND iv_execfunc = 'FKT01'.
          lv_exerr = text-006.
          ls_return-type = 'E'.
          ls_return-message = lv_exerr.
          APPEND ls_return TO et_return.
          RETURN.
        ENDIF.

        IF iv_execfunc = 'FKT05' AND lt_inspection[] IS INITIAL.
          lv_exerr = text-008.
          ls_return-type = 'E'.
          ls_return-message = lv_exerr.
          APPEND ls_return TO et_return.
          RETURN.

        ENDIF.
*      WHEN 'ZE01LISC'. "License Section Head
*      WHEN 'ZE01INS'.
      WHEN OTHERS.
    ENDCASE.

  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->DECISIONVALIDATE_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_DECISIONVALIDATE
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD decisionvalidate_get_entityset.

    TYPES BEGIN OF ty_data.
    TYPES caseid      TYPE scmg_ext_key.
    TYPES positionid  TYPE zde_positionid.
    TYPES function    TYPE srmactfc.
    TYPES success     TYPE boolean.
    TYPES END OF ty_data.

    DATA lt_data TYPE TABLE OF ty_data.
    DATA ls_data TYPE ty_data.
    DATA ls_message TYPE bapireturn.
    TRY .
        ls_data-caseid = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CaseId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
    TRY .
        ls_data-positionid = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PositionId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
    TRY .
        ls_data-function = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'Function' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.
    ls_data-caseid = |{ ls_data-caseid ALPHA = IN }|.

    CLEAR ls_message.
    NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->fshry_fiori_valid(
      EXPORTING
        iv_case_id     = ls_data-caseid
        iv_position_id = ls_data-positionid
        iv_actfunc     = ls_data-function
      IMPORTING
        es_message     = ls_message
        ev_success     = ls_data-success
    ).

    APPEND ls_data TO lt_data[].
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING lt_data[] TO et_entityset[].

    IF ls_message IS NOT INITIAL.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
        EXPORTING
          textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
          message = ls_message-message.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->FISHING_BILLING
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_BILLINGDOCUMENT
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD FISHING_BILLING.
    DATA: lv_comp_en    TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
          lv_comp_ar    TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
          rt_case_id    TYPE RANGE OF scmg_ext_key,
          rt_partner_id TYPE RANGE OF bu_partner,
          lv_caseid     TYPE scmg_ext_key,
          lr_status     TYPE RANGE OF scmgstatusonr,
          ls_entityset  TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_billingdocument.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_freetext) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'FreeSearch' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_appl_no) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        lv_comp_en = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameEn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        lv_comp_ar = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameAr' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_permitno) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PermitNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        lv_caseid = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CaseId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.

    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_epda_cases(
      EXPORTING
        iv_read_type      = 'C'
        iv_comp_name_en   = lv_comp_en
        iv_comp_name_ar   = lv_comp_ar
        iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
        ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
        ir_permit_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
        ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
      RECEIVING
        et_data           = DATA(lt_data)
    ).
    LOOP AT lt_data INTO DATA(ls_data_n) WHERE case_type = 'ZE02'.
      ls_data_n-status = '56'.
      MODIFY lt_data FROM ls_data_n TRANSPORTING status.
    ENDLOOP.
    lr_status = VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 20  ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 21  ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 55  ) ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 56  ) ).

    DELETE lt_data WHERE status NOT IN lr_status.

    rt_case_id = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-case_id ) ) .

    rt_partner_id = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = wa-created_by_bp ) ).

    IF rt_case_id IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT DISTINCT bill_h~vbeln,
                  acct_h~opbel,
                  acct_h~budat,
                  bill_h~kunrg,
                  bill_h~xblnr,
                  bill_h~rfbsk,
                  bill_h~fkart,
                  bill_h~netwr,
                  bill_h~waerk,
                  sale_i~ext_key,
                  cs_attr~case_title,
                  cs_attr~case_type
                  FROM  vbrk              AS bill_h                                        INNER JOIN
                        vbap              AS sale_i   ON bill_h~xblnr = sale_i~vbeln       INNER JOIN
                        scmg_t_case_attr  AS cs_attr  ON sale_i~ext_key = cs_attr~ext_key  INNER JOIN
                        dfkkko            AS acct_h   ON bill_h~vbeln = acct_h~awkey
                        INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_billing_docs)
                        WHERE  sale_i~ext_key    IN @rt_case_id
                        AND ( cs_attr~case_type EQ 'ZE01' OR
                              cs_attr~case_type EQ 'ZE02' )
                        AND sale_i~ext_key IS NOT NULL
*                        AND bill_h~bukrs = 'REPA'
                        AND ( bill_h~fkart = 'ZBV' OR
                              bill_h~fkart = 'YBV' OR
                              bill_h~fkart = 'YQCR' )
                        AND (
                              bill_h~rfbsk = 'C' AND
                              NOT EXISTS ( SELECT *
                                           FROM   dfkkko AS acc_h            INNER JOIN
                                                  dfkkop AS acc_i            ON acc_h~opbel = acc_i~opbel
                                           WHERE  acc_h~awkey = bill_h~vbeln AND
                                                  acc_i~augst = '9' )        AND
                              EXISTS ( SELECT *
                                       FROM   dfkkko AS acc_h2               INNER JOIN
                                              dfkkop AS acc_i2               ON acc_h2~opbel = acc_i2~opbel
                                       WHERE  acc_h2~awkey = bill_h~vbeln    AND
                                              acc_i2~augst = '' )
                             ).
      IF lt_billing_docs IS NOT INITIAL.

        SELECT a~name_text , b~partner FROM adrp AS a LEFT OUTER JOIN but000 AS b ON a~persnumber = b~persnumber
                                                                       INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_adrp)
                                                                       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_billing_docs
                                                                       WHERE b~partner EQ @lt_billing_docs-kunrg.

        LOOP AT lt_billing_docs ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_bd>).
          READ TABLE lt_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_data>) WITH KEY case_id = <fs_bd>-ext_key.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            ls_entityset-applicationnumber = <fs_data>-application_number.
            ls_entityset-applicationtype = <fs_data>-application_type_tx.
            ls_entityset-billingdocid = <fs_bd>-opbel.
            ls_entityset-caseid = |{ <fs_data>-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|.
            ls_entityset-casetitle = <fs_data>-case_title.
            ls_entityset-casetype = <fs_data>-case_type.
            ls_entityset-compnamear = <fs_data>-comp_name_ar.
            ls_entityset-compnameen = <fs_data>-comp_name_en.
            ls_entityset-documentid = <fs_bd>-opbel.
            ls_entityset-payer = <fs_bd>-kunrg.
            ls_entityset-referencedocid = <fs_bd>-xblnr.
            ls_entityset-submissiondate = <fs_data>-created_on.
            ls_entityset-permitnumber = <fs_data>-permit_id.
            TRY .
                ls_entityset-payername = lt_adrp[ partner = <fs_bd>-kunrg ]-name_text.       "name_last.
              CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
            ENDTRY.
            ls_entityset-completeddate = <fs_bd>-budat.
            ls_entityset-netvalue = <fs_bd>-netwr.
          ENDIF.
          APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
        ENDLOOP.
        SORT et_entityset DESCENDING BY submissiondate.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->GET_DOMAIN
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] DOMAIN                         TYPE        STRING
* | [<-()] VALUES                         TYPE        TY_DD07V
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
METHOD get_domain.
  DATA: lv_domain  TYPE ddobjname.
  lv_domain = CONV #( domain ).
  CALL FUNCTION 'DDIF_DOMA_GET'
    EXPORTING
      name      = lv_domain
      langu     = sy-langu
    TABLES
      dd07v_tab = values.
ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->GET_EXPANDED_FISHING
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
