* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR(optional)
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IO_EXPAND                      TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ODATA_EXPAND(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITYSET                   TYPE REF TO DATA
* | [<---] ET_EXPANDED_CLAUSES            TYPE        STRING_TABLE
* | [<---] ET_EXPANDED_TECH_CLAUSES       TYPE        STRING_TABLE
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD get_expanded_fishing.
    IF iv_entity_name EQ zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>gc_application.
      TYPES:
        BEGIN OF ty_application,
          partnerset            TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_partner WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          boatset               TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_boat WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          additionalservicesset TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_additionalservices WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          fishingareaset        TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_fishingarea WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          attachmentset         TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_attachment WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          feesset               TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_fees WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          labourset             TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_labour WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          transferset           TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_transfer WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          treeslist             TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_noctreeslist WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          honeybee              TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_locatehoneybee WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          additionalserv        TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_nocadditionalserv WITH DEFAULT KEY.
          INCLUDE               TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_application.
    TYPES END OF ty_application.

      DATA: lv_comp_en         TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
            lv_comp_ar         TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
            lv_caseid          TYPE scmg_ext_key,
            lv_createdon       TYPE sy-datum,
            lv_createdon_h     TYPE sy-datum,
            lv_sign            TYPE char2,
            lv_from_date       TYPE datum,
            lv_to_date         TYPE datum,
            ls_data            TYPE zst_cs_ega_epda_fshry_bp,
            ls_application     TYPE ty_application,
            lt_application     TYPE TABLE OF ty_application,
            lo_nationality(15) TYPE c.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_freetext) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'FreeSearch' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_appl_no) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_comp_en = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameEn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_comp_ar = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameAr' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_permitno) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PermitNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_caseid = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CaseId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_createdon   = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CreatedOn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
          lv_sign        = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CreatedOn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-option.
          lv_createdon_h = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CreatedOn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-high.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.
**Getting one week dates
      CLEAR:lv_from_date,lv_to_date.
      IF lv_sign = 'EQ'.
        IF NOT lv_createdon IS INITIAL.
          CALL FUNCTION 'HR_GBSSP_GET_WEEK_DATES'
            EXPORTING
              p_pdate    = lv_createdon "sy-datum
            IMPORTING
              p_sunday   = lv_from_date
              p_saturday = lv_to_date.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        lv_from_date = lv_createdon.
        lv_to_date   = lv_createdon_h.
      ENDIF.
      IF NOT lv_caseid IS INITIAL.
        lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN } |.
      ENDIF.

**Getting one week dates
      DATA(lt_children) = io_expand->get_children( ).
**include fishery data

      DATA(lt_data) =    NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_fshry_cases(
              iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
              iv_from_odata     = abap_true
              iv_get_sel_node   = VALUE zst_fshry_bopf_node_read_flg(
                                 partner             = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'PARTNERSET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 additional_services = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'ADDITIONALSERVICESSET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 attachment          = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'ATTACHMENTSET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 boat                = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'BOATSET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 fee                 = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'FEESSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                                 workers             = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'LABOURSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                                 fishing_area        = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'FISHINGAREASET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 transfer            = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'TRANSFERSET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 trees_list          = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'TREESLIST' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 honey_bee           = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'HONEYBEE' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 )
              iv_from_date      = lv_from_date
              iv_to_date        = lv_to_date
              ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
              ir_lic_id         = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
              ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
          ).

      IF NOT lt_data IS INITIAL.
        DELETE lt_data WHERE case_id EQ ' '.
        DATA(apptypes) = get_domain( domain = 'ZDO_EGA_EPDA_APPLICATION_TYPE' ).
**Adding changes by Uday
        READ TABLE lt_data INTO DATA(ls_cases_fromboat) INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          IF NOT ls_cases_fromboat-case_id IS INITIAL.
            IF NOT ls_cases_fromboat-lic_no IS INITIAL.
              lv_permitno  = ls_cases_fromboat-lic_no.
              NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_fshry_cases_from_boat(
                EXPORTING
                  iv_get_all_node = abap_true
                  ir_lic_id       = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
                RECEIVING
                  et_data         = DATA(lt_data_boat) ).
              DELETE lt_data WHERE application_number EQ '  '.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        SELECT case_type, application_type, business_type, exclude, min_amount
          FROM zdt_epda_fees_ch
          INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_fees_check)
          ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
**Ending changes by Uday
        LOOP AT lt_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_data>) .
          CLEAR ls_application.
          MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data> TO ls_application.
          ls_application-companybpid001 = <fs_data>-owner_id.
          ls_application-companybpid    = <fs_data>-owner_id.
          ls_application-permit_id      = <fs_data>-lic_no.
          ls_application-portname       = <fs_data>-port_tx.
          ls_application-refer_case_id  = <fs_data>-ref_case_id.

**Added by Uday
*            IF NOT  <fs_data>-lic_no  IS INITIAL.
*              NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_lic_details(
*                EXPORTING
*                  iv_lic_no      = <fs_data>-lic_no    " Contract Number
*                IMPORTING
*                  es_lic_details = DATA(ls_lic_details)   " Real Estate Contract
*              ).
*              IF NOT ls_lic_details-contract_start_date IS INITIAL.
*                <fs_data>-issue_date = ls_lic_details-contract_start_date.
*              ENDIF.
*            ENDIF.

**Added by Uday
          ls_application-permit_issue_date = <fs_data>-issue_date.
          ls_application-permit_expiry_date = <fs_data>-expire_date.
          IF <fs_data>-case_type BETWEEN 'ZE14' AND 'ZE30'.
            IF <fs_data>-arch_contract IS NOT INITIAL.
              ls_application-permit_id          = <fs_data>-arch_contract.
              ls_application-permit_issue_date  = <fs_data>-arch_contract_st.
              ls_application-permit_expiry_date = <fs_data>-arch_contract_end.
            ENDIF.
          ELSE.
          ENDIF.
          ls_application-portname = <fs_data>-port_tx.
          ls_application-typeoffishing = <fs_data>-fishing_type_tx.
          ls_application-reefballs = COND #( WHEN <fs_data>-arti_reef_balls EQ 'X' THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' ).
          ls_application-case_id = me->remove( ls_application-case_id ).
          ls_application-application_number = <fs_data>-application_number.
          ls_application-application_type = <fs_data>-lic_type.
          ls_application-comp_name_en = <fs_data>-owner_name.
          IF <fs_data>-owner_name IS INITIAL.
            ls_application-comp_name_en = <fs_data>-owner_name_ar.
          ENDIF.
          ls_application-comp_name_ar = <fs_data>-owner_name_ar.
          ls_application-ministry_let_no  = <fs_data>-ministry_let_no.
*          ls_application-ministry_let_date = COND string( WHEN <fs_data>-ministry_let_date IS NOT INITIAL
*                                      THEN |{ <fs_data>-ministry_let_date+6(2) }| && '.' &&
*                                           |{ <fs_data>-ministry_let_date+4(2) }| && '.' &&
*                                           |{ <fs_data>-ministry_let_date+0(4) }| ELSE '' ) .
          ls_application-ministry_let_date = <fs_data>-ministry_let_date.
**Added by Uday
          READ TABLE lt_data_boat INTO DATA(ls_data_boat) WITH KEY lic_no = <fs_data>-lic_no.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            IF NOT ls_data_boat-workers IS INITIAL.
              DATA(lv_flag) = abap_true.
            ENDIF.
            DATA(lv_flag_attach) = abap_true.
            IF  ls_data_boat-attachment IS INITIAL.
              CLEAR lv_flag_attach.
              CLEAR lv_flag.
            ENDIF.
*            IF <fs_data>-lic_type = '02' AND <fs_data>-case_type = 'ZE07' .
*              DELETE <fs_data>-workers WHERE renew NE abap_true.
*            ENDIF.
*            IF lv_flag IS NOT INITIAL AND lv_flag_attach IS NOT INITIAL.
*              IF <fs_data>-lic_type = '02' AND <fs_data>-case_type = 'ZE07'.
*                DELETE ls_data_boat-workers WHERE renew NE abap_true.
*              ENDIF.
*            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
          IF <fs_data>-case_type = 'ZE08'.
            READ TABLE <fs_data>-transfer INTO DATA(lv_transfer_n) INDEX 1.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              IF NOT lv_transfer_n-duration IS INITIAL.
                IF sy-langu = 'E'.
                  CONCATENATE lv_transfer_n-duration 'Months' INTO DATA(duration_text) SEPARATED BY space.
                ELSEIF sy-langu = 'A'.
                  CONCATENATE lv_transfer_n-duration 'Months' INTO duration_text SEPARATED BY space.
                ENDIF.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.
**Added by Uday

          TRY.
              ls_application-appl_declar_name = COND #( WHEN sy-langu EQ 'E' THEN <fs_data>-partner[ fshry_bp_type = '1' ]-bp_name
                                                        ELSE <fs_data>-partner[ fshry_bp_type = '1'  ]-bp_name_ar ).
            CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
          ENDTRY.
          TRY.
              ls_application-application_type_tx = apptypes[ domvalue_l = <fs_data>-lic_type ]-ddtext.
            CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
          ENDTRY.
*        ls_application-companybpid001 = <fs_data>-partner[ ]-
          DATA(lv_range_text) = ls_application-main_activity_text.
*          ls_application-permit_issue_date = COND string( WHEN <fs_data>-issue_date IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                    THEN |{ <fs_data>-issue_date+6(2) }| &&
*                                                         |{ <fs_data>-issue_date+4(2) }| &&
*                                                         |{ <fs_data>-issue_date+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
*          ls_application-permit_expiry_date = COND string( WHEN <fs_data>-expire_date IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                    THEN |{ <fs_data>-expire_date+6(2) }| &&
*                                                         |{ <fs_data>-expire_date+4(2) }| &&
*                                                         |{ <fs_data>-expire_date+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
***Adding Changes by Uday
*          ls_application-permitrequestdate = COND string( WHEN <fs_data>-permitrequestdate IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                    THEN |{ <fs_data>-permitrequestdate+6(2) }| &&
*                                                         |{ <fs_data>-permitrequestdate+4(2) }| &&
*                                                         |{ <fs_data>-permitrequestdate+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
*          ls_application-permit_issue_date  = <fs_data>-issue_date.
*          ls_application-permit_expiry_date = <fs_data>-expire_date.
          ls_application-permitrequestdate  = <fs_data>-permitrequestdate.


          IF <fs_data>-case_type = 'ZE06'.
*            ls_application-permit_issue_date = COND string( WHEN <fs_data>-permitrequestdate IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                      THEN |{ <fs_data>-permitrequestdate+6(2) }| &&
*                                                           |{ <fs_data>-permitrequestdate+4(2) }| &&
*                                                           |{ <fs_data>-permitrequestdate+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
*            ls_application-permit_expiry_date = COND string( WHEN <fs_data>-permitrequestdate IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                      THEN |{ <fs_data>-permitrequestdate+6(2) }| &&
*                                                           |{ <fs_data>-permitrequestdate+4(2) }| &&
*                                                           |{ <fs_data>-permitrequestdate+0(4) }| ELSE '' ).
            ls_application-permit_issue_date  = <fs_data>-permitrequestdate.
            ls_application-permit_expiry_date = <fs_data>-permitrequestdate.

          ENDIF.
**Adding Changes by Uday
          ls_application-production_capacity = me->remove( ls_application-production_capacity ).
          SORT <fs_data>-partner BY owner_id fshry_bp_type app_flg DESCENDING.
          DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM <fs_data>-partner COMPARING owner_id fshry_bp_type.
          ls_application-partnerset               = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-partnerset FOR wa1 IN <fs_data>-partner
                                                            ( VALUE #(
                                                              ownernameen        = wa1-bp_name
                                                              bp_name            = wa1-bp_name_ar
                                                              bp_type            = wa1-fshry_bp_type
                                                              application_number = ls_application-application_number
                                                              applicanttype      = wa1-relkind
                                                              applicant          = wa1-app_flg
                                                              applicanttypetx    = wa1-fshry_bp_type_tx
                                                              emirates_id        = wa1-emirates_id
                                                              nationality        = wa1-nationality_tx
                                                              eidexpiry          = wa1-eid_expiry_on
                                                              email_address      = wa1-email_address
                                                              mobile_number      = wa1-mobile_number
                                                              birthdate          = wa1-birthdate        "Added By Uday
                                                              resident_type      = wa1-resident_type    "Added By Uday
                                                              owner_fee_exem     = <fs_data>-owner_fee_exem "Added By Uday
                                                              owner_fee_exem_tx  = <fs_data>-owner_fee_exem_tx "Added By Uday
                                                              own_type           = wa1-own_type
                                                              relationship       = wa1-relationship
                                                              relat_mobile       = wa1-relat_mobile
                                                              relat_email        = wa1-relat_email
                                                              ) ) ).
          IF <fs_data>-case_type = 'ZE10'.
**Getting the TRANSFER owner details.
            NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_bp_details(
              EXPORTING
                iv_bp_id      = <fs_data>-new_owner_id
              IMPORTING
                es_bp_details = ls_data ).
            DATA lt_partnerset            TYPE TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_partner.
            REFRESH lt_partnerset.
*            ls_application-partnerset     = VALUE ty_application-partnerset( ( ownernameen = ls_data-bp_name
            lt_partnerset     = VALUE ty_application-partnerset( ( ownernameen = ls_data-bp_name
                                                                              bp_name     = ls_data-bp_name_ar
                                                                              application_number = ls_application-application_number
                                                                              applicanttype = ls_data-relkind
                                                                              applicant = ls_data-app_flg
                                                                              applicanttypetx = 'New Owner' "ls_data-fshry_bp_type_tx
                                                                              emirates_id = ls_data-emirates_id
                                                                              nationality = ls_data-nationality_tx
                                                                              eidexpiry  = ls_data-eid_expiry_on
                                                                              email_address = ls_data-email_address
                                                                              mobile_number = ls_data-mobile_number
                                                                              birthdate     = ls_data-birthdate        "Added By Uday
                                                                              resident_type = ls_data-resident_type    "Added By Uday
                                                                              owner_fee_exem = <fs_data>-owner_fee_exem "Added By Uday
                                                                              owner_fee_exem_tx = <fs_data>-owner_fee_exem_tx
                                                                              own_type       = '4' ) ).
            APPEND LINES OF lt_partnerset TO ls_application-partnerset.
          ENDIF.
          LOOP AT ls_application-partnerset ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<partnerset>).
            IF <partnerset>-applicant IS NOT INITIAL.
              <partnerset>-applicanttypetx = 'Applicant'(010).
            ENDIF.
          ENDLOOP.
          SORT ls_application-partnerset.
          DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM ls_application-partnerset COMPARING ALL FIELDS.
          IF  ls_application-reefballs IS NOT INITIAL.
            ls_application-fishingareaset = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-fishingareaset FOR wa2 IN <fs_data>-fishing_area
                                                             ( CORRESPONDING #( BASE ( VALUE #(
                                                               gps = wa2-gps
                                                               fishingtype = wa2-fishing_type_tx
                                                               coordinatesnx = wa2-coordinates_n_x
                                                               coordinatesey = wa2-coordinates_e_y
                                                               ) ) wa2 ) ) ).
          ENDIF.
          ls_application-additionalservicesset = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-additionalservicesset FOR wa3 IN <fs_data>-additional_services
                                                           ( VALUE #(
                                                             labouraccomodation = wa3-labour_accom_tx
                                                             boatparking = wa3-boat_parking_tx
                                                             storage = wa3-storage_tx
                                                             applicationnumber = ls_application-application_number
                                                             caseid = ls_application-case_id
                                                             ) ) ).
          IF <fs_data>-case_type = 'ZE10'.
            DELETE <fs_data>-boat WHERE transfer NE abap_true.
          ENDIF.
          ls_application-boatset = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-boatset FOR wa4 IN <fs_data>-boat
                                                           ( CORRESPONDING #( BASE ( VALUE #(
                                                              licenseno = <fs_data>-lic_no
                                                              portname = <fs_data>-port_tx
                                                              number = wa4-boat_id
                                                              name = wa4-boat_name
                                                              type = wa4-boat_type_tx
*                                                              expirydate = COND string( WHEN wa4-boat_lic_exp_on IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                                           THEN |{ wa4-boat_lic_exp_on+6(2) }| && '.' &&
*                                                                          |{ wa4-boat_lic_exp_on+4(2) }| && '.' &&
*                                                                          |{ wa4-boat_lic_exp_on+0(4) }| ELSE '' )
                                                              expirydate = wa4-boat_lic_exp_on
                                                              length = wa4-length
                                                              enginenumber = wa4-engine_no
                                                              enginepower = wa4-engine_power
                                                              numberofcrew = wa4-max_crew_cnt
                                                              transfer     = COND #( WHEN wa4-transfer EQ abap_true THEN 'Transferred'  ELSE '' )
                                                              status = wa4-boat_status_tx
                                                              typeoffishing = wa4-fishing_type_tx
                                                              ) ) wa4 ) ) ).
          IF lv_flag_attach  = abap_true.
            ls_application-attachmentset = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-attachmentset FOR wa5_n IN ls_data_boat-attachment
                                                              ( CORRESPONDING #( BASE ( VALUE #(
                                                                     doc_type = wa5_n-doc_type
                                                                     file_descr = wa5_n-file_descr
                                                                     file_data = wa5_n-file_data
                                                                     file_mime_type = wa5_n-file_mime_type
                                                                     doc_type_desc = wa5_n-doc_type_desc
                                                                     file_name     = wa5_n-file_name
                                                                 ) ) wa5_n ) ) ).
          ELSE.
            ls_application-attachmentset = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-attachmentset FOR wa5 IN <fs_data>-attachment
                                                             ( CORRESPONDING #( BASE ( VALUE #(
                                                                    doc_type = wa5-doc_type
                                                                    file_descr = wa5-file_descr
                                                                    file_data = wa5-file_data
                                                                    file_mime_type = wa5-file_mime_type
                                                                    doc_type_desc = wa5-doc_type_desc
                                                                    file_name     = wa5-file_name
                                                                    attachment_key = wa5-attach_key
                                                                ) ) wa5 ) ) ).
          ENDIF.

          ls_application-feesset = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-feesset FOR wa6 IN <fs_data>-fees
                                                           ( CORRESPONDING #( BASE ( VALUE #(
                                                                  fee_type         = wa6-fee_type
                                                                  fee_type_tx      = wa6-fee_type_t
                                                                  amount           = wa6-amount
                                                                  years_duration   = wa6-nyears
                                                                  months_duration  = wa6-nmonths
                                                                  fee_exemption    = wa6-fee_rules_t
                                                                  currency         = 'AED'
                                                                  application_type =  ls_application-application_type
                                                              ) ) wa6 ) ) ).

          READ TABLE ls_application-feesset INTO DATA(ls_fee_total) WITH KEY fee_type = '000'.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            DELETE ls_application-feesset WHERE fee_type EQ '000'.
            IF <fs_data>-case_type BETWEEN 'ZE14' AND 'ZE30'.
              TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_fees_sort,
                       fee_type TYPE char3,
                       order    TYPE numc3,
                     END OF ty_fees_sort,
                     tt_fees_sort TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_fees_sort WITH DEFAULT KEY.
              DATA: lt_fees_sort TYPE tt_fees_sort.
              APPEND '000999' TO lt_fees_sort.
              APPEND '009100' TO lt_fees_sort.
              APPEND '010001' TO lt_fees_sort.
              APPEND '090900' TO lt_fees_sort.
              LOOP AT ls_application-feesset ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fee>).
                READ TABLE lt_fees_sort INTO DATA(ls_fees_sort) WITH KEY fee_type = <fee>-fee_type.
                IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
                  <fee>-application_number = ls_fees_sort-order.
                ELSE.
                  <fee>-application_number = '999'.
                ENDIF.
              ENDLOOP.
              SORT ls_application-feesset BY application_number.
              LOOP AT ls_application-feesset ASSIGNING <fee>.
                CLEAR: <fee>-application_number.
              ENDLOOP.
            ELSE.
              SORT ls_application-feesset BY fee_type.
            ENDIF.
            APPEND ls_fee_total TO ls_application-feesset.
          ENDIF.
          LOOP AT ls_application-feesset ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fees_set>) WHERE fee_type EQ '010'.
            <fees_set>-license_relevant = abap_true.
            READ TABLE lt_fees_check WITH KEY
            case_type        = <fs_data>-case_type
            application_type = '01'
            exclude          = abap_true TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              <fees_set>-license_relevant = abap_false.
            ENDIF.
          ENDLOOP.
          DATA(ls_partner_nation) = VALUE #( <fs_data>-partner[ own_type = |2| ] OPTIONAL ).
          IF ls_partner_nation-nationality IS NOT INITIAL.
            IF ls_partner_nation-nationality = 'AE'.
              lo_nationality = 'UAE Local'.
            ELSE.
              lo_nationality = 'UAE Non Local'.
            ENDIF.
            LOOP AT ls_application-feesset ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_feeset>) WHERE fee_type = '010'.
              <fs_feeset>-nationality = lo_nationality.
            ENDLOOP.
          ENDIF.
          IF <fs_data>-case_type BETWEEN 'ZE14' AND 'ZE30'.
          ELSE.
            DATA(ls_fee) = VALUE #( ls_application-feesset[ fee_type = |009| ] OPTIONAL ).
            IF ls_fee-fee_type IS INITIAL.
              ls_fee-fee_type     = '009'.
              ls_fee-fee_type_tx  = 'Exemption'.
              ls_fee-amount       = '0.00'.
              ls_fee-currency     = 'AED'.
              ls_fee-application_type = ls_application-application_type.
              IF NOT ls_application-feesset[] IS INITIAL.
                INSERT ls_fee INTO ls_application-feesset INDEX 2 .
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
**Labour set
          IF lv_flag  = abap_true.
            ls_application-labourset = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-labourset FOR wa7_n IN ls_data_boat-workers
                                                      ( CORRESPONDING #( BASE ( VALUE #(
                                                           licenseno        = <fs_data>-lic_no
                                                           portname         = <fs_data>-port_tx
                                                           number           = wa7_n-worker_id
                                                           name             = wa7_n-bp_name
                                                           nationality      = wa7_n-nationality
                                                           visastatus       = wa7_n-visa_status_tx
*                                                           visaexpirydate   = COND string( WHEN wa7_n-card_exp_on IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                                               THEN |{ wa7_n-card_exp_on+6(2) }| && '.' &&
*                                                                                    |{ wa7_n-card_exp_on+4(2) }| && '.' &&
*                                                                                    |{ wa7_n-card_exp_on+0(4) }| ELSE '' )
                                                           visaexpirydate   = wa7_n-card_exp_on
                                                           roomno           = wa7_n-room_no
                                                           eid              = wa7_n-emirates_id
                                                           status           = wa7_n-worker_status_tx
                                                           uid              = wa7_n-visa_id
                                                           phoneno          = wa7_n-mobile_number
                                                           boatnumber       = wa7_n-boat_id
                                                           worker_bp        = wa7_n-worker_bp
                                                           renew            = wa7_n-renew
                                                           card_print       = wa7_n-card_print ) ) wa7_n ) ) ).

          ELSE.
            ls_application-labourset = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-labourset FOR wa7 IN <fs_data>-workers
                                                     ( CORRESPONDING #( BASE ( VALUE #(
                                                              licenseno = <fs_data>-lic_no
                                                          portname = <fs_data>-port_tx
                                                          name             = wa7-bp_name
                                                          number           = wa7-worker_id
                                                          nationality      = wa7-nationality
                                                          visastatus       = wa7-visa_status_tx
*                                                          visaexpirydate   = COND string( WHEN wa7-card_exp_on IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                                              THEN |{ wa7-card_exp_on+6(2) }| && '.' &&
*                                                                             |{ wa7-card_exp_on+4(2) }| && '.' &&
*                                                                             |{ wa7-card_exp_on+0(4) }| ELSE '' )
                                                          visaexpirydate   = wa7-card_exp_on
                                                          roomno           = wa7-room_no
                                                          eid              = wa7-emirates_id
                                                          status           = wa7-worker_status_tx
                                                          uid              = wa7-visa_id
                                                          phoneno          = wa7-mobile_number
                                                          boatnumber       = wa7-boat_id
                                                          renew            = wa7-renew
                                                           card_print      = wa7-card_print ) ) wa7 ) ) ).
          ENDIF.
          IF <fs_data>-case_type = 'ZE09'.
            REFRESH :ls_application-fishingareaset.
            ls_application-fishingareaset = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-fishingareaset FOR wa2_n IN <fs_data>-fishing_area
                                                             ( CORRESPONDING #( BASE ( VALUE #(
                                                               gps = wa2_n-gps
                                                               coordinatesnx = wa2_n-coordinates_n_x
                                                               coordinatesey = wa2_n-coordinates_e_y
                                                               depth         = wa2_n-depth
                                                               materialdetails = wa2_n-material
                                                               ) ) wa2_n ) ) ).

          ENDIF.
***
          IF <fs_data>-case_type = 'ZE08'.
            ls_application-transferset = VALUE #( BASE ls_application-transferset FOR wa8 IN <fs_data>-transfer
                                             ( CORRESPONDING #( BASE ( VALUE #(
                                                   duration         = wa8-duration
                                                   licno            = wa8-lic_no
                                                   boatnumber       = wa8-boat_id
                                                   bpno             = wa8-owner_id
                                                   ownernamear      = wa8-bp_name_ar
                                                   ownernameen      = wa8-bp_name
                                                   nationality      = wa8-nationality_tx
                                                   eid              = wa8-emirates_id
                                                   durationtext     = duration_text
*                                                   expirydate       =  COND string( WHEN wa8-eid_expiry_on IS NOT INITIAL
*                                                                               THEN |{ wa8-eid_expiry_on+6(2) }| && '.' &&
*                                                                                    |{ wa8-eid_expiry_on+4(2) }| && '.' &&
*                                                                                    |{ wa8-eid_expiry_on+0(4) }| ELSE '' )
                                                   expirydate       = wa8-eid_expiry_on ) ) wa8 ) ) ).
          ENDIF.

          MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data>-tree_list        TO ls_application-treeslist.
          MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data>-locate_honey_bee TO ls_application-honeybee.
          MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data>-additional_serv  TO ls_application-additionalserv.
          APPEND ls_application TO lt_application.
        ENDLOOP.

        copy_data_to_ref(
          EXPORTING
            is_data = lt_application
          CHANGING
            cr_data = er_entityset
        ).

        APPEND 'PARTNERSET' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'ADDITIONALSERVICESSET' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'ADDITIONALSERVICESSET' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'BOATSET' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'FISHINGAREASET' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'ATTACHMENTSET' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'FEESSET' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'LABOURSET' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'TREESLIST' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'HONEYBEE' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'ADDITIONALSERV' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->GET_EXPANDED_NOC_CONSULTANT
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION(optional)
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER(optional)
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH(optional)
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR(optional)
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IO_EXPAND                      TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ODATA_EXPAND(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITYSET                   TYPE REF TO DATA
* | [<---] ET_EXPANDED_CLAUSES            TYPE        STRING_TABLE
* | [<---] ET_EXPANDED_TECH_CLAUSES       TYPE        STRING_TABLE
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD get_expanded_noc_consultant.
    IF iv_entity_name EQ zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>gc_application.

      TYPES:
        BEGIN OF ty_application,
          partnerset        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_partner              WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          attachmentset     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_attachment           WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          feesset           TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_fees                 WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          feesconditionsset TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_feesconditions       WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          company           TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_noccompany           WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          consultant        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_consultant           WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          chemicalsdetails  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_nocchemicals         WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          material          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_nocmaterials         WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          supplier          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_nocsuppliers         WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          location          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_noclocation          WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          datadictionaryset TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_datadictionary       WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          envstudy          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_nocenviromentalstudy WITH DEFAULT KEY,
          vehicles          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_nocvehicles          WITH DEFAULT KEY.
          INCLUDE       TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_application.
    TYPES END OF ty_application.

      DATA: lv_comp_en         TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
            lv_comp_ar         TYPE zde_epda_company_name,
            lv_caseid          TYPE scmg_ext_key,
            lv_createdon       TYPE sy-datum,
            lv_createdon_h     TYPE sy-datum,
            lv_sign            TYPE char2,
            lv_from_date       TYPE datum,
            lv_to_date         TYPE datum,
            ls_data            TYPE zst_cs_ega_epda_fshry_bp,
            ls_application     TYPE ty_application,
            lt_application     TYPE TABLE OF ty_application,
            lo_nationality(15) TYPE c.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_freetext) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'FreeSearch' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_appl_no) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'ApplicationNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_comp_en = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameEn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_comp_ar = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CompNameAr' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          DATA(lv_permitno) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PermitNumber' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_caseid = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CaseId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY .
          lv_createdon   = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CreatedOn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
          lv_sign        = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CreatedOn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-option.
          lv_createdon_h = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'CreatedOn' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-high.
        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      ENDTRY.
**Getting one week dates
      CLEAR:lv_from_date,lv_to_date.
      IF lv_sign = 'EQ'.
        IF NOT lv_createdon IS INITIAL.
          CALL FUNCTION 'HR_GBSSP_GET_WEEK_DATES'
            EXPORTING
              p_pdate    = lv_createdon "sy-datum
            IMPORTING
              p_sunday   = lv_from_date
              p_saturday = lv_to_date.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        lv_from_date = lv_createdon.
        lv_to_date   = lv_createdon_h.
      ENDIF.
      IF NOT lv_caseid IS INITIAL.
        lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN } |.
      ENDIF.

**Getting one week dates
      DATA(lt_children) = io_expand->get_children( ).
**include fishery data

      DATA(lt_data) =    NEW zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api( )->get_noc_cases(
              iv_freetext       = lv_freetext
              iv_from_odata     = abap_true
              iv_get_sel_node   = VALUE zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api=>ty_noc_bopf_node_read_flg(
                                 partner     = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'PARTNERSET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 attachment  = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'ATTACHMENTSET' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 fees        = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'FEESSET' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                                 company     = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'COMPANY' ] ) THEN abap_true )
                                 consultant  = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'CONSULTANT' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 chemical    = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'CHEMICALSDETAILS' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 material    = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'MATERIAL' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 supplier    = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'SUPPLIER' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 location    = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'LOCATION' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 envstudy    = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'ENVSTUDY' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 vehicles    = COND #( WHEN line_exists( lt_children[ tech_nav_prop_name = 'VEHICLES' ] ) THEN abap_true  )
                                 )
              iv_from_date      = lv_from_date
              iv_to_date        = lv_to_date
              ir_case_id        = COND #( WHEN lv_caseid IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_caseid ) ) )
              ir_permit_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_permitno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_permitno ) ) )
              ir_applic_id      = COND #( WHEN lv_appl_no IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_appl_no ) ) )
          ).

      IF NOT lt_data IS INITIAL.
        DELETE lt_data WHERE case_id EQ ' '.

        DATA: lt_data_dictionary TYPE sral_ad_search_param_values,
              ls_consultant      TYPE zst_ega_epda_consultant.
        CALL FUNCTION 'ZFE_CJ_DATA_DICTIONARY_STRUCT'
          EXPORTING
            iv_typename   = 'ZST_CS_EGA_EPDA_CONSULTANT'
          IMPORTING
            et_dictionary = lt_data_dictionary.

        SELECT case_type, application_type, business_type, exclude, min_amount
          FROM zdt_epda_fees_ch
          INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_fees_check)
          ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.


        LOOP AT lt_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_data>) .
          CLEAR ls_application.
          LOOP AT <fs_data>-consultant ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<consultant>).
            me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <consultant> CHANGING c_target = ls_consultant ).
*            MOVE-CORRESPONDING <consultant> TO ls_consultant.
            IF <consultant>-category IS INITIAL.
              CALL FUNCTION 'ZFE_CJ_CONSULTANT_GRADE_CALC'
                EXPORTING
                  im_consultant = ls_consultant
                IMPORTING
                  ev_total      = <consultant>-total
                  ev_grade      = <consultant>-category.
            ENDIF.
          ENDLOOP.
          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data> CHANGING c_target = ls_application ).
          ls_application-companybpid  = <fs_data>-comp_bp_id.
          ls_application-searchkey = '3'.
*          MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data> TO ls_application.
          ls_application-permit_id = <fs_data>-permit.
          IF ls_application-permit_id IS INITIAL.
            ls_application-permit_id     = <fs_data>-application_number.
          ENDIF.

          ls_application-dedapplnno = <fs_data>-ded_appln_no.
          ls_application-dedstatus = <fs_data>-ded_status_tx.
          ls_application-dednote = <fs_data>-ded_note.
          ls_application-dedblocktyp = <fs_data>-block_type_tx.

          ls_application-comp_street     = <fs_data>-moi_refer.
          SHIFT ls_application-refer_case_id LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.
          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data>-company[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-company[] ).
*          MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data>-company[]    TO ls_application-company[].
          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data>-consultant[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-consultant[] ).
*          MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data>-consultant[] TO ls_application-consultant[].
          CLEAR: ls_application-partnerset[].
          LOOP AT <fs_data>-partner INTO DATA(ls_partner).
            APPEND INITIAL LINE TO ls_application-partnerset ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<partner_set>).
            IF ls_partner-app_flg IS NOT INITIAL.
              ls_partner-bp_type_tx = 'Applicant'(010).
            ENDIF.
            me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = ls_partner CHANGING c_target = <partner_set> ).
*            MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_partner TO <partner_set>.
            <partner_set>-ownernameen = ls_partner-bp_name_en.
            <partner_set>-applicanttypetx = ls_partner-rltxt.
            IF <partner_set>-applicanttypetx IS INITIAL.
              <partner_set>-applicanttypetx = ls_partner-bp_type_tx.
            ENDIF.
            IF <partner_set>-applicanttypetx IS INITIAL.
              <partner_set>-applicanttypetx = ls_partner-own_type_tx.
            ENDIF.
          ENDLOOP.
          SORT ls_application-partnerset.
          DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM ls_application-partnerset COMPARING ALL FIELDS.
          CLEAR: ls_application-attachmentset[].
          LOOP AT <fs_data>-attachment INTO DATA(ls_attach).
            APPEND INITIAL LINE TO ls_application-attachmentset ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<attach_set>).
            me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = ls_attach CHANGING c_target = <attach_set> ).
*            MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_attach TO <attach_set>.
            <attach_set>-attachment_key = ls_attach-attach_key.
          ENDLOOP.
          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data>-fees[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-feesset[] ).
          LOOP AT ls_application-feesset ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fees_set>) WHERE fee_type EQ '010'.
            <fees_set>-license_relevant = abap_true.
            READ TABLE lt_fees_check WITH KEY
            case_type        = <fs_data>-case_type
            application_type = <fs_data>-application_type
            exclude          = abap_true TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              <fees_set>-license_relevant = abap_false.
            ENDIF.
          ENDLOOP.
*          MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data>-fees[]       TO ls_application-feesset[].
          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data>-fees_cond[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-feesconditionsset[] ).
*          MOVE-CORRESPONDING <fs_data>-fees_cond[]  TO ls_application-feesconditionsset[].
          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = lt_data_dictionary[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-datadictionaryset[] ).
*          MOVE-CORRESPONDING lt_data_dictionary[]   TO ls_application-datadictionaryset[].
          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data>-chemical[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-chemicalsdetails[] ).
          IF ls_application-chemicalsdetails[] IS INITIAL. APPEND INITIAL LINE TO ls_application-chemicalsdetails[]. ENDIF.

          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data>-material[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-material[] ).

          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data>-supplier[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-supplier[] ).

          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data>-location[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-location[] ).

          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data>-envstudy[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-envstudy[] ).

          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <fs_data>-vehicles[] CHANGING c_target = ls_application-vehicles[] ).

          TRY.
              ls_application-appl_declar_name = COND #( WHEN sy-langu EQ 'E' THEN <fs_data>-partner[ 1 ]-bp_name_en
                                                        ELSE <fs_data>-partner[ 1 ]-bp_name ).
            CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
          ENDTRY.


          APPEND ls_application TO lt_application.
        ENDLOOP.

        copy_data_to_ref(
          EXPORTING
            is_data = lt_application
          CHANGING
            cr_data = er_entityset
        ).

        APPEND 'PARTNERSET'        TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'FISHINGAREASET'    TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'FEESSET'           TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'FEESCONDITIONSSET' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'COMPANY'           TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'CONSULTANT'        TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'DATADICTIONARYSET' TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'CHEMICALSDETAILS'  TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'MATERIAL'          TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'SUPPLIER'          TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'LOCATION'          TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
        APPEND 'ENVSTUDY'          TO et_expanded_tech_clauses.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->OVERVIEWSET_FISH_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_OVERVIEW
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD overviewset_fish_get_entityset.
    DATA: lv_wiid   TYPE sww_wiid,
          lv_caseid TYPE scmg_ext_key,
          lv_applno TYPE zde_ega_epda_application_no.

    IF it_key_tab[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      READ TABLE it_key_tab INTO DATA(ls_key) WITH KEY name = 'WiId'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        lv_wiid   = ls_key-value.
      ENDIF.
      READ TABLE it_key_tab INTO ls_key WITH KEY name = 'CaseId'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        lv_caseid   = ls_key-value.
        lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
      ENDIF.
      READ TABLE it_key_tab INTO ls_key WITH KEY name = 'ApplicationNumber'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        lv_applno   = ls_key-value.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF it_filter_select_options[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      READ TABLE it_filter_select_options INTO DATA(ls_filter) WITH KEY property = 'WiId'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        READ TABLE ls_filter-select_options INTO DATA(ls_select) INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          lv_wiid   = ls_select-low.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      READ TABLE it_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter WITH KEY property = 'CaseId'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        READ TABLE ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_select INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          lv_caseid   = ls_select-low.
          lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      READ TABLE it_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter WITH KEY property = 'ApplicationNumber'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        READ TABLE ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_select INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          lv_applno   = ls_select-low.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF NOT lv_applno IS INITIAL.

      NEW zcl_ega_epda_fshry_handler_api( )->get_fshry_cases(
        EXPORTING
          ir_applic_id      =  COND #( WHEN lv_applno IS NOT INITIAL THEN VALUE #( ( sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = lv_applno ) ) )
        RECEIVING
          et_data           = DATA(lt_appl_data)
      ).
      READ TABLE lt_appl_data INTO DATA(ls_appl_data) INDEX 1.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lv_caseid = ls_appl_data-case_id.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    lv_caseid = |{ lv_caseid ALPHA = IN }|.
*    DATA(lt_data) = zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_wi_overview( im_wi_id = lv_wiid ).
    zcl_ega_epda_appln_handler_api=>get_wi_overview(
      EXPORTING
        im_wi_id     = lv_wiid    " Work item ID
        im_case_id   = lv_caseid   " Case ID
      RECEIVING
        rt_data      = DATA(lt_data)    " EPDA - My Case Workitem Overview Tabletype
    ).
****
    SELECT SINGLE *  FROM zdt_epda_fisry_r INTO @DATA(ls_epda_appln_r) WHERE case_id = @lv_caseid .
******
    SORT lt_data DESCENDING BY assign_date assign_time.
    et_entityset = VALUE #( FOR wa IN lt_data
                  (   wi_id = wa-wi_id
                      case_id = |{ wa-case_id ALPHA = OUT }|
                      assign_date = |{ wa-assign_date+6(2) }| && |{ wa-assign_date+4(2) }| &&
                                     |{ wa-assign_date+0(4) }| && |{ wa-assign_time }|
                      completion_date = COND string( WHEN wa-completion_date IS NOT INITIAL
                                         THEN |{ wa-completion_date+6(2) }| && |{ wa-completion_date+4(2) }| &&
                                         |{ wa-completion_date+0(4) }| && |{ wa-completion_time }| ELSE '' )
                      application_number = wa-application_number
                      wi_rh_task = wa-wi_rh_task
                      task = wa-task
                      wi_aagent = wa-wi_aagent
                      assign_user_name = wa-assign_user_name
                      wi_stat = wa-wi_stat
                      comments = wa-comments
                      permit_id =  ls_epda_appln_r-lic_no
                      comp_name_en = ls_appl_data-owner_name
                      comp_name_ar = ls_appl_data-owner_name_ar
                      locationid = ls_appl_data-port
                      locationdesc = ls_appl_data-port_tx
                       ) ).

  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Private Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->MOVE_CORRESPOND
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] I_SOURCE                       TYPE        ANY
* | [<-->] C_TARGET                       TYPE        ANY
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD move_correspond.

*    me->move_corresponding( ).

    DATA: lo_source_struct TYPE REF TO cl_abap_structdescr.


    FIELD-SYMBOLS: <source_tab> TYPE STANDARD TABLE,
                   <target_tab> TYPE STANDARD TABLE.

    DATA(lo_source) = cl_abap_typedescr=>describe_by_data( i_source ).
    ASSERT lo_source->kind NE cl_abap_typedescr=>kind_ref.

    DATA(lo_target) = cl_abap_typedescr=>describe_by_data( c_target ).

    CHECK lo_source->kind EQ lo_target->kind.

    CASE lo_source->kind.
      WHEN cl_abap_typedescr=>kind_elem.
        DESCRIBE FIELD i_source TYPE DATA(lv_type).
        CASE lv_type.
          WHEN 'D'.
            DESCRIBE FIELD c_target TYPE DATA(lv_target_type).
            IF lv_target_type EQ 'D'.
              MOVE i_source TO c_target.
            ELSE.
              IF i_source IS INITIAL.
                i_source = '18000101'.
              ENDIF.
              c_target = i_source.
*              c_target = i_source+6(2) && i_source+4(2) && i_source(4).
            ENDIF.
          WHEN OTHERS.
            MOVE i_source TO c_target.
        ENDCASE.
      WHEN cl_abap_typedescr=>kind_struct.
        lo_source_struct ?= cl_abap_structdescr=>describe_by_data( i_source ).
        DATA(lt_comp) = lo_source_struct->get_components( ).
        LOOP AT lt_comp INTO DATA(ls_comp).
          IF ls_comp-as_include IS NOT INITIAL.
            lo_source_struct ?= ls_comp-type.
            DATA(lt_incl) = lo_source_struct->get_components( ).
            APPEND LINES OF lt_incl TO lt_comp.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
        LOOP AT lt_comp INTO ls_comp.

          ASSIGN COMPONENT ls_comp-name OF STRUCTURE i_source TO FIELD-SYMBOL(<source_field>).
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            ASSIGN COMPONENT ls_comp-name OF STRUCTURE c_target TO FIELD-SYMBOL(<target_field>).
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <source_field> CHANGING c_target = <target_field> ).
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
      WHEN cl_abap_typedescr=>kind_table.
        ASSIGN i_source TO <source_tab>.
        ASSIGN c_target TO <target_tab>.
        CLEAR: <target_tab>[].
        LOOP AT <source_tab> ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<source_line>).
          APPEND INITIAL LINE TO <target_tab> ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<target_line>).
          me->move_correspond( EXPORTING i_source = <source_line> CHANGING c_target = <target_line> ).
        ENDLOOP.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDMETHOD.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_DPC_EXT->PERMITSEARCHSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_TD_MPC=>TT_PERMITSEARCH
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  METHOD permitsearchset_get_entityset.
*SUPER->PERMITSEARCHSET_GET_ENTITYSET(
*  EXPORTING
*    IV_ENTITY_NAME           = IV_ENTITY_NAME
*    IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME       = IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME
*    IV_SOURCE_NAME           = IV_SOURCE_NAME
*    IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS = IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS
*    IS_PAGING                = IS_PAGING
*    IT_KEY_TAB               = IT_KEY_TAB
*    IT_NAVIGATION_PATH       = IT_NAVIGATION_PATH
*    IT_ORDER                 = IT_ORDER
*    IV_FILTER_STRING         = IV_FILTER_STRING
*    IV_SEARCH_STRING         = IV_SEARCH_STRING
**    io_tech_request_context  = io_tech_request_context
**  IMPORTING
**    et_entityset             = et_entityset
**    es_response_context      = es_response_context
*       ).

    DATA lr_permit_no       TYPE RANGE OF zde_ega_permit_ref_number.
    DATA ls_permit_no       LIKE LINE OF lr_permit_no.
    DATA lr_comp_en         TYPE RANGE OF zde_epda_company_name.
    DATA ls_comp_en         LIKE LINE OF lr_comp_en.
    DATA lr_comp_ar         TYPE RANGE OF zde_epda_company_name_arabic.
    DATA ls_comp_ar         LIKE LINE OF lr_comp_ar.

    DATA ls_entityset     TYPE zcl_zega_epda_td_mpc=>ts_permitsearch.
    DATA lv_query TYPE string.
    REFRESH et_entityset[].
    TRY .
        DATA(lv_permitid) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PermitId' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY .
        DATA(lv_search) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'FreeSearch' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    ENDTRY.

    CLEAR lv_query.
    IF lv_search IS NOT INITIAL.
      NEW zcl_epda_consult_noc_handl_api( )->permit_srch_filter_param(
        EXPORTING
          iv_search     = lv_search
        IMPORTING
          et_sel_params = DATA(lt_sel_params) ).

      LOOP AT lt_sel_params INTO DATA(ls_sel_params) WHERE attribute_name = 'PERMITID'.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_sel_params TO ls_permit_no.
        APPEND ls_permit_no TO lr_permit_no.
      ENDLOOP.

      LOOP AT lt_sel_params INTO ls_sel_params WHERE attribute_name = 'COMP_NAME_EN'.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_sel_params TO ls_comp_en.
        APPEND ls_comp_en TO lr_comp_en.
      ENDLOOP.

      LOOP AT lt_sel_params INTO ls_sel_params WHERE attribute_name = 'COMP_NAME_AR'.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_sel_params TO ls_comp_ar.
        APPEND ls_comp_ar TO lr_comp_ar.
      ENDLOOP.

      lv_query = 'permitid IN @lr_permit_no OR comp_name_en IN @lr_comp_en OR comp_name_ar IN @lr_comp_ar'.
    ELSEIF lv_permitid IS NOT INITIAL.

      ls_permit_no-sign = 'I'.
      ls_permit_no-option = 'EQ'.
      ls_permit_no-low = |{ lv_permitid ALPHA = IN }| .
      APPEND ls_permit_no TO lr_permit_no.
      lv_query = 'permitid IN @lr_permit_no'.
    ELSE.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.


    SELECT permitid,
    contracttype,
    startdate,
    enddate,
    contractname,
    oldpermit,
    licensefromtx,
    trade_license,
    applicationtypetx,
    mainact,
    mainacttx,
    business_type,
    businees_type_description,
    licensetype,
    licensetypetext,
    comp_name_en,
    comp_name_ar,
    permitstatustx,
    renewed
    FROM zi_epda_pm_mast_lst INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_permit_md)
      WHERE (lv_query).
*              permitid IN @lr_permit_no.
*          OR comp_name_en IN @lr_comp_en
*          OR comp_name_ar IN @lr_comp_ar.
    DATA lv_time TYPE srmwfppcrt.
    CLEAR lv_time.
    LOOP AT lt_permit_md INTO DATA(ls_permit_md).

      ls_entityset-permitid = ls_permit_md-permitid.
      ls_entityset-contracttype = ls_permit_md-contracttype.
      ls_entityset-startdate = ls_permit_md-startdate && lv_time.
      ls_entityset-enddate = ls_permit_md-enddate && lv_time.
      ls_entityset-contractname = ls_permit_md-contractname.
      ls_entityset-oldpermit = ls_permit_md-oldpermit.
      ls_entityset-licensefromtx = ls_permit_md-licensefromtx.
      ls_entityset-tradelicense = ls_permit_md-trade_license.
      ls_entityset-applicationtypetx = ls_permit_md-applicationtypetx.
      ls_entityset-mainact = ls_permit_md-mainact.
      ls_entityset-mainacttx = ls_permit_md-mainacttx.
      ls_entityset-businesstype = ls_permit_md-business_type.
      ls_entityset-busineestypedescription = ls_permit_md-businees_type_description.
      ls_entityset-licensetype = ls_permit_md-licensetype.
      ls_entityset-licensetypetext = ls_permit_md-licensetypetext.
      ls_entityset-compnameen = ls_permit_md-comp_name_en.
      ls_entityset-compnamear = ls_permit_md-comp_name_ar.
      ls_entityset-permitstatustx = ls_permit_md-permitstatustx.
      ls_entityset-renewed  = ls_permit_md-renewed.
      APPEND ls_entityset TO et_entityset.
    ENDLOOP.




  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.
